7/-!
8# Collision-free strong well-posedness of the periodic particle system (Proposition B.1)
9
10Package PART-01.  Source: HRS periodic main paper, Proposition B.1 `B:thm:particle-wp`
11(M L11645-11656) and its proof in §B "Direct collision-free regimes" (M L11724-12000):
12
13* Lemma `B:lem:particle-maximal` (L11731-11737): maximal strong solution up to `τ_⋆` with the
14  collision alternative `B:eq:particle-collision-alternative` (L11734).
15* Cluster dispersion `Q_I` (L11759-11779): `Q_I = ∑_{i∈I}|x_i - x̄_I|² = k⁻¹ ∑_{i<j∈I}|x_i - x_j|²`
16  on `ℝ^d`, the enlargement identity `B:eq:cluster-enlargement` (L11770), and the smooth
17  function on `(𝕋^d)^N` with `Q_I⁻¹(0) = Δ_I` equal to the Euclidean expression near `Δ_I`.
18* Lemma `B:lem:cluster-ito` (L11781-11802): Itô equation `B:eq:cluster-ito` (L11784-11788),
19  `𝒜_I ≥ 0` (`B:eq:cluster-internal-positive`, L11791), remainder bound
20  `B:eq:cluster-remainder` (L11795) under the exterior separation
21  `B:eq:larger-cluster-separated` (L11799).
22* Lemma `B:lem:cluster-barrier` (L11849-11856): logarithmic barrier, descending induction.
23* Proposition `B:prop:particle-no-collision-dge2` (L11912-11918).
24* Lemma `B:lem:d1-pair-barrier` (L11938-11948) and Proposition
25  `B:prop:particle-no-collision-d1` (L11969-11971) for `d = 1`.
26* Conclusion (L11989-12000), including the random initial configuration.
27
28## Representation decisions (see `docs/ISSUES.md` §D1 and `HRS/Stochastic/SPEC.md`)
29
30* Everything is formulated on the covering space `E = Fin N → Euc d` through the lifted SDE of
31  `HRS/Stochastic/Bridge.lean`: drift `liftedDrift D`, additive noise `liftedNoise D`, state
32  space `liftedFree d N` (open).  The source's maximal solution and its `τ_⋆` are the output of
33  `exists_sde_solution_maximal` on `liftedFree d N` (bundled as `IsMaximalParticleSolution`);
34  the source's force-truncation construction (L11740-11756) is already inside SI-01 and is not
35  repeated.
36* **Cluster dispersion on the torus.**  Instead of a tubular neighbourhood of `Δ_I` we use the
37  geodesic representative `geoRep z = centeredRep (mk z) ∈ (-1/2,1/2]^d` and a smooth periodic
38  pair function `pairDispersion x = χ(c)|c|² + (1-χ(c))/64`, `c = centeredRep x`, with `χ` a
39  smooth bump equal to `1` on `|c| ≤ 1/8` and `0` on `|c| ≥ 1/4`.  Then
40  `Q_I(X) = (2k)⁻¹ ∑_{i,j∈I} pairDispersion(x_i - x_j)`; it is smooth, `Q_I⁻¹(0) = Δ_I`, and on
41  `{Q_I < clusterRadius I}` (`= 1/(128 k)`) every internal pair is within geodesic distance
42  `1/8`, so `Q_I` is *exactly* the Euclidean expression `k⁻¹∑_{i<j}|ỹ_i - ỹ_j|²` of the
43  compatible lifts `ỹ` and the derivative identities of L11805-11810 hold verbatim.
44* **Itô equation.**  The generator of the lifted SDE applied to `Q_I` is `clusterGenerator`
45  (`D Q_I[b] + ½ ∑_k D² Q_I[σ_k, σ_k]`, the drift of `ito_formula_localized`).  The singular
46  internal term `𝒜_I` is `clusterInternal` (`B:eq:cluster-internal-formula` with the Euclidean
47  singular force `eucRieszGrad`), and `ℛ_I := clusterGenerator - 2d(k-1)/β - 𝒜_I`
48  (`clusterRemainder`), exactly as the source defines it (L11785, L11839-11844).
49* **Kernel inputs.**  The analytic lemmas take the interface `KernelLocal d s` (smoothness of
50  `∇g` off `0` and the local form `∇g = ∇g_E + smooth` with the smooth part vanishing at `0`).
51  For `d ≥ 2` it is discharged from `HRS/Kernel/Riesz.lean` and `HRS/PDE/Convention.lean` plus
52  the gradient identification `periodicRieszGrad_hasGradientAt` (section `KernelInputs`,
53  from `HRS/Kernel/GradSmooth.lean`); for `d = 1` the inputs are proved in
54  `HRS/Kernel/D1.lean` (section `KernelD1Inputs`).
55* **Temperature.**  `β_N = ∞` is `invβ = 0`; the barrier proofs are uniform in `invβ ≥ 0`
56  (the diffusion contribution to `ℒ(-log Q_I)` is `(4 - 2d(k-1)) invβ / Q_I ≤ 0`, which is `0`
57  at zero temperature), so no separate Grönwall argument is needed.
58* **Random initial configuration.**  The barrier lemmas are stated for the random `Y₀` of the
59  maximal solution directly (no moment assumption): the martingale term is killed by the
60  set-integral form of optional stopping on the `ℱ_0`-measurable sets `{Q_I(Y₀) ≥ 1/m}`, which
61  exhaust `Ω` since `Y₀ ∈ liftedFree`.  This replaces the source's conditioning/Fubini step
62  (L11999).
63* `exists_isStrongSolution_of_no_exit` of `Bridge.lean` originally quantified over stopping
64  times without positivity, making its `hnoexit` premise unsatisfiable (`τ ≡ 0`, `Y ≡ Y₀`);
65  it now requires `0 < τ`.  `exists_isStrongSolution_of_maximal` below is the same reduction
66  phrased through `IsMaximalParticleSolution`.
67-/
68
69noncomputable section

78/-! ### The Euclidean singular force and the kernel interface -/
79
80open Classical in
81/-- The radial coefficient `a_s(z) > 0` of the Euclidean singular force (M L11815):
82`∇|z|^{-s} = -s|z|^{-s-2} z` for `s > 0`, `∇(-log|z|) = -|z|^{-2} z` for `s = 0`. -/
83def rieszRadial (s : ℝ) (z : Euc d) : ℝ :=
84  if s = 0 then ‖z‖ ^ (-2 : ℝ) else s * ‖z‖ ^ (-s - 2)
85
86/-- The Euclidean singular force `∇g_E(z) = -a_s(z) z` (M L11815). -/
87def eucRieszGrad (s : ℝ) (z : Euc d) : Euc d := -(rieszRadial s z) • z
88
89theorem rieszRadial_pos {s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) {z : Euc d} (hz : z ≠ 0) : 0 < rieszRadial s z := by
90  unfold rieszRadial
91  have hn : 0 < ‖z‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hz
92  split_ifs with h
93  · exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hn _
94  · exact mul_pos (lt_of_le_of_ne hs (Ne.symm h)) (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hn _)
95
96/-- The local kernel facts used by the particle analysis, for a given dimension and exponent:
97`∇g` is smooth off the origin, and near the origin `∇g(mk x) = ∇g_E(x) + r(mk x)` with `r`
98smooth and `r(0) = 0` (the gradient of the smooth even remainder `h` of the local decomposition
99`g = g_E + h`, M L11839-11842).  Discharged by `kernelLocal_of_two_le` (`d ≥ 2`) and
100`kernelLocal_d1` (`d = 1`). -/
101structure KernelLocal (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : Prop where
102  smoothOffZero : Torus.IsSmoothOffZero (periodicRieszGrad d s)
103  local_sing : ∃ (r : Torus d → Euc d) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧ r 0 = 0 ∧
104    ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
105      periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x) = eucRieszGrad s x + r (Torus.mk x)
106
107section KernelInputs
108
109variable {s : ℝ}
110
111/-- **Kernel input (`d ≥ 2`).**  `periodicRieszGrad` is the gradient of the lift of
112`periodicRiesz` away from the origin.  Proved in `HRS/Kernel/GradSmooth.lean`
113(`KernelGrad.periodicRieszGrad_hasGradientAt`). -/
114theorem periodicRieszGrad_hasGradientAt (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (x : Euc d)
115    (hx : Torus.mk x ≠ 0) :
116    HasGradientAt (Torus.lift (periodicRiesz d s)) (periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x)) x :=
117  KernelGrad.periodicRieszGrad_hasGradientAt hd hs0 hsd hx x rfl
118
119theorem norm_sq_rpow (y : Euc d) (t : ℝ) : (‖y‖ ^ 2) ^ t = ‖y‖ ^ (2 * t) := by
120  rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul (norm_nonneg _)]; norm_num
121
122/-- Gradient of the Euclidean Riesz singularity `|z|^{-s}`, `s > 0`. -/
123theorem hasGradientAt_norm_rpow_neg (hs : 0 < s) {y : Euc d} (hy : y ≠ 0) :
124    HasGradientAt (fun z : Euc d => ‖z‖ ^ (-s)) (eucRieszGrad s y) y := by
125  have hn : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy
126  have hfun : (fun z : Euc d => ‖z‖ ^ (-s)) = fun z => (‖z‖ ^ 2) ^ (-s / 2) := by
127    funext z; rw [norm_sq_rpow]; ring_nf
128  rw [hfun, hasGradientAt_iff_hasFDerivAt]
129  have h := (hasStrictFDerivAt_norm_sq y).hasFDerivAt.rpow_const (p := -s / 2)
130    (Or.inl (by positivity))
131  convert h using 1
132  ext v
133  simp only [eucRieszGrad, rieszRadial, if_neg hs.ne', InnerProductSpace.toDual_apply_apply,
134    ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply, innerSL_apply_apply, smul_eq_mul, inner_smul_left,
135    nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat, conj_trivial]
136  rw [norm_sq_rpow]
137  ring_nf
138
139/-- Gradient of the Euclidean logarithmic singularity `-log|z|`. -/
140theorem hasGradientAt_neg_log_norm {y : Euc d} (hy : y ≠ 0) :
141    HasGradientAt (fun z : Euc d => -Real.log ‖z‖) (eucRieszGrad 0 y) y := by
142  have hn : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy
143  have hfun : (fun z : Euc d => -Real.log ‖z‖) = fun z => -(1 / 2 : ℝ) * Real.log (‖z‖ ^ 2) := by
144    funext z; rw [Real.log_pow]; push_cast; ring
145  rw [hfun, hasGradientAt_iff_hasFDerivAt]
146  have h := ((hasStrictFDerivAt_norm_sq y).hasFDerivAt.log (by positivity)).const_mul (-(1 / 2 : ℝ))
147  convert h using 1
148  ext v
149  simp only [eucRieszGrad, rieszRadial, InnerProductSpace.toDual_apply_apply,
150    ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply, innerSL_apply_apply, smul_eq_mul, inner_smul_left,
151    nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat, conj_trivial]
152  simp only [ite_true]
153  rw [Real.rpow_neg (norm_nonneg _), Real.rpow_two]
154  field_simp
155
156theorem mk_ne_zero_of_norm_lt_one {x : Euc d} (hx : ‖x‖ < 1) (hx0 : x ≠ 0) : Torus.mk x ≠ 0 := by
157  intro h
158  apply hx0
159  ext i
160  have hi : ((x i : ℝ) : AddCircle (1 : ℝ)) = 0 := congrFun h i
161  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := (AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff (1 : ℝ)).mp hi
162  have hle : |x i| ≤ ‖x‖ := by
163    have := PiLp.norm_apply_le x i
164    rwa [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
165  rw [← hn] at hle
166  have h1 : |(n : ℝ)| < 1 := by simpa using hle.trans_lt hx
167  have h2 : |n| < 1 := by exact_mod_cast h1
168  have h3 : n = 0 := by rw [abs_lt] at h2; omega
169  rw [← hn, h3]; simp
170
171/-- From a local decomposition `g = sing(|x|) + r₀` with `∇ sing(|·|) = ∇g_E`, the local form of
172the force. -/
173theorem local_grad_of_decomp (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (sing : ℝ → ℝ)
174    (r0 : Torus d → ℝ) (δ : ℝ) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hr0 : Torus.IsSmooth r0)
175    (hloc : ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
176      periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk x) = sing ‖x‖ + r0 (Torus.mk x))
177    (hsing : ∀ y : Euc d, y ≠ 0 → HasGradientAt (fun z : Euc d => sing ‖z‖) (eucRieszGrad s y) y) :
178    ∃ (r : Torus d → Euc d) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧ r 0 = 0 ∧
179      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
180        periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x) = eucRieszGrad s x + r (Torus.mk x) := by
181  have hc : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift r0) := hr0
182  have hdiff : Differentiable ℝ (Torus.lift r0) := hc.differentiable (by simp)
183  refine ⟨Torus.grad r0, min δ 1, lt_min hδ one_pos, KernelD1Aux.isSmooth_grad hr0, ?_, ?_⟩
184  · have h1 : Torus.grad r0 0 = gradient (Torus.lift r0) 0 := by
185      have := KernelD1Aux.lift_grad r0 0
186      have hm : Torus.mk (0 : Euc d) = 0 := by funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
187      simpa [Torus.lift, hm] using this
188    rw [h1]
189    apply KernelD1Aux.gradient_zero_of_even _ (hdiff 0)
190    filter_upwards [Metric.ball_mem_nhds (0 : Euc d) hδ] with z hz
191    have hz' : ‖z‖ < δ := by simpa using hz
192    show r0 (Torus.mk (-z)) = r0 (Torus.mk z)
193    by_cases hz0 : z = 0
194    · simp [hz0]
195    · have h2 := hloc z hz' hz0
196      have h3 := hloc (-z) (by simpa using hz') (neg_ne_zero.mpr hz0)
197      have hmn : Torus.mk (-z) = -Torus.mk z := by funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
198      have hg : periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk (-z)) = periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk z) := by
199        rw [hmn, periodicRiesz_neg]
200      rw [norm_neg] at h3
201      linarith
202  · intro x hx hx0
203    have hxδ : ‖x‖ < δ := lt_of_lt_of_le hx (min_le_left _ _)
204    have hx1 : ‖x‖ < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le hx (min_le_right _ _)
205    have hG := periodicRieszGrad_hasGradientAt hd hs0 hsd x
206      (mk_ne_zero_of_norm_lt_one hx1 hx0)
207    have hR : HasGradientAt (Torus.lift r0) (gradient (Torus.lift r0) x) x :=
208      (hdiff x).hasGradientAt
209    have h1 := hsing x hx0
210    rw [hasGradientAt_iff_hasFDerivAt] at h1 hR
211    have hS := h1.add hR
212    have hopen : IsOpen {z : Euc d | ‖z‖ < δ ∧ z ≠ 0} :=
213      (isOpen_lt continuous_norm continuous_const).inter isOpen_ne
214    have hev : (fun z : Euc d => periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk z)) =ᶠ[𝓝 x]
215        (fun z => sing ‖z‖ + Torus.lift r0 z) := by
216      filter_upwards [hopen.mem_nhds ⟨hxδ, hx0⟩] with z hz
217      exact hloc z hz.1 hz.2
218    have hS' : HasGradientAt (fun z : Euc d => periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk z))
219        (eucRieszGrad s x + gradient (Torus.lift r0) x) x := by
220      rw [hasGradientAt_iff_hasFDerivAt, map_add]
221      exact HasFDerivAt.congr_of_eventuallyEq hS hev
222    rw [hG.unique hS', ← KernelD1Aux.lift_grad r0 x]
223    rfl
224
225theorem Torus.grad_mk_aux (φ : Torus d → ℝ) (y : Euc d) :
226    Torus.grad φ (Torus.mk y) = gradient (Torus.lift φ) y := by
227  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := Torus.rep_mk y
228  unfold Torus.grad
229  rw [hn]
230  have hper : (fun x => Torus.lift φ (x + Lattice.toEuc n)) = Torus.lift φ :=
231    funext fun x => Torus.lift_periodic φ x n
232  unfold gradient
233  rw [← fderiv_comp_add_right (Lattice.toEuc n) (f := Torus.lift φ) (x := y), hper]
234
235theorem mk_neg_euc (x : Euc d) : Torus.mk (-x) = -Torus.mk x := by
236  funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
237
238/-- Local form of the force for `d ≥ 2` (M L11839: `g = g_E + h`, `h` smooth and even, hence
239`∇h(0) = 0`).  From `periodicRiesz_local_singularity` / `periodicLog_local_singularity`
240(KERN-01b), `periodicRiesz_neg`, and `periodicRieszGrad_hasGradientAt`. -/
241theorem periodicRieszGrad_local_singularity (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
242    ∃ (r : Torus d → Euc d) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧ r 0 = 0 ∧
243      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
244        periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x) = eucRieszGrad s x + r (Torus.mk x) := by
245  by_cases hs : s = 0
246  · subst hs
247    obtain ⟨r0, δ, hδ, hr0, hloc⟩ := periodicLog_local_singularity hd
248    exact local_grad_of_decomp hd hs0 hsd (fun r => -Real.log r) r0 δ hδ hr0 hloc
249      (fun y hy => hasGradientAt_neg_log_norm hy)
250  · have hspos : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm hs)
251    obtain ⟨r0, δ, hδ, hr0, hloc⟩ := periodicRiesz_local_singularity hd hspos hsd
252    exact local_grad_of_decomp hd hs0 hsd (fun r => r ^ (-s)) r0 δ hδ hr0 hloc
253      (fun y hy => hasGradientAt_norm_rpow_neg hspos hy)
254
255/-- The kernel interface for `d ≥ 2`, from `KernelGrad.periodicRieszGrad_smoothOffZero`
256(`HRS/Kernel/GradConv.lean`, stated with a textually identical local copy of
257`Torus.IsSmoothOffZero`) and `periodicRieszGrad_local_singularity`. -/
258theorem kernelLocal_of_two_le (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) : KernelLocal d s :=
259  ⟨KernelGrad.periodicRieszGrad_smoothOffZero hd hs0 hsd,
260    periodicRieszGrad_local_singularity hd hs0 hsd⟩
261
262end KernelInputs

266/-! The one-dimensional kernel facts needed by cases (ii)-(iii) of Proposition B.1 and by
267Lemma `B:lem:d1-pair-barrier` are proved in `HRS/Kernel/D1.lean`
268(`periodicRiesz_local_singularity_d1`, `periodicLog_local_singularity_d1`,

270`periodicRieszGrad_local_singularity_d1`). That file states the last one with its own copy
271`KernelD1.eucRieszGrad` of the Euclidean force, which is definitionally `eucRieszGrad`. -/
272
273variable {s : ℝ}
274
275theorem kernelD1_eucRieszGrad_eq : (KernelD1.eucRieszGrad s : Euc 1 → Euc 1) = eucRieszGrad s :=
276  rfl

278/-- The kernel interface for `d = 1`, assembled from the results of `HRS/Kernel/D1.lean`. -/
279theorem kernelLocal_d1 (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hs1 : s < 1) : KernelLocal 1 s :=
280  ⟨periodicRieszGrad_smoothOffZero_d1 hs0 hs1, periodicRieszGrad_local_singularity_d1 hs0 hs1⟩
281
282end KernelD1Inputs
283
284/-! ### Cluster dispersion `Q_I` on the torus (M L11759-11779) -/
285
286/-- The geodesic representative of a covering-space vector: the representative of `mk z` in
287`(-1/2, 1/2]^d`.  For `‖z - n‖ < 1/4`, `n ∈ ℤ^d`, it is `z - n`. -/
288def geoRep (z : Euc d) : Euc d := Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk z)
289
290/-- The smooth cutoff `χ` on `ℝ^d`: `1` on `|z| ≤ 1/8`, `0` on `|z| ≥ 1/4`, values in `[0,1]`. -/
291def clusterBump (d : ℕ) : ContDiffBump (0 : Euc d) :=
292  ⟨1 / 8, 1 / 4, by norm_num, by norm_num⟩
293
294/-- The periodic pair dispersion `ψ(x) = χ(c)|c|² + (1 - χ(c))/64` with `c = centeredRep x`:
295a smooth function on `𝕋^d`, equal to the squared geodesic distance `|c|²` for `|c| ≤ 1/8`,
296`≥ 1/64` for `|c| ≥ 1/8`, vanishing exactly at `x = 0`. -/
297def pairDispersion (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
298  clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x) * ‖Torus.centeredRep x‖ ^ 2 +
299    (1 - clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x)) * (1 / 64)
300
301/-- The cluster dispersion `Q_I(X) = (2k)⁻¹ ∑_{i,j∈I} ψ(x_i - x_j)` on `(𝕋^d)^N`
302(`B:eq:cluster-dispersion-rd`, M L11762-11766, with `k⁻¹∑_{i<j} = (2k)⁻¹∑_{i,j}`; the smooth
303torus version of M L11775-11779). -/
304def clusterDispersion (I : Finset (Fin N)) (X : Config d N) : ℝ :=
305  (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, pairDispersion (X i - X j)
306
307/-- The lift of `Q_I` to the covering space `(ℝ^d)^N`. -/
308def clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : ℝ :=
309  clusterDispersion I fun i => Torus.mk (Y i)
310
311/-- The cluster diagonal `Δ_I = {X : x_i = x_{i'} for all i, i' ∈ I}` (M L11777). -/
312def clusterDiagonal (I : Finset (Fin N)) : Set (Config d N) :=
313  {X | ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, X i = X j}
314
315/-- The radius `q_I = 1/(128 k)` below which `Q_I` is the Euclidean expression of the compatible
316lifts (every internal pair is within geodesic distance `1/8`). -/
317def clusterRadius (I : Finset (Fin N)) : ℝ := 1 / (128 * (I.card : ℝ))
318
319/-- `x_i - x̄_I` in the compatible lift: `k⁻¹ ∑_{j∈I} geoRep(y_i - y_j)` for `i ∈ I`, `0`
320otherwise. -/
321def clusterCentered (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) (i : Fin N) : Euc d :=
322  if i ∈ I then (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) else 0
323
324/-- Coordinates of `rep` lie in `[0,1)`. -/
325theorem Torus.rep_coord_mem (x : Torus d) (i : Fin d) : 0 ≤ Torus.rep x i ∧ Torus.rep x i < 1 := by
326  have h := (AddCircle.equivIco (1 : ℝ) 0 (x i)).2
327  simpa [Torus.rep] using h
328
329theorem Torus.centeredRep_apply (x : Torus d) (i : Fin d) :
330    Torus.centeredRep x i = if Torus.rep x i ≤ 1 / 2 then Torus.rep x i else Torus.rep x i - 1 :=
331  rfl
332
333/-- `mk` of the centered representative is the original point. -/
334theorem Torus.mk_centeredRep (x : Torus d) : Torus.mk (Torus.centeredRep x) = x := by
335  funext i
336  have h : (((Torus.rep x) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = x i := congrFun (Torus.mk_rep x) i
337  show (((Torus.centeredRep x) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = x i
338  rw [Torus.centeredRep_apply]
339  split_ifs with hc
340  · exact h
341  · rw [AddCircle.coe_sub, AddCircle.coe_period, sub_zero]; exact h
342
343/-- The coordinates of `geoRep z` lie in `(-1/2, 1/2]`. -/
344theorem geoRep_coord (z : Euc d) (i : Fin d) : -(1 / 2) < geoRep z i ∧ geoRep z i ≤ 1 / 2 := by
345  obtain ⟨h0, h1⟩ := Torus.rep_coord_mem (Torus.mk z) i
346  show -(1 / 2) < Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk z) i ∧ Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk z) i ≤ 1 / 2
347  rw [Torus.centeredRep_apply]
348  split_ifs with hc
349  · constructor <;> linarith
350  · constructor <;> linarith [not_le.1 hc]
351
352/-- `geoRep z` is congruent to `z` modulo `ℤ^d`, coordinatewise. -/
353theorem geoRep_int (z : Euc d) (i : Fin d) : ∃ m : ℤ, z i - geoRep z i = m := by
354  have h : (((geoRep z i : ℝ)) : UnitAddCircle) = ((z i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) :=
355    congrFun (Torus.mk_centeredRep (Torus.mk z)) i
356  have h0 : (((z i - geoRep z i : ℝ)) : UnitAddCircle) = 0 := by
357    rw [AddCircle.coe_sub, sub_eq_zero]
358    exact h.symm
359  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := (AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff (1 : ℝ)).1 h0
360  exact ⟨n, by simpa using hn.symm⟩
361
362/-- Characterisation of `geoRep`: the unique vector with coordinates in `(-1/2,1/2]` that is
363congruent to `z` modulo `ℤ^d`. -/
364theorem eq_geoRep_of (z w : Euc d) (hw : ∀ i, -(1 / 2) < w i ∧ w i ≤ 1 / 2)
365    (hint : ∀ i, ∃ m : ℤ, z i - w i = m) : geoRep z = w := by
366  ext i
367  obtain ⟨m1, h1⟩ := hint i
368  obtain ⟨m2, h2⟩ := geoRep_int z i
369  obtain ⟨a1, a2⟩ := hw i
370  obtain ⟨b1, b2⟩ := geoRep_coord z i
371  have h3 : ((m1 - m2 : ℤ) : ℝ) = geoRep z i - w i := by push_cast; linarith
372  have h4 : ((m1 - m2 : ℤ) : ℝ) < 1 := by rw [h3]; linarith
373  have h5 : (-1 : ℝ) < ((m1 - m2 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by rw [h3]; linarith
374  have h6 : m1 - m2 < 1 := by exact_mod_cast h4
375  have h7 : -1 < m1 - m2 := by exact_mod_cast h5
376  have h8 : m1 - m2 = 0 := by omega
377  rw [h8] at h3
378  simp at h3
379  linarith
380
381theorem geoRep_zero : geoRep (0 : Euc d) = 0 := by
382  refine eq_geoRep_of 0 0 (fun i => by simp) (fun i => ⟨0, by simp⟩)
383
384/-- `geoRep z = z - v` when `v ∈ ℤ^d` and `z - v` has coordinates in `(-1/2,1/2]`. -/
385theorem geoRep_eq_sub (z v : Euc d) (hv : ∀ i, ∃ m : ℤ, v i = m)
386    (hz : ∀ i, -(1 / 2) < (z - v) i ∧ (z - v) i ≤ 1 / 2) : geoRep z = z - v := by
387  refine eq_geoRep_of z (z - v) hz fun i => ?_
388  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hv i
389  exact ⟨m, by simpa using hm⟩
390
391/-- `geoRep z = z - n` when `‖z - n‖ < 1/4`, `n ∈ ℤ^d`. -/
392theorem geoRep_eq_of_norm_lt (z : Euc d) (n : Lattice d) (h : ‖z - Lattice.toEuc n‖ < 1 / 4) :
393    geoRep z = z - Lattice.toEuc n := by
394  refine geoRep_eq_sub z _ (fun i => ⟨n i, by simp [Lattice.toEuc]⟩) fun i => ?_
395  have := PiLp.norm_apply_le (z - Lattice.toEuc n) i
396  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
397  constructor <;> linarith [abs_le.1 (this.trans h.le)]
398
399theorem coord_abs_le_norm (w : Euc d) (i : Fin d) : |w i| ≤ ‖w‖ := by
400  have := PiLp.norm_apply_le w i
401  rwa [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
402
403/-- The helper function `F(w) = χ(w)|w|² + (1-χ(w))/64`, so that `ψ(x) = F(centeredRep x)`. -/
404def pairDispersionF (w : Euc d) : ℝ :=
405  clusterBump d w * ‖w‖ ^ 2 + (1 - clusterBump d w) * (1 / 64)
406
407theorem pairDispersion_eq_F (x : Torus d) :
408    pairDispersion x = pairDispersionF (Torus.centeredRep x) := rfl
409
410theorem contDiff_pairDispersionF : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (pairDispersionF (d := d)) := by
411  unfold pairDispersionF
412  exact ((clusterBump d).contDiff.mul (contDiff_norm_sq ℝ)).add
413    ((contDiff_const.sub (clusterBump d).contDiff).mul contDiff_const)
414
415theorem pairDispersionF_eq_of_le_norm {w : Euc d} (hw : 1 / 4 ≤ ‖w‖) :
416    pairDispersionF w = 1 / 64 := by
417  unfold pairDispersionF
418  have : clusterBump d w = 0 :=
419    (clusterBump d).zero_of_le_dist (by simpa [clusterBump, dist_zero_right] using hw)
420  rw [this]; ring
421
422theorem pairDispersion_nonneg (x : Torus d) : 0 ≤ pairDispersion x := by
423  rw [pairDispersion_eq_F]
424  unfold pairDispersionF
425  have h0 : 0 ≤ clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x) := (clusterBump d).nonneg
426  have h1 : clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x) ≤ 1 := (clusterBump d).le_one
427  exact add_nonneg (mul_nonneg h0 (sq_nonneg _)) (mul_nonneg (by linarith) (by norm_num))
428
429theorem Torus.mk_zero : Torus.mk (0 : Euc d) = 0 := by
430  funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
431
432/-- `ψ` vanishes exactly at the origin. -/
433theorem pairDispersion_eq_zero_iff (x : Torus d) : pairDispersion x = 0 ↔ x = 0 := by
434  constructor
435  · intro h
436    rw [pairDispersion_eq_F] at h
437    unfold pairDispersionF at h
438    set c := Torus.centeredRep x with hc
439    have h0 : 0 ≤ clusterBump d c := (clusterBump d).nonneg
440    have h1 : clusterBump d c ≤ 1 := (clusterBump d).le_one
441    have ha : 0 ≤ clusterBump d c * ‖c‖ ^ 2 := mul_nonneg h0 (sq_nonneg _)
442    have hb : 0 ≤ (1 - clusterBump d c) * (1 / 64) := mul_nonneg (by linarith) (by norm_num)
443    have hb' : (1 - clusterBump d c) * (1 / 64) = 0 := by linarith
444    have hchi : clusterBump d c = 1 := by linarith
445    have ha' : clusterBump d c * ‖c‖ ^ 2 = 0 := by linarith
446    rw [hchi, one_mul] at ha'
447    have hc0 : c = 0 := norm_eq_zero.1 (pow_eq_zero_iff (by norm_num) |>.1 ha')
448    rw [← Torus.mk_centeredRep x, ← hc, hc0, Torus.mk_zero]
449  · rintro rfl
450    have : Torus.centeredRep (0 : Torus d) = 0 := by
451      have := geoRep_zero (d := d)
452      rwa [geoRep, Torus.mk_zero] at this
453    rw [pairDispersion_eq_F, this]
454    unfold pairDispersionF
455    have : clusterBump d (0 : Euc d) = 1 :=
456      (clusterBump d).one_of_mem_closedBall (by simp [clusterBump])
457    rw [this]; simp
458
459/-- `ψ` is the squared geodesic distance on `{|c| ≤ 1/8}`. -/
460theorem pairDispersion_eq_sq (x : Torus d) (hx : ‖Torus.centeredRep x‖ ≤ 1 / 8) :
461    pairDispersion x = ‖Torus.centeredRep x‖ ^ 2 := by
462  rw [pairDispersion_eq_F]
463  unfold pairDispersionF
464  have : clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x) = 1 :=
465    (clusterBump d).one_of_mem_closedBall (by simpa [clusterBump] using hx)
466  rw [this]; ring
467
468/-- `ψ ≥ 1/64` off `{|c| < 1/8}`. -/
469theorem le_pairDispersion (x : Torus d) (hx : 1 / 8 ≤ ‖Torus.centeredRep x‖) :
470    1 / 64 ≤ pairDispersion x := by
471  rw [pairDispersion_eq_F]
472  unfold pairDispersionF
473  have h0 : 0 ≤ clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x) := (clusterBump d).nonneg
474  have h1 : clusterBump d (Torus.centeredRep x) ≤ 1 := (clusterBump d).le_one
475  have h2 : 1 / 64 ≤ ‖Torus.centeredRep x‖ ^ 2 := by nlinarith
476  nlinarith
477
478/-- `ψ ∈ C^∞(𝕋^d)`. -/
479theorem pairDispersion_smooth : Torus.IsSmooth (pairDispersion (d := d)) := by
480  unfold Torus.IsSmooth Torus.IsCk
481  rw [contDiff_iff_contDiffAt]
482  intro z0
483  have hlift : ∀ z, Torus.lift (pairDispersion (d := d)) z = pairDispersionF (geoRep z) :=
484    fun z => rfl
485  by_cases h : ∀ i, geoRep z0 i < 1 / 2
486  · -- away from the cut locus: `geoRep` is locally `z - v`
487    set v : Euc d := z0 - geoRep z0 with hv
488    have hvint : ∀ i, ∃ m : ℤ, v i = m := fun i => by
489      obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := geoRep_int z0 i
490      exact ⟨m, by simpa [hv] using hm⟩
491    have hev : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 z0, ∀ i, -(1 / 2) < (z - v) i ∧ (z - v) i ≤ 1 / 2 := by
492      refine Filter.eventually_all.2 fun i => ?_
493      have hc : Continuous fun z : Euc d => (z - v) i := by
494        have : Continuous fun z : Euc d => z i := (EuclideanSpace.proj i : Euc d →L[ℝ] ℝ).continuous
495        exact this.sub continuous_const
496      have e0 : (z0 - v) i = geoRep z0 i := by simp [hv]
497      have h1 : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 z0, -(1 / 2) < (z - v) i :=
498        continuousAt_const.eventually_lt hc.continuousAt (by rw [e0]; exact (geoRep_coord z0 i).1)
499      have h2 : ∀ᶠ z in 𝓝 z0, (z - v) i < 1 / 2 :=
500        hc.continuousAt.eventually_lt continuousAt_const (by rw [e0]; exact h i)
501      filter_upwards [h1, h2] with z a b using ⟨a, b.le⟩
502    have heq : Torus.lift (pairDispersion (d := d)) =ᶠ[𝓝 z0] fun z => pairDispersionF (z - v) := by
503      filter_upwards [hev] with z hz
504      rw [hlift, geoRep_eq_sub z v hvint hz]
505    exact ((contDiff_pairDispersionF.comp (contDiff_id.sub contDiff_const)).contDiffAt).congr_of_eventuallyEq heq
506  · -- at the cut locus: `ψ ≡ 1/64` nearby
507    push Not at h
508    obtain ⟨i0, hi0⟩ := h
509    have hi0' : geoRep z0 i0 = 1 / 2 := le_antisymm (geoRep_coord z0 i0).2 hi0
510    have heq : Torus.lift (pairDispersion (d := d)) =ᶠ[𝓝 z0] fun _ => (1 / 64 : ℝ) := by
511      have hball : Metric.ball z0 (1 / 4) ∈ 𝓝 z0 := Metric.ball_mem_nhds _ (by norm_num)
512      filter_upwards [hball] with z hz
513      rw [hlift]
514      apply pairDispersionF_eq_of_le_norm
515      refine le_trans ?_ (coord_abs_le_norm (geoRep z) i0)
516      by_contra hlt
517      push Not at hlt
518      obtain ⟨m1, h1⟩ := geoRep_int z i0
519      obtain ⟨m2, h2⟩ := geoRep_int z0 i0
520      have hd : |z i0 - z0 i0| < 1 / 4 := by
521        have := coord_abs_le_norm (z - z0) i0
522        rw [Metric.mem_ball, dist_eq_norm] at hz
523        simpa using this.trans_lt hz
524      have hg := abs_lt.1 hlt
525      have hd' := abs_lt.1 hd
526      have h3 : ((m1 - m2 : ℤ) : ℝ) = (z i0 - z0 i0) - geoRep z i0 + 1 / 2 := by
527        push_cast; rw [hi0'] at h2; linarith
528      have h4 : (0 : ℝ) < ((m1 - m2 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by rw [h3]; linarith
529      have h5 : ((m1 - m2 : ℤ) : ℝ) < 1 := by rw [h3]; linarith
530      have h6 : 0 < m1 - m2 := by exact_mod_cast h4
531      have h7 : m1 - m2 < 1 := by exact_mod_cast h5
532      omega
533    exact (contDiffAt_const).congr_of_eventuallyEq heq
534
535theorem clusterDispersion_nonneg (I : Finset (Fin N)) (X : Config d N) :
536    0 ≤ clusterDispersion I X := by
537  unfold clusterDispersion
538  exact mul_nonneg (by positivity)
539    (Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => pairDispersion_nonneg _)
540
541/-- `Q_I⁻¹(0) = Δ_I` (M L11779). -/
542theorem clusterDispersion_eq_zero_iff (I : Finset (Fin N)) (X : Config d N) :
543    clusterDispersion I X = 0 ↔ X ∈ clusterDiagonal I := by
544  unfold clusterDispersion clusterDiagonal
545  constructor
546  · intro h i hi j hj
547    have hk : (0 : ℝ) < I.card := by exact_mod_cast Finset.card_pos.2 ⟨i, hi⟩
548    have hsum : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, pairDispersion (X i - X j) = 0 := by
549      rcases mul_eq_zero.1 h with h1 | h1
550      · exfalso; revert h1; positivity
551      · exact h1
552    have h2 := (Finset.sum_eq_zero_iff_of_nonneg
553      (fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => pairDispersion_nonneg (X i - X j))).1 hsum i hi
554    have h3 := (Finset.sum_eq_zero_iff_of_nonneg
555      (fun j _ => pairDispersion_nonneg (X i - X j))).1 h2 j hj
556    exact sub_eq_zero.1 ((pairDispersion_eq_zero_iff _).1 h3)
557  · intro h
558    have : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, pairDispersion (X i - X j) = 0 := fun i hi j hj => by
559      rw [h i hi j hj, sub_self]; exact (pairDispersion_eq_zero_iff 0).2 rfl
560    rw [Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i hi => Finset.sum_eq_zero fun j hj => this i hi j hj, mul_zero]
561
562/-- `Q_I ∈ C^∞((𝕋^d)^N)`: its lift to the covering space is `C^∞` (M L11779). -/
563theorem contDiff_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) :
564    ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I) := by
565  have hfun : clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I = fun Y => (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) *
566      ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, Torus.lift pairDispersion (Y i - Y j) := by
567    funext Y
568    unfold clusterDispersionLift clusterDispersion
569    congr 1
570  have hs : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift (pairDispersion (d := d))) :=
571    pairDispersion_smooth
572  rw [hfun]
573  refine contDiff_const.mul (ContDiff.sum fun i _ => ContDiff.sum fun j _ => ?_)
574  exact hs.comp ((contDiff_apply ℝ _ i).sub (contDiff_apply ℝ _ j))
575
576/-- Pairwise identity for vectors: `∑_{i,j}|a_i - a_j|² = 2k ∑|a_i|² - 2|∑ a_i|²`. -/
577theorem sum_sum_norm_sub_sq {ι V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [InnerProductSpace ℝ V]
578    (I : Finset ι) (a : ι → V) :
579    ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖a i - a j‖ ^ 2 =
580      2 * (I.card : ℝ) * ∑ i ∈ I, ‖a i‖ ^ 2 - 2 * ‖∑ i ∈ I, a i‖ ^ 2 := by
581  have h : ∀ i j, ‖a i - a j‖ ^ 2 = ‖a i‖ ^ 2 - 2 * inner ℝ (a i) (a j) + ‖a j‖ ^ 2 :=
582    fun i j => norm_sub_sq_real _ _
583  have hin : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ (a i) (a j) = ‖∑ i ∈ I, a i‖ ^ 2 := by
584    rw [← real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq, sum_inner]
585    simp_rw [inner_sum]
586  simp_rw [h, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hin,
587    Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
588  rw [← Finset.mul_sum]
589  ring
590
591/-- Mean identity: `∑|a_i - ā|² = ∑|a_i|² - |∑ a_i|²/k`. -/
592theorem sum_norm_sub_mean_sq {ι V : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup V] [InnerProductSpace ℝ V]
593    (I : Finset ι) (a : ι → V) :
594    ∑ i ∈ I, ‖a i - (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ I, a j‖ ^ 2 =
595      ∑ i ∈ I, ‖a i‖ ^ 2 - ‖∑ i ∈ I, a i‖ ^ 2 / (I.card : ℝ) := by
596  by_cases hk : I.card = 0
597  · rw [Finset.card_eq_zero.1 hk]; simp
598  have hk' : (I.card : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hk
599  set S := ∑ j ∈ I, a j with hS
600  have h : ∀ i, ‖a i - (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • S‖ ^ 2 =
601      ‖a i‖ ^ 2 - 2 * (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) * inner ℝ (a i) S + (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) ^ 2 * ‖S‖ ^ 2 := by
602    intro i
603    rw [norm_sub_sq_real, real_inner_smul_right, norm_smul, mul_pow, Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
604    ring
605  have hin : ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ (a i) S = ‖S‖ ^ 2 := by
606    rw [← real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq, ← sum_inner]
607  simp_rw [h, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hin,
608    Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
609  field_simp
610  ring
611
612/-- Compatibility of geodesic representatives: if `y_p, y_q` are within `1/8` of `y_r`, then
613`geoRep(y_p - y_q) = geoRep(y_p - y_r) - geoRep(y_q - y_r)`. -/
614theorem geoRep_sub_compat (Y : Fin N → Euc d) (p q r : Fin N)
615    (hp : ‖geoRep (Y p - Y r)‖ < 1 / 8) (hq : ‖geoRep (Y q - Y r)‖ < 1 / 8) :
616    geoRep (Y p - Y q) = geoRep (Y p - Y r) - geoRep (Y q - Y r) := by
617  refine eq_geoRep_of _ _ (fun i => ?_) (fun i => ?_)
618  · have h1 := coord_abs_le_norm (geoRep (Y p - Y r)) i
619    have h2 := coord_abs_le_norm (geoRep (Y q - Y r)) i
620    have h3 := abs_le.1 (h1.trans hp.le)
621    have h4 := abs_le.1 (h2.trans hq.le)
622    simp only [PiLp.sub_apply]
623    constructor <;> linarith [h3.1, h3.2, h4.1, h4.2]
624  · obtain ⟨m1, h1⟩ := geoRep_int (Y p - Y r) i
625    obtain ⟨m2, h2⟩ := geoRep_int (Y q - Y r) i
626    refine ⟨m1 - m2, ?_⟩
627    simp only [PiLp.sub_apply] at h1 h2 ⊢
628    push_cast
629    linarith
630
631/-- When all internal pairs are within geodesic distance `1/8`, `Q_I` is the Euclidean expression
632of the compatible lifts. -/
633theorem cluster_euclid_of_pairs (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
634    (hpairs : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8) :
635    clusterDispersionLift I Y =
636        (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 ∧
637      clusterDispersionLift I Y = ∑ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 := by
638  have h1 : clusterDispersionLift I Y =
639      (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 := by
640    unfold clusterDispersionLift clusterDispersion
641    congr 1
642    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
643    rw [← mk_sub']
644    exact pairDispersion_eq_sq _ (hpairs i hi j hj).le
645  refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
646  by_cases hI : I = ∅
647  · subst hI; simp [clusterDispersionLift, clusterDispersion]
648  obtain ⟨i0, hi0⟩ := Finset.nonempty_iff_ne_empty.2 hI
649  have hk : (I.card : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (Finset.card_pos.2 ⟨i0, hi0⟩).ne'
650  set a : Fin N → Euc d := fun i => geoRep (Y i - Y i0) with ha
651  have hcomp : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) = a i - a j := fun i hi j hj =>
652    geoRep_sub_compat Y i j i0 (hpairs i hi i0 hi0) (hpairs j hj i0 hi0)
653  have hcc : ∀ i ∈ I, clusterCentered I Y i = a i - (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ I, a j := by
654    intro i hi
655    unfold clusterCentered
656    rw [if_pos hi, Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => hcomp i hi j hj, Finset.sum_sub_distrib,
657      Finset.sum_const, smul_sub, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℝ, smul_smul]
658    simp [hk]
659  have e1 : ∑ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 =
660      ∑ i ∈ I, ‖a i - (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ I, a j‖ ^ 2 :=
661    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => by rw [hcc i hi]
662  have e2 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 = ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖a i - a j‖ ^ 2 :=
663    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => by rw [hcomp i hi j hj]
664  rw [h1, e1, e2, sum_norm_sub_mean_sq, sum_sum_norm_sub_sq]
665  field_simp
666
667/-- Below `clusterRadius I` every internal pair is within geodesic distance `1/8` and `Q_I` is
668the Euclidean expression `∑_{i∈I}|ỹ_i - ȳ_I|²` of the compatible lifts (M L11779, L11765). -/
669theorem clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
670    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) :
671    (∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8) ∧
672      clusterDispersionLift I Y =
673        (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 ∧
674      clusterDispersionLift I Y = ∑ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 := by
675  have hk : 0 < I.card := by
676    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos I.card with h | h
677    · exfalso
678      rw [Finset.card_eq_zero.1 h] at hq
679      simp [clusterDispersionLift, clusterDispersion, clusterRadius] at hq
680    · exact h
681  have hk' : (0 : ℝ) < I.card := by exact_mod_cast hk
682  have hpairs : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8 := by
683    intro i hi j hj
684    by_contra hnot
685    push Not at hnot
686    have hle := le_pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y i) - Torus.mk (Y j))
687      (by rw [← mk_sub']; exact hnot)
688    have hsingle : pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y i) - Torus.mk (Y j)) ≤
689        ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y i) - Torus.mk (Y j)) :=
690      (Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y i) - Torus.mk (Y j)))
691        (fun j _ => pairDispersion_nonneg _) hj).trans
692        (Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => ∑ j ∈ I, pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y i) - Torus.mk (Y j)))
693          (fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => pairDispersion_nonneg _) hi)
694    unfold clusterDispersionLift clusterDispersion clusterRadius at hq
695    rw [div_mul_eq_mul_div, one_mul, div_lt_div_iff₀ (by positivity) (by positivity)] at hq
696    nlinarith
697  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := cluster_euclid_of_pairs I Y hpairs
698  exact ⟨hpairs, h1, h2⟩
699
700/-- The enlargement identity `B:eq:cluster-enlargement` (M L11770):
701`Q_{I∪{j}} = Q_I + (k/(k+1)) |x_j - x̄_I|²` for `j ∉ I`, below the radius of `I ∪ {j}`. -/
702theorem clusterDispersionLift_insert (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) (j : Fin N)
703    (hj : j ∉ I) (hq : clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y < clusterRadius (insert j I)) :
704    clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y = clusterDispersionLift I Y +
705      ((I.card : ℝ) / ((I.card : ℝ) + 1)) *
706        ‖(1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ i ∈ I, geoRep (Y j - Y i)‖ ^ 2 := by
707  obtain ⟨hpJ, hJ, -⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid (insert j I) Y hq
708  have hpI : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ l ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y l)‖ < 1 / 8 := fun i hi l hl =>
709    hpJ i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi) l (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hl)
710  have hI := (cluster_euclid_of_pairs I Y hpI).1
711  set a : Fin N → Euc d := fun i => geoRep (Y i - Y j) with ha
712  have haj : a j = 0 := by simp [ha, geoRep_zero]
713  have hcomp : ∀ i ∈ insert j I, ∀ l ∈ insert j I, geoRep (Y i - Y l) = a i - a l :=
714    fun i hi l hl => geoRep_sub_compat Y i l j (hpJ i hi j (Finset.mem_insert_self _ _))
715      (hpJ l hl j (Finset.mem_insert_self _ _))
716  have hcompI : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ l ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y l) = a i - a l := fun i hi l hl =>
717    hcomp i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi) l (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hl)
718  have hneg : ∀ i ∈ I, geoRep (Y j - Y i) = - a i := by
719    intro i hi
720    rw [hcomp j (Finset.mem_insert_self _ _) i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi), haj, zero_sub]
721  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hneg, Finset.sum_neg_distrib, smul_neg, norm_neg]
722  have hcardJ : ((insert j I).card : ℝ) = I.card + 1 := by
723    rw [Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hj]; push_cast; ring
724  have eJ : ∑ i ∈ insert j I, ∑ l ∈ insert j I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y l)‖ ^ 2 =
725      ∑ i ∈ insert j I, ∑ l ∈ insert j I, ‖a i - a l‖ ^ 2 :=
726    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l hl => by rw [hcomp i hi l hl]
727  have eI : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ l ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y l)‖ ^ 2 = ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ l ∈ I, ‖a i - a l‖ ^ 2 :=
728    Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l hl => by rw [hcompI i hi l hl]
729  rw [hJ, hI, hcardJ, eJ, eI]
730  by_cases hk : I.card = 0
731  · rw [Finset.card_eq_zero.1 hk]; simp [haj]
732  have hk' : (I.card : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hk
733  simp only [Finset.sum_insert hj, haj, sub_self, norm_zero, zero_sub, sub_zero, norm_neg,
734    Finset.sum_add_distrib]
735  rw [sum_sum_norm_sub_sq]
736  rw [norm_smul, mul_pow, Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
737  simp
738  field_simp
739  ring
740
741/-- The Euclidean quadratic form `(2k)⁻¹ ∑_{i,j∈I} |(Y_i - n_i) - (Y_j - n_j)|²` with a fixed
742integer shift `n`: the local Euclidean form of `Q_I` below the radius. -/
743def clusterQuad (I : Finset (Fin N)) (n : Fin N → Euc d) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : ℝ :=
744  (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖(Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)‖ ^ 2
745
746theorem fderiv_clusterQuad (I : Finset (Fin N)) (n Y v : Fin N → Euc d) :
747    fderiv ℝ (clusterQuad I n) Y v = (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I,
748      2 * inner ℝ ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) (v i - v j) := by
749  have h1 : ∀ i j, HasFDerivAt (fun Y : Fin N → Euc d => (Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j))
750        ((ContinuousLinearMap.proj i : (Fin N → Euc d) →L[ℝ] Euc d) - ContinuousLinearMap.proj j) Y :=
751    fun i j => ((hasFDerivAt_apply i Y).sub_const (n i)).sub ((hasFDerivAt_apply j Y).sub_const (n j))
752  have hterm := fun i j => (h1 i j).norm_sq
753  have hsum := HasFDerivAt.const_mul (HasFDerivAt.fun_sum (u := I) (fun i _ =>
754    HasFDerivAt.fun_sum (u := I) (fun j _ => hterm i j))) (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ)))
755  have := hsum.fderiv
756  unfold clusterQuad
757  rw [this]
758  simp only [smul_apply, sum_apply,
759    ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, innerSL_apply_apply, sub_apply,
760    ContinuousLinearMap.proj_apply, smul_eq_mul, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat]
761
762theorem cluster_card_pos (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
763    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) : 0 < I.card := by
764  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos I.card with h | h
765  · exfalso
766    rw [Finset.card_eq_zero.1 h] at hq
767    simp [clusterDispersionLift, clusterDispersion, clusterRadius] at hq
768  · exact h
769
770theorem geoRep_antisymm (Y : Fin N → Euc d) (i j : Fin N) (h : ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8) :
771    geoRep (Y j - Y i) = - geoRep (Y i - Y j) := by
772  refine eq_geoRep_of _ _ (fun k => ?_) (fun k => ?_)
773  · have := abs_le.1 ((coord_abs_le_norm (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) k).trans h.le)
774    simp only [PiLp.neg_apply]
775    constructor <;> linarith [this.1, this.2]
776  · obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := geoRep_int (Y i - Y j) k
777    refine ⟨-m, ?_⟩
778    simp only [PiLp.sub_apply, PiLp.neg_apply] at hm ⊢
779    push_cast
780    linarith
781
782theorem clusterDispersionLift_local (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y0 : Fin N → Euc d)
783    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y0 < clusterRadius I) :
784    ∃ n : Fin N → Euc d,
785      (∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, (Y0 i - n i) - (Y0 j - n j) = geoRep (Y0 i - Y0 j)) ∧
786        clusterDispersionLift I =ᶠ[𝓝 Y0] clusterQuad I n := by
787  obtain ⟨hpairs, -, -⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid I Y0 hq
788  obtain ⟨i0, hi0⟩ := Finset.card_pos.1 (cluster_card_pos I Y0 hq)
789  set n : Fin N → Euc d := fun i => (Y0 i - Y0 i0) - geoRep (Y0 i - Y0 i0) with hn
790  have hnint : ∀ i k, ∃ m : ℤ, n i k = m := fun i k => by
791    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := geoRep_int (Y0 i - Y0 i0) k
792    exact ⟨m, by simpa [hn] using hm⟩
793  have hw0 : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, (Y0 i - n i) - (Y0 j - n j) = geoRep (Y0 i - Y0 j) := by
794    intro i hi j hj
795    rw [geoRep_sub_compat Y0 i j i0 (hpairs i hi i0 hi0) (hpairs j hj i0 hi0)]
796    simp only [hn]; abel
797  refine ⟨n, hw0, ?_⟩
798  have hcont : ∀ (i : Fin N) (k : Fin d), Continuous fun Y : Fin N → Euc d => Y i k := fun i k =>
799    (EuclideanSpace.proj k : Euc d →L[ℝ] ℝ).continuous.comp (continuous_apply i)
800  have h1 : ∀ᶠ Y in 𝓝 Y0, clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I :=
801    (contDiff_clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I).continuous.continuousAt.eventually_lt
802      continuousAt_const hq
803  have h2 : ∀ᶠ Y in 𝓝 Y0, ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ∀ k : Fin d,
804      -(1 / 2) < ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) k ∧ ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) k ≤ 1 / 2 := by
805    refine (Filter.eventually_all_finset I).2 fun i hi => (Filter.eventually_all_finset I).2
806      fun j hj => Filter.eventually_all.2 fun k => ?_
807    have hc : Continuous fun Y : Fin N → Euc d => ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) k := by
808      have := ((hcont i k).sub (continuous_const (y := n i k))).sub ((hcont j k).sub (continuous_const (y := n j k)))
809      exact this
810    have hv : ((Y0 i - n i) - (Y0 j - n j)) k = geoRep (Y0 i - Y0 j) k := by rw [hw0 i hi j hj]
811    have hb := abs_lt.1 ((coord_abs_le_norm (geoRep (Y0 i - Y0 j)) k).trans_lt (hpairs i hi j hj))
812    have e1 : ∀ᶠ Y in 𝓝 Y0, -(1 / 2) < ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) k :=
813      continuousAt_const.eventually_lt hc.continuousAt (by rw [hv]; linarith [hb.1])
814    have e2 : ∀ᶠ Y in 𝓝 Y0, ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) k < 1 / 2 :=
815      hc.continuousAt.eventually_lt continuousAt_const (by rw [hv]; linarith [hb.2])
816    filter_upwards [e1, e2] with Y a b using ⟨a, b.le⟩
817  filter_upwards [h1, h2] with Y hY hw
818  obtain ⟨-, h3, -⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid I Y hY
819  rw [h3]
820  unfold clusterQuad
821  congr 1
822  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
823  rw [eq_geoRep_of (Y i - Y j) ((Y i - n i) - (Y j - n j)) (hw i hi j hj) (fun k => ?_)]
824  obtain ⟨m1, h1⟩ := hnint i k
825  obtain ⟨m2, h2⟩ := hnint j k
826  refine ⟨m1 - m2, ?_⟩
827  simp only [PiLp.sub_apply]
828  push_cast
829  linarith
830
831/-- Regrouping of the pair sum into the single sum `∑_i ⟨2 cc_i, v_i⟩`. -/
832theorem cluster_pair_regroup (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
833    (hpairs : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8) (hk : 0 < I.card)
834    (v : Fin N → Euc d) :
835    (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I,
836        2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v i - v j) =
837      ∑ i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (v i) := by
838  have hk' : (I.card : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hk.ne'
839  have hanti : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y j - Y i) = - geoRep (Y i - Y j) :=
840    fun i hi j hj => geoRep_antisymm Y i j (hpairs i hi j hj)
841  have e1 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v i - v j) =
842      ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v i) -
843        ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v j) := by
844    rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
845    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
846    rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
847    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
848    rw [inner_sub_right]; ring
849  have e2 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v j) =
850      - ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v i) := by
851    rw [Finset.sum_comm, ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
852    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => ?_
853    rw [← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
854    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
855    rw [hanti i hi j hj, inner_neg_left]; ring
856  have e3 : ∀ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (v i) =
857      inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (v i) * (I.card : ℝ) := by
858    intro i hi
859    unfold clusterCentered
860    rw [if_pos hi, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← sum_inner, smul_smul, real_inner_smul_left]
861    field_simp
862  have e4 : ∑ i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (v i) =
863      ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (v i) :=
864    (Finset.sum_subset (Finset.subset_univ I) (fun i _ hi => by
865      simp [clusterCentered, hi])).symm
866  rw [e1, e2, e4, Finset.sum_congr rfl e3, ← Finset.sum_mul]
867  field_simp
868  ring
869
870/-- `∇_{x_i} Q_I = 2(x_i - x̄_I)` for `i ∈ I`, `0` otherwise (M L11807), below the radius. -/
871theorem fderiv_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
872    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) (v : Fin N → Euc d) :
873    fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y v =
874      ∑ i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (v i) := by
875  obtain ⟨hpairs, -, -⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid I Y hq
876  obtain ⟨n, hn, hev⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_local I Y hq
877  rw [hev.fderiv_eq, fderiv_clusterQuad,
878    ← cluster_pair_regroup I Y hpairs (cluster_card_pos I Y hq) v]
879  congr 1
880  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
881  rw [hn i hi j hj]
882
883/-- `∑_{i∈I} |∇_{x_i} Q_I|² = 4 Q_I` (M L11809), below the radius. -/
884theorem sum_sq_grad_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
885    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) :
886    ∑ i, ‖(2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 = 4 * clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
887  obtain ⟨-, -, h3⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid I Y hq
888  have e4 : ∑ i, ‖(2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 =
889      ∑ i ∈ I, ‖(2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 :=
890    (Finset.sum_subset (Finset.subset_univ I) (fun i _ hi => by
891      simp [clusterCentered, hi])).symm
892  rw [e4, h3, Finset.mul_sum]
893  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
894  rw [norm_smul]; simp; ring
895
896/-- Second derivative of `Q_I` below the radius. -/
897theorem fderiv_fderiv_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
898    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) (v w : Fin N → Euc d) :
899    fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y w v =
900      (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, 2 * inner ℝ (w i - w j) (v i - v j) := by
901  obtain ⟨n, hn, hev⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_local I Y hq
902  have hQ := contDiff_clusterDispersionLift (d := d) (N := N) I
903  have hdiff : DifferentiableAt ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I)) Y := by
904    have := hQ.fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_cast)
905    exact (this.differentiable (by norm_num)).differentiableAt
906  set L : (Fin N → Euc d) → ℝ := fun Y' => (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I,
907      2 * inner ℝ (v i - v j) ((Y' i - n i) - (Y' j - n j)) with hL
908  have h1 : (fun Y' => fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y' v) =ᶠ[𝓝 Y] L := by
909    filter_upwards [Filter.EventuallyEq.fderiv (𝕜 := ℝ) hev] with Y' hY'
910    rw [hY', fderiv_clusterQuad]
911    simp only [hL, real_inner_comm]
912  have hproj : ∀ i j, HasFDerivAt (fun Y' : Fin N → Euc d => (Y' i - n i) - (Y' j - n j))
913        ((ContinuousLinearMap.proj i : (Fin N → Euc d) →L[ℝ] Euc d) - ContinuousLinearMap.proj j) Y :=
914    fun i j => ((hasFDerivAt_apply i Y).sub_const (n i)).sub ((hasFDerivAt_apply j Y).sub_const (n j))
915  have hterm := fun i j => ((innerSL ℝ (v i - v j)).hasFDerivAt.comp Y (hproj i j)).const_mul 2
916  have hLd := HasFDerivAt.const_mul (HasFDerivAt.fun_sum (u := I) (fun i _ =>
917    HasFDerivAt.fun_sum (u := I) (fun j _ => hterm i j))) (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ)))
918  have e1 : fderiv ℝ (fun Y' => fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y' v) Y = fderiv ℝ L Y :=
919    h1.fderiv_eq
920  have e2 : fderiv ℝ (fun Y' => fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y' v) Y =
921      (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y).flip v := by
922    have := fderiv_clm_apply (c := fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I)) (u := fun _ => v)
923      (x := Y) hdiff (differentiableAt_const v)
924    simpa using this
925  have e3 : fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y w v =
926      fderiv ℝ L Y w := by
927    have := congrArg (fun f => f w) (e2.symm.trans e1)
928    simpa using this
929  have key := HasFDerivAt.fderiv (f := L) hLd
930  rw [e3, key]
931  simp only [smul_apply, sum_apply, ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, innerSL_apply_apply,
932    sub_apply, ContinuousLinearMap.proj_apply, smul_eq_mul, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat]
933  congr 1
934  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
935  rw [real_inner_comm]
936
937/-- `∑_{i∈I} Δ_{x_i} Q_I = 2d(k-1)` (M L11808), below the radius. -/
938theorem laplacian_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
939    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) :
940    ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ a : Fin d,
941      fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y (Pi.single i (EuclideanSpace.single a 1))
942        (Pi.single i (EuclideanSpace.single a 1)) =
943      2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) := by
944  have hk := cluster_card_pos I Y hq
945  have hk' : (I.card : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hk.ne'
946  have key : ∀ (i : Fin N) (a : Fin d),
947      fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y (Pi.single i (EuclideanSpace.single a 1))
948        (Pi.single i (EuclideanSpace.single a 1)) = (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) * (if i ∈ I then 2 * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) else 0) := by
949    intro i a
950    rw [fderiv_fderiv_clusterDispersionLift I Y hq]
951    set e : Fin N → Euc d := Pi.single i (EuclideanSpace.single a 1) with he
952    have h2 : ∀ i j, 2 * inner ℝ (e i - e j) (e i - e j) = 2 * ‖e i - e j‖ ^ 2 := fun i j => by
953      rw [real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq]
954    simp_rw [h2, ← Finset.mul_sum]
955    rw [sum_sum_norm_sub_sq]
956    have hT : ∑ p ∈ I, ‖e p‖ ^ 2 = if i ∈ I then 1 else 0 := by
957      have : ∀ p, ‖e p‖ ^ 2 = (Pi.single i (1 : ℝ) : Fin N → ℝ) p := fun p => by
958        by_cases hp : p = i
959        · subst hp; simp [he]
960        · simp [he, Pi.single_apply, hp]
961      simp_rw [this]
962      exact Finset.sum_pi_single' i 1 I
963    have hS : ∑ p ∈ I, e p = if i ∈ I then EuclideanSpace.single a (1 : ℝ) else 0 := by
964      exact Finset.sum_pi_single' i _ I
965    rw [hT, hS]
966    by_cases hi : i ∈ I <;> simp [hi] <;> field_simp
967  simp_rw [key]
968  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
969  rw [← Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.mul_sum]
970  rw [← Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
971  have : Finset.filter (fun i => i ∈ I) Finset.univ = I := by ext; simp
972  rw [this]
973  field_simp
974
975/-! ### The generator of `Q_I`: the internal term `𝒜_I` and the remainder `ℛ_I`
976(Lemma `B:lem:cluster-ito`, M L11781-11845) -/
977
978/-- The generator of the lifted particle SDE applied to `Q_I`:
979`D Q_I(Y)[b(Y)] + ½ ∑_k D² Q_I(Y)[σ_k, σ_k]` (the Itô drift of `Q_I(Y_t)`). -/
980def clusterGenerator (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : ℝ :=
981  fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y (liftedDrift D Y) +
982    (1 / 2) * ∑ k : Fin N × Fin d,
983      fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y (liftedNoise D k) (liftedNoise D k)
984
985/-- The singular internal term `𝒜_I = (2/N) ∑_{i<j∈I} (x_i - x_j) · M ∇g_E(x_i - x_j)`
986(`B:eq:cluster-internal-formula`, M L11813, written as `N⁻¹ ∑_{i,j∈I}`), with the Euclidean
987singular force at the geodesic representative of `y_i - y_j`. -/
988def clusterInternal (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : ℝ :=
989  (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I,
990    inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j))))
991
992/-- The remainder `ℛ_I` of `B:eq:cluster-ito` (M L11785): the generator minus the constant
993diffusion drift `2d(k-1)/β` and the internal term (exterior forces, the smooth part of the
994kernel, and the confinement, M L11822-11844). -/
995def clusterRemainder (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : ℝ :=
996  clusterGenerator D I Y - 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ - clusterInternal D I Y
997
998/-- The constant diffusion drift: `½ ∑_k D² Q_I[σ_k, σ_k] = 2d(k-1)/β` below the radius
999(M L11808, L11811). -/
1000theorem cluster_diffusion_term (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
1001    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) :
1002    (1 / 2) * ∑ k : Fin N × Fin d,
1003        fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y (liftedNoise D k) (liftedNoise D k) =
1004      2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ := by
1005  have hc : Real.sqrt (2 * D.invβ) * Real.sqrt (2 * D.invβ) = 2 * D.invβ :=
1006    Real.mul_self_sqrt (by linarith [D.hβ])
1007  have key : ∀ k : Fin N × Fin d,
1008      fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y (liftedNoise D k) (liftedNoise D k) =
1009        2 * D.invβ * fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I)) Y
1010          (Pi.single k.1 (EuclideanSpace.single k.2 1)) (Pi.single k.1 (EuclideanSpace.single k.2 1)) := by
1011    intro k
1012    simp only [liftedNoise, map_smul, ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
1013    rw [← mul_assoc, hc]
1014  have hl := laplacian_clusterDispersionLift I Y hq
1015  simp_rw [key]
1016  simp only [Fintype.sum_prod_type, ← Finset.mul_sum]
1017  rw [hl]
1018  ring
1019
1020/-- The quadratic variation density of the martingale term:
1021`∑_k (D Q_I[σ_k])² = (8/β) Q_I` (`B:eq:cluster-ito`, M L11787, from L11809). -/
1022theorem cluster_qv (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
1023    (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I) :
1024    ∑ k : Fin N × Fin d, (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y (liftedNoise D k)) ^ 2 =
1025      8 * D.invβ * clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
1026  have hc : Real.sqrt (2 * D.invβ) ^ 2 = 2 * D.invβ := Real.sq_sqrt (by linarith [D.hβ])
1027  have key : ∀ k : Fin N × Fin d, fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y (liftedNoise D k) =
1028      Real.sqrt (2 * D.invβ) * (2 * clusterCentered I Y k.1 k.2) := by
1029    intro k
1030    rw [fderiv_clusterDispersionLift I Y hq, Finset.sum_eq_single k.1]
1031    · have hin : ∀ (x : Euc d) (a : Fin d), inner ℝ x (EuclideanSpace.single a (1 : ℝ)) = x a := by
1032        intro x a; simp [EuclideanSpace.inner_single_right]
1033      simp [liftedNoise, inner_smul_right, hin]
1034    · intro i _ hi
1035      simp [liftedNoise, Pi.single_apply, hi]
1036    · simp
1037  have hn : ∀ i, ∑ a : Fin d, (2 * clusterCentered I Y i a) ^ 2 =
1038      ‖(2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 := by
1039    intro i
1040    rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_sq_eq]
1041    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
1042    simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, PiLp.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
1043    rw [sq_abs]
1044  have key2 : ∀ k : Fin N × Fin d,
1045      (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y (liftedNoise D k)) ^ 2 =
1046        2 * D.invβ * (2 * clusterCentered I Y k.1 k.2) ^ 2 := fun k => by rw [key k, mul_pow, hc]
1047  simp_rw [key2]
1048  rw [Fintype.sum_prod_type]
1049  simp_rw [← Finset.mul_sum, hn]
1050  rw [sum_sq_grad_clusterDispersionLift I Y hq]
1051  ring
1052
1053open Matrix in
1054theorem inner_matVec_eq (M : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℝ) (z : Euc d) :
1055    inner ℝ z (Torus.matVec M z) = (z.ofLp) ⬝ᵥ (M *ᵥ z.ofLp) := by
1056  unfold Torus.matVec
1057  have : Matrix.toEuclideanLin M z = WithLp.toLp 2 (M *ᵥ z.ofLp) := by
1058    rfl
1059  rw [this]
1060  simp [EuclideanSpace.inner_eq_star_dotProduct, dotProduct_comm]
1061
1062open Matrix in
1063theorem inner_matVec_nonpos {M : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℝ} (hM : Torus.SymNonpos M) (z : Euc d) :
1064    inner ℝ z (Torus.matVec M z) ≤ 0 := by
1065  rw [inner_matVec_eq]
1066  have h := hM.dotProduct_mulVec_nonneg z.ofLp
1067  have e : (z.ofLp) ⬝ᵥ ((Torus.symPart M) *ᵥ z.ofLp) = (z.ofLp) ⬝ᵥ (M *ᵥ z.ofLp) := by
1068    have h1 : (z.ofLp) ⬝ᵥ (Mᵀ *ᵥ z.ofLp) = (z.ofLp) ⬝ᵥ (M *ᵥ z.ofLp) := by
1069      rw [Matrix.dotProduct_mulVec, Matrix.vecMul_transpose, dotProduct_comm]
1070    unfold Torus.symPart
1071    rw [smul_mulVec, add_mulVec, dotProduct_smul, dotProduct_add, h1]
1072    simp; ring
1073  simp only [star_trivial, neg_mulVec, dotProduct_neg, e] at h
1074  linarith
1075
1076/-- `𝒜_I ≥ 0` (`B:eq:cluster-internal-positive`, M L11791): `z · M ∇g_E(z) = -a_s(z) zᵀ Sym(M) z`
1077and `Sym M ≤ 0` (M L11815-11820); the antisymmetric part of `M` drops out. -/
1078theorem clusterInternal_nonneg (D : DynData d) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (I : Finset (Fin N))
1079    (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : 0 ≤ clusterInternal D I Y := by
1080  unfold clusterInternal
1081  refine mul_nonneg (by positivity) (Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => ?_)
1082  generalize geoRep (Y i - Y j) = z
1083  by_cases hz : z = 0
1084  · simp [hz]
1085  have ha := rieszRadial_pos D.hs0 hz
1086  unfold eucRieszGrad
1087  have e : Torus.matVec D.M (-(rieszRadial D.s z) • z) = -(rieszRadial D.s z) • Torus.matVec D.M z := by
1088    unfold Torus.matVec; exact map_smul _ _ _
1089  rw [e, real_inner_smul_right]
1090  have := inner_matVec_nonpos hM z
1091  nlinarith

1093/-- Coordinatewise, `|centeredRep z|_i` is the norm of `z_i` in the circle `ℝ/ℤ`. -/
1094theorem Torus.abs_centeredRep_apply (z : Torus d) (i : Fin d) :
1095    |Torus.centeredRep z i| = ‖z i‖ := by
1096  have hz : z i = (((Torus.rep z) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := by
1097    have := congrFun (Torus.mk_rep z) i
1098    simpa [Torus.mk] using this.symm
1099  have hr : (Torus.rep z) i ∈ Set.Ico (0 : ℝ) 1 := by
1100    have := (AddCircle.equivIco (1 : ℝ) 0 (z i)).2
1101    simpa [Torus.rep] using this
1102  have hc : Torus.centeredRep z i =
1103      if (Torus.rep z) i ≤ 1 / 2 then (Torus.rep z) i else (Torus.rep z) i - 1 := rfl
1104  rw [hc, hz]
1105  by_cases h : (Torus.rep z) i ≤ 1 / 2
1106  · simp only [h, ↓reduceIte]
1107    have : ‖(((Torus.rep z) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle)‖ = |(Torus.rep z) i| := by
1108      rw [AddCircle.norm_coe_eq_abs_iff (p := (1 : ℝ)) (by norm_num)]
1109      rw [abs_of_nonneg hr.1]; norm_num; linarith
1110    exact this.symm
1111  · simp only [h, ↓reduceIte]
1112    have h2 : (((Torus.rep z) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) =
1113        ((((Torus.rep z) i - 1 : ℝ)) : UnitAddCircle) := by
1114      have : ((1 : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = 0 := AddCircle.coe_period 1
1115      rw [AddCircle.coe_sub, this, sub_zero]
1116    rw [h2]
1117    have : ‖((((Torus.rep z) i - 1 : ℝ)) : UnitAddCircle)‖ = |(Torus.rep z) i - 1| := by
1118      rw [AddCircle.norm_coe_eq_abs_iff (p := (1 : ℝ)) (by norm_num)]
1119      rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> norm_num <;> linarith [hr.2]
1120    exact this.symm

1122theorem Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt (x y : Torus d) :
1123    Torus.tdist x y = Real.sqrt (∑ i, ‖x i - y i‖ ^ 2) := by
1124  unfold Torus.tdist
1125  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
1126  congr 1
1127  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
1128  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, Torus.abs_centeredRep_apply]
1129  rfl

1131theorem Torus.continuous_tdist : Continuous fun p : Torus d × Torus d => Torus.tdist p.1 p.2 := by
1132  simp_rw [Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt]
1133  fun_prop

1135theorem Torus.tdist_eq_zero_iff (x y : Torus d) : Torus.tdist x y = 0 ↔ x = y := by
1136  rw [Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt]
1137  constructor
1138  · intro h
1139    have h1 : ∑ i, ‖x i - y i‖ ^ 2 ≤ 0 := Real.sqrt_eq_zero'.1 h
1140    have h2 : ∀ i ∈ Finset.univ, ‖x i - y i‖ ^ 2 = 0 :=
1141      (Finset.sum_eq_zero_iff_of_nonneg fun i _ => sq_nonneg _).1
1142        (le_antisymm h1 (Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => sq_nonneg _))
1143    funext i
1144    have := h2 i (Finset.mem_univ i)
1145    exact sub_eq_zero.1 (norm_eq_zero.1 (pow_eq_zero_iff (two_ne_zero) |>.1 this))
1146  · rintro rfl
1147    simp

1149theorem Torus.tdist_pos_of_ne {x y : Torus d} (h : x ≠ y) : 0 < Torus.tdist x y :=
1150  lt_of_le_of_ne (norm_nonneg _) (fun h0 => h ((Torus.tdist_eq_zero_iff x y).1 h0.symm))

1152theorem Torus.continuous_tdist_comp {α : Type*} [TopologicalSpace α] {f g : α → Torus d}
1153    (hf : Continuous f) (hg : Continuous g) : Continuous fun a => Torus.tdist (f a) (g a) := by
1154  have := (Torus.continuous_tdist (d := d)).comp (hf.prodMk hg)
1155  exact this

1157theorem isOpen_setOf_mk_ne_zero : IsOpen {x : Euc d | Torus.mk x ≠ 0} :=
1158  (isClosed_singleton (x := (0 : Torus d))).isOpen_compl.preimage Torus.continuous_mk

1160/-- The lifted drift in covering-space coordinates:
1161`b_i(Y) = N⁻¹ ∑_{j≠i} M (lift ∇g)(Y_i - Y_j) - ∇(lift V)(Y_i)`. -/
1162theorem liftedDrift_apply (D : DynData d) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) (i : Fin N) :
1163    liftedDrift D Y i = (1 / (N : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
1164      Torus.matVec D.M (Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (Y i - Y j)) -
1165        gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (Y i) := by
1166  unfold liftedDrift particleDrift
1167  rw [Torus.grad_mk_aux D.V (Y i)]
1168  congr 3
1169
1170theorem mk_sub_ne_zero_of_mem_liftedFree {Y : Fin N → Euc d} (hY : Y ∈ liftedFree d N)
1171    {i j : Fin N} (hij : i ≠ j) : Torus.mk (Y i - Y j) ≠ 0 := by
1172  intro h
1173  apply hY
1174  refine ⟨i, j, hij, ?_⟩
1175  rw [mk_sub'] at h
1176  exact sub_eq_zero.1 h

1179theorem Torus.rem_tdist_comm (x y : Torus d) : Torus.tdist x y = Torus.tdist y x := by
1180  rw [Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt, Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt]
1181  congr 1
1182  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => by rw [norm_sub_rev]
1183
1184theorem Torus.rem_tdist_triangle (x y z : Torus d) :
1185    Torus.tdist x z ≤ Torus.tdist x y + Torus.tdist y z := by
1186  rw [Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt, Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt, Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt]
1187  let a : Euc d := WithLp.toLp 2 fun i => ‖x i - y i‖
1188  let b : Euc d := WithLp.toLp 2 fun i => ‖y i - z i‖
1189  have ha : ‖a‖ = Real.sqrt (∑ i, ‖x i - y i‖ ^ 2) := by
1190    rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]; simp [a]
1191  have hb : ‖b‖ = Real.sqrt (∑ i, ‖y i - z i‖ ^ 2) := by
1192    rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]; simp [b]
1193  rw [← ha, ← hb]
1194  refine le_trans ?_ (norm_add_le a b)
1195  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
1196  refine Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_)
1197  have h1 : ‖x i - z i‖ ≤ ‖x i - y i‖ + ‖y i - z i‖ := by
1198    calc ‖x i - z i‖ = ‖(x i - y i) + (y i - z i)‖ := by congr 1; abel
1199      _ ≤ _ := norm_add_le _ _
1200  have h2 : ‖(a + b) i‖ = ‖x i - y i‖ + ‖y i - z i‖ := by
1201    simp [a, b]
1202    positivity
1203  rw [h2]
1204  exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) h1 2
1205
1206theorem Torus.rem_tdist_mk_le (a b : Euc d) : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk a) (Torus.mk b) ≤ ‖a - b‖ := by
1207  rw [Torus.tdist_eq_sqrt, EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
1208  refine Real.sqrt_le_sqrt (Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_)
1209  have h1 : ‖(Torus.mk a i - Torus.mk b i : UnitAddCircle)‖ ≤ |a i - b i| := by
1210    have : (Torus.mk a i - Torus.mk b i : UnitAddCircle) = (((a i - b i : ℝ)) : UnitAddCircle) := by
1211      simp [Torus.mk]
1212    rw [this]
1213    have := QuotientAddGroup.norm_mk_le_norm (S := AddSubgroup.zmultiples (1 : ℝ)) (m := a i - b i)
1214    simpa using this
1215  have h2 : ‖(a - b) i‖ = |a i - b i| := by simp
1216  rw [h2]
1217  exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) h1 2

1219theorem rem_mk_centeredRep_eq (w : Torus d) : Torus.mk (Torus.centeredRep w) = w := by
1220  funext i
1221  have hz : w i = (((Torus.rep w) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := by
1222    have := congrFun (Torus.mk_rep w) i
1223    simpa [Torus.mk] using this.symm
1224  have hc : Torus.centeredRep w i =
1225      if (Torus.rep w) i ≤ 1 / 2 then (Torus.rep w) i else (Torus.rep w) i - 1 := rfl
1226  show (((Torus.centeredRep w) i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = w i
1227  rw [hc, hz]
1228  split_ifs
1229  · rfl
1230  · rw [AddCircle.coe_sub, (AddCircle.coe_period 1 : ((1 : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = 0), sub_zero]
1231
1232theorem rem_centeredRep_eq_of_mk_eq {v : Euc d} {w : Torus d} (hv : Torus.mk v = w)
1233    (hlt : ∀ a, |v a| < 1 / 2) : Torus.centeredRep w = v := by
1234  ext a
1235  have hr : (Torus.rep w) a ∈ Set.Ico (0 : ℝ) 1 := by
1236    have := (AddCircle.equivIco (1 : ℝ) 0 (w a)).2
1237    simpa [Torus.rep] using this
1238  have hz : w a = (((Torus.rep w) a : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := by
1239    have := congrFun (Torus.mk_rep w) a
1240    simpa [Torus.mk] using this.symm
1241  have hva : (((v a : ℝ)) : UnitAddCircle) = w a := congrFun hv a
1242  have h1 : ((v a : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = (((Torus.rep w) a : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := hva.trans hz
1243  have h2 := (QuotientAddGroup.eq).mp h1
1244  rw [AddSubgroup.mem_zmultiples_iff] at h2
1245  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := h2
1246  simp only [zsmul_eq_mul, mul_one] at hn
1247  have hc : Torus.centeredRep w a =
1248      if (Torus.rep w) a ≤ 1 / 2 then (Torus.rep w) a else (Torus.rep w) a - 1 := rfl
1249  have hva2 := abs_lt.1 (hlt a)
1250  have hn1 : (-1 : ℝ) < n := by linarith [hr.1]
1251  have hn2 : (n : ℝ) < 2 := by linarith [hr.2]
1252  have hn3 : (-1 : ℤ) < n := by exact_mod_cast hn1
1253  have hn4 : n < 2 := by exact_mod_cast hn2
1254  have : n = 0 ∨ n = 1 := by omega
1255  show Torus.centeredRep w a = v a
1256  rw [hc]
1257  rcases this with h | h
1258  · subst h
1259    have : (Torus.rep w) a = v a := by simp at hn; linarith
1260    have hle : (Torus.rep w) a ≤ 1 / 2 := by rw [this]; linarith
1261    simp only [hle, ↓reduceIte]
1262    exact this
1263  · subst h
1264    have : (Torus.rep w) a = v a + 1 := by simp at hn; linarith
1265    have hle : ¬ (Torus.rep w) a ≤ 1 / 2 := by rw [this]; intro hh; linarith
1266    simp only [hle, ↓reduceIte]
1267    rw [this]; ring
1268
1269theorem rem_mk_geoRep_eq (z : Euc d) : Torus.mk (geoRep z) = Torus.mk z := rem_mk_centeredRep_eq _
1270
1271theorem rem_geoRep_eq_of_mk_eq {z v : Euc d} (hv : Torus.mk v = Torus.mk z) (hlt : ‖v‖ < 1 / 2) :
1272    geoRep z = v :=
1273  rem_centeredRep_eq_of_mk_eq hv fun a => lt_of_le_of_lt (by simpa using PiLp.norm_apply_le v a) hlt

1275theorem rem_geoRep_neg_of_small {z : Euc d} (h : ‖geoRep z‖ < 1 / 2) : geoRep (-z) = -geoRep z :=
1276  rem_geoRep_eq_of_mk_eq (by rw [mk_neg_euc, rem_mk_geoRep_eq, mk_neg_euc]) (by rwa [norm_neg])

1278theorem rem_geoRep_add_of_small {z w : Euc d} (hz : ‖geoRep z‖ < 1 / 4) (hw : ‖geoRep w‖ < 1 / 4) :
1279    geoRep (z + w) = geoRep z + geoRep w :=
1280  rem_geoRep_eq_of_mk_eq (by rw [Torus.mk_add, Torus.mk_add, rem_mk_geoRep_eq, rem_mk_geoRep_eq])
1281    ((norm_add_le _ _).trans_lt (by linarith))

1283theorem rem_exists_bound_periodic {E' : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E'] (F : Euc d → E')
1284    (S : Set (Euc d)) (hS : IsClosed S) (hSrep : ∀ w ∈ S, Torus.rep (Torus.mk w) ∈ S)
1285    (hF : ContinuousOn F S)
1286    (hper : ∀ (w : Euc d) (n : Lattice d), F (w + Lattice.toEuc n) = F w) :
1287    ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ w ∈ S, ‖F w‖ ≤ B := by
1288  let Box : Set (Euc d) :=
1289    (WithLp.toLp 2) '' (Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1)
1290  have hBox : IsCompact Box :=
1291    (isCompact_univ_pi fun _ => isCompact_Icc).image (PiLp.continuous_toLp 2 _)
1292  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := (hBox.inter_right hS).exists_bound_of_continuousOn
1293    (hF.mono Set.inter_subset_right)
1294  refine ⟨B, fun w hw => ?_⟩
1295  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := Torus.rep_mk w
1296  have hmem : Torus.rep (Torus.mk w) ∈ Box := by
1297    refine ⟨fun a => Torus.rep (Torus.mk w) a, fun a _ => ?_, rfl⟩
1298    have := (AddCircle.equivIco (1 : ℝ) 0 (Torus.mk w a)).2
1299    simp only [zero_add] at this
1300    exact ⟨this.1, this.2.le⟩
1301  have h1 := hB (Torus.rep (Torus.mk w)) ⟨hmem, hSrep w hw⟩
1302  rw [hn, hper] at h1
1303  exact h1
1304
1305theorem rem_exists_norm_le_of_smooth_zero (r : Torus d → Euc d) (hr : Torus.IsSmooth r)
1306    (h0 : r 0 = 0) :
1307    ∃ L : ℝ, 0 ≤ L ∧ ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ ≤ 1 / 8 → ‖r (Torus.mk x)‖ ≤ L * ‖x‖ := by
1308  have hc : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift r) := hr
1309  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := hc.contDiffOn.exists_lipschitzOnWith (s := Metric.closedBall (0 : Euc d) (1 / 8))
1310    (by simp) (convex_closedBall _ _) (isCompact_closedBall _ _)
1311  refine ⟨L, L.2, fun x hx => ?_⟩
1312  have h := hL.dist_le_mul x (by simpa using hx) 0 (by simp)
1313  have e : Torus.lift r 0 = 0 := by
1314    have : Torus.mk (0 : Euc d) = 0 := by funext i; simp [Torus.mk]
1315    simp [Torus.lift, this, h0]
1316  rw [e, dist_zero_right, dist_zero_right] at h
1317  exact h

1319theorem rem_exists_fderiv_bound_off_zero {κ : Torus d → Euc d} (hK : Torus.IsSmoothOffZero κ)
1320    {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1321    ∃ L : ℝ, 0 ≤ L ∧ ∀ w : Euc d, ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk w) 0 →
1322      ‖fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift κ) w‖ ≤ L := by
1323  let S : Set (Euc d) := {w | ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk w) 0}
1324  have hS : IsClosed S := by
1325    have : Continuous fun w : Euc d => Torus.tdist (Torus.mk w) 0 :=
1326      Torus.continuous_tdist_comp Torus.continuous_mk continuous_const
1327    exact isClosed_le continuous_const this
1328  have hSne : S ⊆ {x : Euc d | Torus.mk x ≠ 0} := by
1329    intro w hw h0
1330    have : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk w) 0 = 0 := (Torus.tdist_eq_zero_iff _ _).2 h0
1331    have hw' : ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk w) 0 := hw
1332    linarith
1333  have hcont : ContinuousOn (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift κ)) S :=
1334    (hK.continuousOn_fderiv_of_isOpen isOpen_setOf_mk_ne_zero (by simp)).mono hSne
1335  have hper : ∀ (w : Euc d) (n : Lattice d),
1336      fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift κ) (w + Lattice.toEuc n) = fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift κ) w := by
1337    intro w n
1338    have hp : (fun x => Torus.lift κ (x + Lattice.toEuc n)) = Torus.lift κ :=
1339      funext fun x => Torus.lift_periodic κ x n
1340    rw [← fderiv_comp_add_right (Lattice.toEuc n) (f := Torus.lift κ) (x := w), hp]
1341  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := rem_exists_bound_periodic _ S hS (fun w hw => by
1342    show ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Torus.rep (Torus.mk w))) 0
1343    rw [Torus.mk_rep]; exact hw) hcont hper
1344  exact ⟨max B 0, le_max_right _ _, fun w hw => (hB w hw).trans (le_max_left _ _)⟩
1345
1346theorem rem_exists_lipschitz_of_periodic {E' : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E'] [NormedSpace ℝ E']
1347    (G : Euc d → E') (hG : ContDiff ℝ 1 G)
1348    (hper : ∀ (w : Euc d) (n : Lattice d), G (w + Lattice.toEuc n) = G w) :
1349    ∃ L : ℝ≥0, LipschitzWith L G := by
1350  have hFper : ∀ (w : Euc d) (n : Lattice d),
1351      fderiv ℝ G (w + Lattice.toEuc n) = fderiv ℝ G w := by
1352    intro w n
1353    have hp : (fun x => G (x + Lattice.toEuc n)) = G := funext fun x => hper x n
1354    rw [← fderiv_comp_add_right (Lattice.toEuc n) (f := G) (x := w), hp]
1355  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := rem_exists_bound_periodic (fderiv ℝ G) Set.univ isClosed_univ
1356    (fun _ _ => Set.mem_univ _) (hG.continuous_fderiv one_ne_zero).continuousOn hFper
1357  refine ⟨⟨max B 0, le_max_right _ _⟩, ?_⟩
1358  refine lipschitzWith_of_nnnorm_fderiv_le (hG.differentiable one_ne_zero) fun x => ?_
1359  have := (hB x (Set.mem_univ _)).trans (le_max_left B 0)
1360  exact_mod_cast this
1361
1362theorem rem_kernel_lipschitz_off_zero {κ : Torus d → Euc d} (hK : Torus.IsSmoothOffZero κ) {ε : ℝ}
1363    (hε : 0 < ε) :
1364    ∃ L : ℝ, 0 ≤ L ∧ ∀ (u v y : Euc d), ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk u) (Torus.mk y) →
1365      ‖v - u‖ ≤ ε / 2 →
1366      ‖Torus.lift κ (v - y) - Torus.lift κ (u - y)‖ ≤ L * ‖v - u‖ := by
1367  obtain ⟨L, hL0, hL⟩ := rem_exists_fderiv_bound_off_zero hK (half_pos hε)
1368  refine ⟨L, hL0, fun u v y hu hv => ?_⟩
1369  let f : Euc d → Euc d := fun z => Torus.lift κ (z + (-y))
1370  have hf : ∀ z ∈ Metric.closedBall u (ε / 2),
1371      ε / 2 ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (z + (-y))) 0 := by
1372    intro z hz
1373    rw [Metric.mem_closedBall, dist_eq_norm] at hz
1374    have h1 := Torus.rem_tdist_triangle (Torus.mk u) (Torus.mk z) (Torus.mk y)
1375    have h2 := Torus.rem_tdist_mk_le u z
1376    have h3 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (z + (-y))) 0 = Torus.tdist (Torus.mk z) (Torus.mk y) := by
1377      rw [← sub_eq_add_neg, mk_sub']
1378      unfold Torus.tdist
1379      rw [sub_zero]
1380    rw [h3]
1381    rw [norm_sub_rev] at hz
1382    linarith
1383  have hne : ∀ z ∈ Metric.closedBall u (ε / 2), Torus.mk (z + (-y)) ≠ 0 := by
1384    intro z hz h0
1385    have := hf z hz
1386    rw [(Torus.tdist_eq_zero_iff _ _).2 h0] at this
1387    linarith
1388  have hdA : ∀ z ∈ Metric.closedBall u (ε / 2), DifferentiableAt ℝ (Torus.lift κ) (z + (-y)) := by
1389    intro z hz
1390    exact ((hK.contDiffAt (isOpen_setOf_mk_ne_zero.mem_nhds (hne z hz))).differentiableAt
1391      (by simp))
1392  have hdiff : ∀ z ∈ Metric.closedBall u (ε / 2), DifferentiableAt ℝ f z := fun z hz =>
1393    DifferentiableAt.comp (g := Torus.lift κ) (f := fun z : Euc d => z + (-y)) z (hdA z hz)
1394      (differentiableAt_id.add_const (-y))
1395  have hbd : ∀ z ∈ Metric.closedBall u (ε / 2), ‖fderiv ℝ f z‖ ≤ L := by
1396    intro z hz
1397    have : fderiv ℝ f z = fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift κ) (z + (-y)) :=
1398      fderiv_comp_add_right (-y) (f := Torus.lift κ) (x := z)
1399    rw [this]
1400    exact hL _ (hf z hz)
1401  have := (convex_closedBall u (ε / 2)).norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_fderiv_le hdiff hbd
1402    (Metric.mem_closedBall_self (by linarith)) (by rw [Metric.mem_closedBall, dist_eq_norm]; exact hv)
1403  simpa [f, sub_eq_add_neg] using this
1404
1405theorem rem_exists_lipschitz_gradient (V : Torus d → ℝ) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 V) :
1406    ∃ L : ℝ≥0, LipschitzWith L (gradient (Torus.lift V)) := by
1407  have hV' : ContDiff ℝ 2 (Torus.lift V) := hV
1408  have h1 : ContDiff ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift V)) := hV'.fderiv_right (by norm_num)
1409  have hg : ContDiff ℝ 1 (gradient (Torus.lift V)) := by
1410    have : gradient (Torus.lift V) =
1411        (InnerProductSpace.toDual ℝ (Euc d)).symm ∘ fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift V) := by
1412      funext x; rfl
1413    rw [this]
1414    exact (InnerProductSpace.toDual ℝ (Euc d)).symm.contDiff.comp h1
1415  refine rem_exists_lipschitz_of_periodic _ hg fun w n => ?_
1416  rw [← Torus.grad_mk_aux, ← Torus.grad_mk_aux, Torus.mk_add, Torus.mk_toEuc, add_zero]

1418theorem rem_abs_sum_inner_le {ι : Type*} (I : Finset ι) (c : ι → Euc d) (hc : ∑ i ∈ I, c i = 0)
1419    (F : ι → Euc d) (Fb : Euc d) (T : Euc d →L[ℝ] Euc d) (L : ℝ)
1420    (hF : ∀ i ∈ I, ‖F i - Fb‖ ≤ L * ‖c i‖) :
1421    |∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T (F i))| ≤ 2 * ‖T‖ * L * ∑ i ∈ I, ‖c i‖ ^ 2 := by
1422  have h1 : ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T (F i)) =
1423      ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T (F i - Fb)) := by
1424    have h2 : ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T Fb) = 0 := by
1425      rw [← sum_inner, ← Finset.smul_sum, hc]
1426      simp
1427    have h3 : ∀ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T (F i)) =
1428        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T (F i - Fb)) + inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (T Fb) := by
1429      intro i _
1430      rw [← inner_add_right, ← map_add, sub_add_cancel]
1431    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl h3, Finset.sum_add_distrib, h2, add_zero]
1432  rw [h1]
1433  refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans ?_
1434  rw [Finset.mul_sum]
1435  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i hi => ?_
1436  refine (abs_real_inner_le_norm _ _).trans ?_
1437  rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_two]
1438  have h4 : ‖T (F i - Fb)‖ ≤ ‖T‖ * (L * ‖c i‖) :=
1439    (T.le_opNorm _).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hF i hi) (norm_nonneg _))
1440  calc 2 * ‖c i‖ * ‖T (F i - Fb)‖ ≤ 2 * ‖c i‖ * (‖T‖ * (L * ‖c i‖)) :=
1441        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h4 (by positivity)
1442    _ = 2 * ‖T‖ * L * ‖c i‖ ^ 2 := by ring
1443
1444theorem rem_cluster_compat {I : Finset (Fin N)} {Y : Fin N → Euc d}
1445    (hsmall : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8) :
1446    (∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y j - Y i) = -geoRep (Y i - Y j)) ∧
1447    (∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ∀ l ∈ I,
1448      geoRep (Y i - Y l) = geoRep (Y i - Y j) + geoRep (Y j - Y l)) := by
1449  refine ⟨fun i hi j hj => ?_, fun i hi j hj l hl => ?_⟩
1450  · have : Y j - Y i = -(Y i - Y j) := by abel
1451    rw [this]
1452    exact rem_geoRep_neg_of_small ((hsmall i hi j hj).trans (by norm_num))
1453  · have : Y i - Y l = (Y i - Y j) + (Y j - Y l) := by abel
1454    rw [this]
1455    exact rem_geoRep_add_of_small ((hsmall i hi j hj).trans (by norm_num))
1456      ((hsmall j hj l hl).trans (by norm_num))

1458theorem rem_clusterCentered_of_mem {I : Finset (Fin N)} {Y : Fin N → Euc d} {i : Fin N} (hi : i ∈ I) :
1459    clusterCentered I Y i = (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) := by
1460  simp [clusterCentered, hi]

1462theorem rem_clusterCentered_sub {I : Finset (Fin N)} {Y : Fin N → Euc d} (hI : I.Nonempty)
1463    (hc : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ∀ l ∈ I,
1464      geoRep (Y i - Y l) = geoRep (Y i - Y j) + geoRep (Y j - Y l))
1465    {i j : Fin N} (hi : i ∈ I) (hj : j ∈ I) :
1466    clusterCentered I Y i - clusterCentered I Y j = geoRep (Y i - Y j) := by
1467  rw [rem_clusterCentered_of_mem hi, rem_clusterCentered_of_mem hj, ← smul_sub, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
1468  have : ∀ l ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y l) - geoRep (Y j - Y l) = geoRep (Y i - Y j) := by
1469    intro l hl
1470    have := hc i hi j hj l hl
1471    rw [this]; abel
1472  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl this, Finset.sum_const, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℝ, smul_smul]
1473  have hk : (I.card : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hI.card_pos.ne'
1474  rw [one_div, inv_mul_cancel₀ hk, one_smul]
