1476theorem rem_clusterCentered_sum {I : Finset (Fin N)} {Y : Fin N → Euc d}
1477    (hneg : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y j - Y i) = -geoRep (Y i - Y j)) :
1478    ∑ i ∈ I, clusterCentered I Y i = 0 := by
1479  have h1 : ∑ i ∈ I, clusterCentered I Y i =
1480      (1 / (I.card : ℝ)) • ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) := by
1481    rw [Finset.smul_sum]
1482    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => rem_clusterCentered_of_mem hi
1483  have h2 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) = 0 := by
1484    have h3 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) = -∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) := by
1485      conv_lhs => rw [Finset.sum_comm]
1486      rw [← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
1487      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
1488      rw [← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
1489      exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => (hneg i hi j hj).symm ▸ by simp
1490    set S := ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y j) with hSdef
1491    have h4 : (2 : ℝ) • S = 0 := by
1492      rw [two_smul]; nth_rewrite 1 [h3]; exact neg_add_cancel _
1493    exact (smul_eq_zero.1 h4).resolve_left two_ne_zero
1494  rw [h1, h2, smul_zero]

1496theorem rem_internal_identity {I : Finset (Fin N)} {Y : Fin N → Euc d} (hI : I.Nonempty)
1497    (hneg : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, geoRep (Y j - Y i) = -geoRep (Y i - Y j))
1498    (hc : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ∀ l ∈ I,
1499      geoRep (Y i - Y l) = geoRep (Y i - Y j) + geoRep (Y j - Y l))
1500    (E : Euc d → Euc d) (hE : ∀ z, E (-z) = -E z) (T : Euc d →L[ℝ] Euc d) :
1501    ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (T (E (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) =
1502      ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) (T (E (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) := by
1503  set c := clusterCentered I Y with hcdef
1504  set x : Fin N → Fin N → Euc d := fun i j => geoRep (Y i - Y j) with hx
1505  have hS : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ (c j) (T (E (x i j))) =
1506      -∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ (c i) (T (E (x i j))) := by
1507    conv_lhs => rw [Finset.sum_comm]
1508    rw [← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
1509    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => ?_
1510    rw [← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
1511    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
1512    have : x j i = -x i j := hneg i hi j hj
1513    rw [this, hE, map_neg, inner_neg_right]
1514  have hsub : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, (inner ℝ (c i) (T (E (x i j))) - inner ℝ (c j) (T (E (x i j)))) =
1515      ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ (x i j) (T (E (x i j))) := by
1516    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
1517    rw [← inner_sub_left, rem_clusterCentered_sub hI hc hi hj]
1518  rw [← hsub]
1519  simp only [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, hS]
1520  simp only [real_inner_smul_left, ← Finset.mul_sum]
1521  ring

1523theorem rem_exterior_separation {I : Finset (Fin N)} (hI : I.Nonempty) {Y : Fin N → Euc d}
1524    {j : Fin N} (hj : j ∉ I) {ε δ : ℝ} (hδ0 : 0 < δ) (hε1 : ε ≤ 1 / 16)
1525    (hεδ : 4 * ε ^ 2 ≤ δ / 4)
1526    (hsmall : ∀ a ∈ I, ∀ b ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y a - Y b)‖ < ε)
1527    (hQ : clusterDispersionLift I Y < δ / 4)
1528    (hδ : δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y) :
1529    ∀ a ∈ I, ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y a)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) := by
1530  by_contra hcon
1531  push Not at hcon
1532  obtain ⟨a, ha, hta⟩ := hcon
1533  set t : Fin N → ℝ := fun b => Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y b)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) with ht
1534  have ht2 : ∀ b ∈ I, t b < 2 * ε := by
1535    intro b hb
1536    have h1 := Torus.rem_tdist_triangle (Torus.mk (Y b)) (Torus.mk (Y a)) (Torus.mk (Y j))
1537    have h2 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y b)) (Torus.mk (Y a)) = ‖geoRep (Y b - Y a)‖ := by
1538      unfold Torus.tdist geoRep
1539      rw [mk_sub']
1540    have h3 := hsmall b hb a ha
1541    show Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y b)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) < 2 * ε
1542    linarith
1543  have ht0 : ∀ b, 0 ≤ t b := fun b => norm_nonneg _
1544  have hε0 : 0 < ε := by
1545    have := ht0 a
1546    linarith
1547  set k : ℝ := (I.card : ℝ) with hk
1548  have hk1 : 1 ≤ k := by
1549    have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ (I.card : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hI.card_pos
1550    exact this
1551  -- the double sum over `insert j I`
1552  set ψ : Fin N → Fin N → ℝ := fun a b => pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y a) - Torus.mk (Y b)) with hψ
1553  have hQI : ∑ a ∈ I, ∑ b ∈ I, ψ a b = 2 * k * clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
1554    have h : clusterDispersionLift I Y = (1 / (2 * k)) * ∑ a ∈ I, ∑ b ∈ I, ψ a b := rfl
1555    have : (2 * k) ≠ 0 := by positivity
1556    rw [h]
1557    field_simp
1558  have hQ' : clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y * (2 * (k + 1)) =
1559      ψ j j + ∑ b ∈ I, ψ j b + (∑ a ∈ I, ψ a j + ∑ a ∈ I, ∑ b ∈ I, ψ a b) := by
1560    have h : clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y =
1561        (1 / (2 * (k + 1))) * ∑ a ∈ insert j I, ∑ b ∈ insert j I, ψ a b := by
1562      unfold clusterDispersionLift clusterDispersion
1563      rw [Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hj]
1564      push_cast
1565      rfl
1566    rw [h, Finset.sum_insert hj]
1567    simp only [Finset.sum_insert hj, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
1568    have : (2 * (k + 1)) ≠ 0 := by positivity
1569    field_simp
1570  have hψjj : ψ j j = 0 := by
1571    have h0 : ‖Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk (Y j) - Torus.mk (Y j))‖ = 0 := by
1572      have := (Torus.tdist_eq_zero_iff (Torus.mk (Y j)) (Torus.mk (Y j))).2 rfl
1573      exact this
1574    show pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y j) - Torus.mk (Y j)) = 0
1575    rw [pairDispersion_eq_sq _ (by rw [h0]; norm_num), h0]; norm_num
1576  have hψja : ∀ b ∈ I, ψ b j = t b ^ 2 := by
1577    intro b hb
1578    have h0 : ‖Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk (Y b) - Torus.mk (Y j))‖ = t b := rfl
1579    show pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y b) - Torus.mk (Y j)) = t b ^ 2
1580    rw [pairDispersion_eq_sq _ (by rw [h0]; linarith [ht2 b hb]), h0]
1581  have hψjb : ∀ b ∈ I, ψ j b = t b ^ 2 := by
1582    intro b hb
1583    have h0 : ‖Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk (Y j) - Torus.mk (Y b))‖ = t b := by
1584      show Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y j)) (Torus.mk (Y b)) = _
1585      exact Torus.rem_tdist_comm _ _
1586    show pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y j) - Torus.mk (Y b)) = t b ^ 2
1587    rw [pairDispersion_eq_sq _ (by rw [h0]; linarith [ht2 b hb]), h0]
1588  have hsum1 : ∑ b ∈ I, ψ j b ≤ k * (4 * ε ^ 2) := by
1589    calc ∑ b ∈ I, ψ j b = ∑ b ∈ I, t b ^ 2 := Finset.sum_congr rfl hψjb
1590      _ ≤ ∑ b ∈ I, 4 * ε ^ 2 := Finset.sum_le_sum fun b hb => by
1591          have := ht2 b hb
1592          nlinarith [ht0 b]
1593      _ = k * (4 * ε ^ 2) := by simp [hk]
1594  have hsum2 : ∑ b ∈ I, ψ b j ≤ k * (4 * ε ^ 2) := by
1595    calc ∑ b ∈ I, ψ b j = ∑ b ∈ I, t b ^ 2 := Finset.sum_congr rfl hψja
1596      _ ≤ ∑ b ∈ I, 4 * ε ^ 2 := Finset.sum_le_sum fun b hb => by
1597          have := ht2 b hb
1598          nlinarith [ht0 b]
1599      _ = k * (4 * ε ^ 2) := by simp [hk]
1600  have hQ0 : 0 ≤ clusterDispersionLift I Y := clusterDispersion_nonneg I _
1601  have hmain : clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y * (2 * (k + 1)) ≤
1602      2 * k * (4 * ε ^ 2) + 2 * k * clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
1603    rw [hQ', hψjj, hQI]; linarith
1604  have : δ * (2 * (k + 1)) ≤ 2 * k * (4 * ε ^ 2) + 2 * k * clusterDispersionLift I Y :=
1605    le_trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hδ (by positivity)) hmain
1606  nlinarith
1607
1608theorem rem_decomp (D : DynData d) {I : Finset (Fin N)} (hI : I.Nonempty)
1609    {Y : Fin N → Euc d} (hq : clusterDispersionLift I Y < clusterRadius I)
1610    (r : Torus d → Euc d)
1611    (hsmall : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8)
1612    (hloc : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, i ≠ j →
1613      periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)) =
1614        eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) + r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))) :
1615    clusterRemainder D I Y =
1616      (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I.erase i,
1617        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))) +
1618      (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ \ I,
1619        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1620          (Torus.matVec D.M (periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))) -
1621      ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (Y i)) := by
1622  obtain ⟨hneg, hcomp⟩ := rem_cluster_compat hsmall
1623  unfold clusterRemainder clusterGenerator
1624  rw [cluster_diffusion_term D I Y hq, fderiv_clusterDispersionLift I Y hq (liftedDrift D Y)]
1625  -- restrict the sum to `I`
1626  have hzero : ∀ i, i ∉ I → inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (liftedDrift D Y i) = 0 := by
1627    intro i hi
1628    simp [clusterCentered, hi]
1629  rw [← Finset.sum_subset (Finset.subset_univ I) (fun i _ hi => hzero i hi)]
1630  -- the drift of each `i ∈ I`
1631  have hdrift : ∀ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (liftedDrift D Y i) =
1632      (1 / (N : ℝ)) * (∑ j ∈ I.erase i,
1633        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1634          (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) +
1635        ∑ j ∈ I.erase i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1636          (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))) +
1637        ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ \ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1638          (Torus.matVec D.M (periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))) -
1639        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i) (gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (Y i)) := by
1640    intro i hi
1641    rw [liftedDrift_apply, inner_sub_right, inner_smul_right, inner_sum]
1642    have hset : Finset.univ.erase i = I.erase i ∪ (Finset.univ \ I) := by
1643      ext j
1644      simp only [Finset.mem_erase, Finset.mem_univ, and_true, Finset.mem_union, Finset.mem_sdiff,
1645        true_and]
1646      constructor
1647      · intro hj
1648        by_cases hjI : j ∈ I
1649        · exact Or.inl ⟨hj, hjI⟩
1650        · exact Or.inr hjI
1651      · rintro (⟨hj, _⟩ | hjI)
1652        · exact hj
1653        · rintro rfl; exact hjI hi
1654    have hdisj : Disjoint (I.erase i) (Finset.univ \ I) := by
1655      rw [Finset.disjoint_left]
1656      intro j hj hj'
1657      exact (Finset.mem_sdiff.1 hj').2 (Finset.mem_of_mem_erase hj)
1658    rw [hset, Finset.sum_union hdisj]
1659    have hsplit : ∀ j ∈ I.erase i,
1660        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1661          (Torus.matVec D.M (Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (Y i - Y j))) =
1662        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1663          (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) +
1664        inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1665          (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))) := by
1666      intro j hj
1667      have hji := Finset.mem_erase.1 hj
1668      show inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1669          (Torus.matVec D.M (periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))) = _
1670      rw [hloc i hi j hji.2 (Ne.symm hji.1)]
1671      unfold Torus.matVec
1672      rw [map_add, inner_add_right]
1673    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hsplit, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
1674    rfl
1675  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hdrift]
1676  have hA : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I.erase i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1677      (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) =
1678      ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1679      (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) := by
1680    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => ?_
1681    rw [← Finset.add_sum_erase I _ hi]
1682    have hx0 : geoRep (Y i - Y i) = 0 := by
1683      have h := hneg i hi i hi
1684      have h2 : (2 : ℝ) • geoRep (Y i - Y i) = 0 := by
1685        rw [two_smul]; nth_rewrite 1 [h]; exact neg_add_cancel _
1686      exact (smul_eq_zero.1 h2).resolve_left two_ne_zero
1687    rw [hx0]
1688    simp [eucRieszGrad, Torus.matVec]
1689  have hE : ∀ z : Euc d, eucRieszGrad D.s (-z) = -eucRieszGrad D.s z := by
1690    intro z
1691    simp [eucRieszGrad, rieszRadial]
1692  have hint := rem_internal_identity hI hneg hcomp (eucRieszGrad D.s) hE
1693    (LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap (Matrix.toEuclideanLin D.M))
1694  have hinternal : clusterInternal D I Y = (1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I.erase i,
1695      inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1696        (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)))) := by
1697    rw [hA]
1698    unfold clusterInternal
1699    congr 1
1700    exact hint.symm
1701  rw [hinternal]
1702  simp only [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
1703  ring
1704
1705theorem rem_bound_internal_remainder (D : DynData d) {I : Finset (Fin N)} {Y : Fin N → Euc d}
1706    (r : Torus d → Euc d) {Lr : ℝ} (hLr0 : 0 ≤ Lr)
1707    (hLr : ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ ≤ 1 / 8 → ‖r (Torus.mk x)‖ ≤ Lr * ‖x‖)
1708    (hsmall : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8)
1709    (hQ1 : clusterDispersionLift I Y =
1710      (1 / (2 * (I.card : ℝ))) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2)
1711    (hQ2 : clusterDispersionLift I Y = ∑ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2) (hI : I.Nonempty) :
1712    |∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I.erase i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1713        (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))| ≤
1714      3 * (I.card : ℝ) * ‖LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap (Matrix.toEuclideanLin D.M)‖ * Lr *
1715        clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
1716  set cm := LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap (Matrix.toEuclideanLin D.M) with hcm
1717  set c := clusterCentered I Y with hc
1718  set x : Fin N → Fin N → Euc d := fun i j => geoRep (Y i - Y j) with hx
1719  set k : ℝ := (I.card : ℝ) with hk
1720  have hk0 : 0 < k := by
1721    have : (0 : ℝ) < (I.card : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hI.card_pos
1722    exact this
1723  have hterm : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I.erase i,
1724      |inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i) (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))| ≤
1725        ‖cm‖ * Lr * (‖c i‖ ^ 2 + ‖x i j‖ ^ 2) := by
1726    intro i hi j hj
1727    have hjI := Finset.mem_of_mem_erase hj
1728    have hr : ‖r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))‖ ≤ Lr * ‖x i j‖ := by
1729      have := hLr (x i j) (hsmall i hi j hjI).le
1730      rwa [show Torus.mk (x i j) = Torus.mk (Y i - Y j) from rem_mk_geoRep_eq _] at this
1731    have h1 : ‖Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))‖ ≤ ‖cm‖ * (Lr * ‖x i j‖) :=
1732      (cm.le_opNorm _).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hr (norm_nonneg _))
1733    refine (abs_real_inner_le_norm _ _).trans ?_
1734    rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_two]
1735    calc 2 * ‖c i‖ * ‖Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)))‖
1736        ≤ 2 * ‖c i‖ * (‖cm‖ * (Lr * ‖x i j‖)) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
1737      _ = ‖cm‖ * Lr * (2 * ‖c i‖ * ‖x i j‖) := by ring
1738      _ ≤ ‖cm‖ * Lr * (‖c i‖ ^ 2 + ‖x i j‖ ^ 2) := by
1739          refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (by positivity)
1740          nlinarith [sq_nonneg (‖c i‖ - ‖x i j‖)]
1741  refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans ?_
1742  have h2 : ∀ i ∈ I, |∑ j ∈ I.erase i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • c i)
1743      (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))| ≤
1744      ∑ j ∈ I, ‖cm‖ * Lr * (‖c i‖ ^ 2 + ‖x i j‖ ^ 2) := by
1745    intro i hi
1746    refine (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans ?_
1747    refine (Finset.sum_le_sum (hterm i hi)).trans ?_
1748    exact Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg (Finset.erase_subset _ _)
1749      (fun j _ _ => by positivity)
1750  refine (Finset.sum_le_sum h2).trans ?_
1751  have h3 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖cm‖ * Lr * (‖c i‖ ^ 2 + ‖x i j‖ ^ 2) =
1752      ‖cm‖ * Lr * (k * ∑ i ∈ I, ‖c i‖ ^ 2 + ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖x i j‖ ^ 2) := by
1753    simp only [← Finset.mul_sum, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul, hk]
1754  rw [h3, ← hQ2]
1755  have h4 : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖x i j‖ ^ 2 = 2 * k * clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
1756    rw [hQ1]; field_simp; rfl
1757  rw [h4]
1758  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (norm_nonneg cm) hLr0]
1759
1760
1761/-- The remainder bound `B:eq:cluster-remainder` (M L11793-11801): for every `δ > 0` there are
1762`q_{I,δ} ∈ (0, q_I)` and `C_{I,δ}` with `ℛ_I ≥ -C_{I,δ} Q_I` whenever `Q_I < q_{I,δ}`, the
1763configuration is collision-free, and `Q_{I∪{j}} ≥ δ` for every `j ∉ I`
1764(`B:eq:larger-cluster-separated`).  For `I = univ` the separation condition is vacuous
1765(`clusterRemainder_bound_univ`). -/
1766theorem clusterRemainder_bound (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
1767    (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card) (δ : ℝ) (hδ : 0 < δ) :
1768    ∃ q C : ℝ, 0 < q ∧ q < clusterRadius I ∧ 0 ≤ C ∧
1769      ∀ Y : Fin N → Euc d, Y ∈ liftedFree d N → clusterDispersionLift I Y < q →
1770        (∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Y) →
1771        -C * clusterDispersionLift I Y ≤ clusterRemainder D I Y := by
1772  have hIne : I.Nonempty := Finset.card_pos.1 (by omega)
1773  obtain ⟨r, δK, hδK, hrs, hr0, hloc⟩ := hK.local_sing
1774  obtain ⟨Lr, hLr0, hLr⟩ := rem_exists_norm_le_of_smooth_zero r hrs hr0
1775  set k : ℝ := (I.card : ℝ) with hk
1776  have hk2 : (2 : ℝ) ≤ k := by
1777    have : (2 : ℝ) ≤ (I.card : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast hI
1778    exact this
1779  have hkpos : 0 < k := by linarith
1780  set ε₀ : ℝ := min (1 / 16) (Real.sqrt δ / 4) with hε₀
1781  have hsqrt : 0 < Real.sqrt δ := Real.sqrt_pos.2 hδ
1782  have hε₀pos : 0 < ε₀ := lt_min (by norm_num) (by positivity)
1783  have hε₀1 : ε₀ ≤ 1 / 16 := min_le_left _ _
1784  have hε₀δ : 4 * ε₀ ^ 2 ≤ δ / 4 := by
1785    have h1 : ε₀ ≤ Real.sqrt δ / 4 := min_le_right _ _
1786    have h2 : ε₀ ^ 2 ≤ (Real.sqrt δ / 4) ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ hε₀pos.le h1 2
1787    have h3 : (Real.sqrt δ) ^ 2 = δ := Real.sq_sqrt hδ.le
1788    have h4 : (Real.sqrt δ / 4) ^ 2 = δ / 16 := by rw [div_pow, h3]; norm_num
1789    linarith
1790  obtain ⟨Lg, hLg0, hLg⟩ := rem_kernel_lipschitz_off_zero hK.smoothOffZero hε₀pos
1791  obtain ⟨LV, hLV⟩ := rem_exists_lipschitz_gradient D.V hV
1792  set cm := LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap (Matrix.toEuclideanLin D.M) with hcm
1793  have hradpos : 0 < clusterRadius I := by unfold clusterRadius; positivity
1794  set q : ℝ := min (min (clusterRadius I / 2) (δK ^ 2 / (2 * k))) (min (ε₀ ^ 2 / (2 * k)) (δ / 4))
1795    with hq
1796  have hqpos : 0 < q :=
1797    lt_min (lt_min (by positivity) (by positivity)) (lt_min (by positivity) (by positivity))
1798  have hq1 : q ≤ clusterRadius I / 2 := (min_le_left _ _).trans (min_le_left _ _)
1799  have hq2 : q ≤ δK ^ 2 / (2 * k) := (min_le_left _ _).trans (min_le_right _ _)
1800  have hq3 : q ≤ ε₀ ^ 2 / (2 * k) := (min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_left _ _)
1801  have hq4 : q ≤ δ / 4 := (min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_right _ _)
1802  refine ⟨q, (3 * k * ‖cm‖ * Lr) / N + 2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg + 2 * (LV : ℝ), hqpos, by linarith, ?_, ?_⟩
1803  · positivity
1804  intro Y hY hQ hsep
1805  set Q := clusterDispersionLift I Y with hQdef
1806  have hQrad : Q < clusterRadius I := by linarith
1807  obtain ⟨hsmall, hQ1', hQ2'⟩ := clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid I Y hQrad
1808  have hQ1 : Q = (1 / (2 * k)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 := hQ1'
1809  have hQ2 : Q = ∑ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 := hQ2'
1810  have hQnn : 0 ≤ Q := clusterDispersion_nonneg I (fun i => Torus.mk (Y i))
1811  have h2k : 0 < 2 * k := by positivity
1812  have hS : ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 = 2 * k * Q := by
1813    rw [hQ1]; field_simp
1814  have hxsq : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 ≤ 2 * k * Q := by
1815    intro i hi j hj
1816    rw [← hS]
1817    refine le_trans (Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2)
1818      (fun j _ => by positivity) hj) ?_
1819    exact Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => ∑ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2)
1820      (fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => by positivity) hi
1821  have hcsq : ∀ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2 ≤ Q := by
1822    intro i hi
1823    rw [hQ2]
1824    exact Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ^ 2)
1825      (fun i _ => by positivity) hi
1826  have e1 : 2 * k * q ≤ δK ^ 2 := by
1827    have := (le_div_iff₀ h2k).1 hq2; linarith
1828  have e2 : 2 * k * q ≤ ε₀ ^ 2 := by
1829    have := (le_div_iff₀ h2k).1 hq3; linarith
1830  have hxlt : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < δK ∧ ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < ε₀ := by
1831    intro i hi j hj
1832    have h := hxsq i hi j hj
1833    have h3 : 2 * k * Q < 2 * k * q := mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left hQ h2k
1834    constructor
1835    · by_contra hc
1836      push Not at hc
1837      linarith [pow_le_pow_left₀ hδK.le hc 2]
1838    · by_contra hc
1839      push Not at hc
1840      linarith [pow_le_pow_left₀ hε₀pos.le hc 2]
1841  have hclt : ∀ i ∈ I, ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ ≤ ε₀ / 2 := by
1842    intro i hi
1843    have h := hcsq i hi
1844    by_contra hc
1845    push Not at hc
1846    have h4 : q ≤ ε₀ ^ 2 / 4 := by
1847      refine hq3.trans ?_
1848      rw [div_le_div_iff₀ h2k (by norm_num)]
1849      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) (sq_nonneg ε₀)
1850    linarith [pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity : 0 ≤ ε₀ / 2) hc.le 2]
1851  have hIne' : I.Nonempty := hIne
1852  obtain ⟨hneg, hcomp⟩ := rem_cluster_compat hsmall
1853  have hsumc := rem_clusterCentered_sum (I := I) (Y := Y) hneg
1854  have hlocI : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ j ∈ I, i ≠ j →
1855      periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)) =
1856        eucRieszGrad D.s (geoRep (Y i - Y j)) + r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)) := by
1857    intro i hi j hj hij
1858    have hne : geoRep (Y i - Y j) ≠ 0 := by
1859      intro h0
1860      apply mk_sub_ne_zero_of_mem_liftedFree hY hij
1861      rw [← rem_mk_geoRep_eq, h0]
1862      funext a; simp [Torus.mk]
1863    have := hloc _ (hxlt i hi j hj).1 hne
1864    rwa [rem_mk_geoRep_eq] at this
1865  have hdec := rem_decomp D hIne' hQrad r hsmall hlocI
1866  rw [hdec]
1867  -- compatible lifts
1868  obtain ⟨i₀, hi₀⟩ := hIne
1869  set ytil : Fin N → Euc d := fun i => Y i₀ - geoRep (Y i₀ - Y i) with hytil
1870  have hytil_mk : ∀ i, Torus.mk (ytil i) = Torus.mk (Y i) := by
1871    intro i
1872    simp only [hytil]
1873    rw [mk_sub', rem_mk_geoRep_eq, mk_sub']
1874    abel
1875  have hytil_sub : ∀ i ∈ I, ∀ l ∈ I, ytil i - ytil l = geoRep (Y i - Y l) := by
1876    intro i hi l hl
1877    have := hcomp i₀ hi₀ i hi l hl
1878    simp only [hytil]
1879    rw [this]
1880    abel
1881  set ybar : Euc d := (1 / k) • ∑ l ∈ I, ytil l with hybar
1882  have hyc : ∀ i ∈ I, ytil i - ybar = clusterCentered I Y i := by
1883    intro i hi
1884    rw [rem_clusterCentered_of_mem hi]
1885    have h1 : ∑ l ∈ I, geoRep (Y i - Y l) = ∑ l ∈ I, (ytil i - ytil l) :=
1886      Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l hl => (hytil_sub i hi l hl).symm
1887    rw [h1, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_const, hybar, smul_sub, ← Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul ℝ,
1888      smul_smul, one_div, inv_mul_cancel₀ hkpos.ne', one_smul]
1889  have hc_norm : ∀ i ∈ I, ‖ybar - ytil i‖ = ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ := by
1890    intro i hi; rw [norm_sub_rev, hyc i hi]
1891  have hNk : k ≤ (N : ℝ) := by
1892    have h : I.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_univ I
1893    have h' : (I.card : ℝ) ≤ (N : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h
1894    exact h'
1895  have hNpos : 0 < (N : ℝ) := by linarith
1896  -- (1) the smooth part of the internal force
1897  have hAr := rem_bound_internal_remainder D r hLr0 hLr hsmall hQ1' hQ2' hIne'
1898  -- (2) the exterior forces
1899  have hB : ∀ j ∈ Finset.univ \ I, |∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1900      (Torus.matVec D.M (periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))| ≤
1901      2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q := by
1902    intro j hj
1903    have hjI : j ∉ I := (Finset.mem_sdiff.1 hj).2
1904    have hsepj := rem_exterior_separation hIne' hjI hδ hε₀1 hε₀δ
1905      (fun a ha b hb => (hxlt a ha b hb).2) (by linarith) (hsep j hjI)
1906    have hF : ∀ i ∈ I, ‖Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (ytil i - Y j) -
1907        Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (ybar - Y j)‖ ≤ Lg * ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ := by
1908      intro i hi
1909      have hu : ε₀ ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (ytil i)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) := by
1910        rw [hytil_mk]; exact hsepj i hi
1911      have hv : ‖ybar - ytil i‖ ≤ ε₀ / 2 := by rw [hc_norm i hi]; exact hclt i hi
1912      have := hLg (ytil i) ybar (Y j) hu hv
1913      rw [norm_sub_rev, hc_norm i hi] at this
1914      exact this
1915    have key := rem_abs_sum_inner_le I (clusterCentered I Y) hsumc
1916      (fun i => Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (ytil i - Y j))
1917      (Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (ybar - Y j)) cm Lg hF
1918    have hF_eq : ∀ i, Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (ytil i - Y j) =
1919        periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j)) := by
1920      intro i
1921      show periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (ytil i - Y j)) = _
1922      rw [mk_sub', hytil_mk, ← mk_sub']
1923    simp only [hF_eq] at key
1924    rw [← hQ2] at key
1925    exact key
1926  have hBtot : |(1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ \ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1927      (Torus.matVec D.M (periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))| ≤
1928      2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q := by
1929    rw [Finset.sum_comm, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (by positivity : 0 < 1 / (N : ℝ))]
1930    have h1 := (Finset.abs_sum_le_sum_abs _ _).trans (Finset.sum_le_sum hB)
1931    rw [Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul] at h1
1932    have hcard : ((Finset.univ \ I).card : ℝ) ≤ N := by
1933      have : (Finset.univ \ I).card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_univ (Finset.univ \ I)
1934      exact_mod_cast this
1935    have h2 : 0 ≤ 2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q := by positivity
1936    calc 1 / (N : ℝ) * |∑ j ∈ Finset.univ \ I, ∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1937          (Torus.matVec D.M (periodicRieszGrad d D.s (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))|
1938        ≤ 1 / (N : ℝ) * (((Finset.univ \ I).card : ℝ) * (2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q)) :=
1939          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (by positivity)
1940      _ ≤ 1 / (N : ℝ) * ((N : ℝ) * (2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q)) :=
1941          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcard h2) (by positivity)
1942      _ = 2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q := by
1943          rw [← mul_assoc, one_div, inv_mul_cancel₀ hNpos.ne', one_mul]
1944  -- (3) the confinement
1945  have hVF : ∀ i ∈ I, ‖gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (ytil i) - gradient (Torus.lift D.V) ybar‖ ≤
1946      (LV : ℝ) * ‖clusterCentered I Y i‖ := by
1947    intro i hi
1948    have := hLV.dist_le_mul (ytil i) ybar
1949    rw [dist_eq_norm, dist_eq_norm, hyc i hi] at this
1950    exact this
1951  have hVkey := rem_abs_sum_inner_le I (clusterCentered I Y) hsumc
1952    (fun i => gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (ytil i)) (gradient (Torus.lift D.V) ybar)
1953    (ContinuousLinearMap.id ℝ (Euc d)) (LV : ℝ) hVF
1954  have hVeq : ∀ i, gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (ytil i) = gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (Y i) := by
1955    intro i
1956    rw [← Torus.grad_mk_aux, hytil_mk, Torus.grad_mk_aux]
1957  simp only [hVeq, ContinuousLinearMap.id_apply] at hVkey
1958  rw [← hQ2] at hVkey
1959  have hidn : ‖ContinuousLinearMap.id ℝ (Euc d)‖ ≤ 1 := ContinuousLinearMap.norm_id_le
1960  have hVkey' : |∑ i ∈ I, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1961      (gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (Y i))| ≤ 2 * (LV : ℝ) * Q := by
1962    refine hVkey.trans ?_
1963    have h0 : 0 ≤ 2 * (LV : ℝ) * Q := by positivity
1964    have e : 2 * ‖ContinuousLinearMap.id ℝ (Euc d)‖ * (LV : ℝ) * Q =
1965        ‖ContinuousLinearMap.id ℝ (Euc d)‖ * (2 * (LV : ℝ) * Q) := by ring
1966    rw [e]
1967    calc _ ≤ 1 * (2 * (LV : ℝ) * Q) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hidn h0
1968      _ = _ := one_mul _
1969  -- assemble
1970  have hArN : |(1 / (N : ℝ)) * ∑ i ∈ I, ∑ j ∈ I.erase i, inner ℝ ((2 : ℝ) • clusterCentered I Y i)
1971      (Torus.matVec D.M (r (Torus.mk (Y i - Y j))))| ≤ (3 * k * ‖cm‖ * Lr) / N * Q := by
1972    rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (by positivity : 0 < 1 / (N : ℝ))]
1973    calc 1 / (N : ℝ) * _ ≤ 1 / (N : ℝ) * (3 * k * ‖cm‖ * Lr * Q) :=
1974          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hAr (by positivity)
1975      _ = (3 * k * ‖cm‖ * Lr) / N * Q := by ring
1976  have h1 := abs_le.1 hArN
1977  have h2 := abs_le.1 hBtot
1978  have h3 := abs_le.1 hVkey'
1979  have e : -((3 * k * ‖cm‖ * Lr) / N + 2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg + 2 * (LV : ℝ)) * Q =
1980      -((3 * k * ‖cm‖ * Lr) / N * Q) - 2 * ‖cm‖ * Lg * Q - 2 * (LV : ℝ) * Q := by ring
1981  rw [e]
1982  linarith [h1.1, h2.1, h3.2]
1983
1984/-- The remainder bound for the full cluster `I = univ` (M L11801: the condition
1985`B:eq:larger-cluster-separated` is empty and the constant is independent of `δ`). -/
1986theorem clusterRemainder_bound_univ (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
1987    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hN : 2 ≤ N) :
1988    ∃ q C : ℝ, 0 < q ∧ q < clusterRadius (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin N)) ∧ 0 ≤ C ∧
1989      ∀ Y : Fin N → Euc d, Y ∈ liftedFree d N →
1990        clusterDispersionLift Finset.univ Y < q →
1991        -C * clusterDispersionLift Finset.univ Y ≤ clusterRemainder D Finset.univ Y := by
1992  obtain ⟨q, C, hq, hqr, hC, h⟩ := clusterRemainder_bound D hK hV (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin N))
1993    (by simpa using hN) 1 one_pos
1994  refine ⟨q, C, hq, hqr, hC, fun Y hY hQ => h Y hY hQ ?_⟩
1995  intro j hj
1996  exact absurd (Finset.mem_univ j) hj

2000/-- `Torus.grad φ (mk y) = gradient (lift φ) y`: the gradient of the lift is `ℤ^d`-periodic. -/
2001theorem Torus.grad_mk (φ : Torus d → ℝ) (hφ : Torus.IsCk 1 φ) (y : Euc d) :
2002    Torus.grad φ (Torus.mk y) = gradient (Torus.lift φ) y :=
2003  Torus.grad_mk_aux φ y

2005/-- The lifted drift is `C¹` on the (open) collision-free region. -/
2006theorem contDiffOn_liftedDrift (D : DynData d)
2007    (hK : Torus.IsSmoothOffZero (periodicRieszGrad d D.s)) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) :
2008    ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (liftedDrift (N := N) D) (liftedFree d N) := by
2009  have hK1 : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s)) {x : Euc d | Torus.mk x ≠ 0} :=
2010    hK.of_le (by exact_mod_cast le_top)
2011  have hMv : ContDiff ℝ 1 (Torus.matVec D.M) :=
2012    (LinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap (Matrix.toEuclideanLin D.M)).contDiff
2013  have hV1 : ContDiff ℝ 1 (gradient (Torus.lift D.V)) := by
2014    have h1 : ContDiff ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift D.V)) := hV.fderiv_right (by norm_num)
2015    have : gradient (Torus.lift D.V) =
2016        (InnerProductSpace.toDual ℝ (Euc d)).symm ∘ fderiv ℝ (Torus.lift D.V) := by
2017      funext x; rfl
2018    rw [this]
2019    exact (InnerProductSpace.toDual ℝ (Euc d)).symm.contDiff.comp h1
2020  refine contDiffOn_pi.2 fun i => ?_
2021  have e : (fun Y : Fin N → Euc d => liftedDrift D Y i) = fun Y =>
2022      (1 / (N : ℝ)) • ∑ j ∈ Finset.univ.erase i,
2023        Torus.matVec D.M (Torus.lift (periodicRieszGrad d D.s) (Y i - Y j)) -
2024          gradient (Torus.lift D.V) (Y i) := funext fun Y => liftedDrift_apply D Y i
2025  show ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (fun Y : Fin N → Euc d => liftedDrift D Y i) (liftedFree d N)
2026  rw [e]
2027  refine ContDiffOn.sub (ContDiffOn.const_smul _ (ContDiffOn.sum fun j hj => ?_)) ?_
2028  · have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => (Finset.mem_erase.1 hj).1 h.symm
2029    refine hMv.comp_contDiffOn (hK1.comp (f := fun Y : Fin N → Euc d => Y i - Y j) ?_ ?_)
2030    · exact ((contDiff_apply ℝ (Euc d) i).sub (contDiff_apply ℝ (Euc d) j)).contDiffOn
2031    · intro Y hY
2032      exact mk_sub_ne_zero_of_mem_liftedFree hY hij
2033  · exact (hV1.comp (contDiff_apply ℝ (Euc d) i)).contDiffOn
2034
2035/-- The lifted drift is continuous and locally Lipschitz on the collision-free region, from the
2036smoothness of `∇g` off the origin and `V ∈ C²` (M L11750: "the original, locally Lipschitz
2037drift on `(ℝ^d)^N ∖ 𝔇_N`"; L11997).  This is the analytic input of
2038`pathwiseUnique_of_locLipschitz` and `exists_sde_solution_maximal`. -/
2039theorem liftedDrift_locLipschitz (D : DynData d)
2040    (hK : Torus.IsSmoothOffZero (periodicRieszGrad d D.s)) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) :
2041    IsLocLipschitzDrift (liftedFree d N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) := by
2042  have hC := contDiffOn_liftedDrift (N := N) D hK hV
2043  refine ⟨?_, fun K hKc hKU T => ?_⟩
2044  · exact hC.continuousOn.comp continuousOn_snd (fun p hp => hp.2)
2045  · have hloc : LocallyLipschitzOn K (liftedDrift (N := N) D) := by
2046      intro x hx
2047      obtain ⟨L, t, ht, hL⟩ := ((hC x (hKU hx)).contDiffAt
2048        ((isOpen_liftedFree d N).mem_nhds (hKU hx))).exists_lipschitzOnWith
2049      exact ⟨L, t, mem_nhdsWithin_of_mem_nhds ht, hL⟩
2050    obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := hloc.exists_lipschitzOnWith_of_compact hKc
2051    exact ⟨L, fun _ _ => hL⟩

2053/-! ### The maximal solution and the collision alternative (Lemma `B:lem:particle-maximal`) -/
2054
2055section Probabilistic
2056
2057variable {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
2058
2059/-- The output of `exists_sde_solution_maximal` for the lifted particle SDE on `liftedFree d N`:
2060a stopping time `τ > 0`, a strong solution on `[0, τ)` with values in the collision-free region,
2061leaving every compact subset of the region as `t ↑ τ` on `{τ < ∞}`.  This is the source's
2062maximal solution `(X_N, τ_⋆)` of Lemma `B:lem:particle-maximal` (M L11731-11737), lifted. -/
2063structure IsMaximalParticleSolution (P : Measure Ω) (ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ)
2064    (W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ) (D : DynData d) (Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d))
2065    (τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) : Prop where
2066  init_meas : ∀ t : ℝ, Measurable[ℱ t] Y₀
2067  stopping : IsStoppingTime ℱ τ
2068  pos : ∀ ω, 0 < τ ω
2069  sol : IsAdditiveSDESolution P ℱ W' (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) (liftedNoise D) Y₀ τ Y
2070  mem : ∀ ω, ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → Y t ω ∈ liftedFree d N
2071  exit : ∀ ω, τ ω < ⊤ → ∀ K : Set (Fin N → Euc d), IsCompact K → K ⊆ liftedFree d N →
2072    ∀ t₀ : ℝ, (t₀ : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω →
2073      ∃ t : ℝ, t₀ < t ∧ (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ Y t ω ∉ K
2074
2075variable {P : Measure Ω} {ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ} {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ}
2076
2077/-- Lemma `B:lem:particle-maximal` (M L11731-11737), existence and uniqueness part: for every
2078Brownian filtration and every adapted collision-free initial lift there is a maximal strong
2079solution, which dominates and extends every other collision-free strong solution.  Reduces to
2080`exists_sde_solution_maximal` and `sde_pathwise_unique` on `liftedFree d N` with
2081`liftedDrift_locLipschitz`; the source's truncation route (L11740-11756) is not needed. -/
2082theorem particle_maximal [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
2083    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W') (Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d))
2084    (hY₀ : ∀ t : ℝ, Measurable[ℱ t] Y₀) (hY₀U : ∀ ω, Y₀ ω ∈ liftedFree d N) :
2085    ∃ (τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)),
2086      IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y ∧
2087      ∀ (τ' : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (Y' : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)),
2088        IsAdditiveSDESolution P ℱ W' (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) (liftedNoise D) Y₀ τ' Y' →
2089        (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ' ω → Y' t ω ∈ liftedFree d N) →
2090        ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, τ' ω ≤ τ ω ∧ ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ' ω → Y' t ω = Y t ω := by
2091  have hb := liftedDrift_locLipschitz (N := N) D hK.smoothOffZero hV
2092  obtain ⟨τ, Y, hstop, hpos, hsol, hmem, hexit, hmax⟩ := exists_sde_solution_maximal hW'
2093    (liftedFree d N) (isOpen_liftedFree d N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) hb (liftedNoise D) Y₀
2094    hY₀ hY₀U
2095  refine ⟨τ, Y, ⟨hY₀, hstop, hpos, hsol, hmem, hexit⟩, fun τ' Y' hsol' hmem' => ?_⟩
2096  have hle := hmax τ' Y' hsol' hmem'
2097  have huniq := sde_pathwise_unique hW' (liftedFree d N) (isOpen_liftedFree d N)
2098    (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) hb (liftedNoise D) Y₀ hsol hsol'
2099    (Eventually.of_forall hmem) hmem'
2100  filter_upwards [hle, huniq] with ω h1 h2
2101  exact ⟨h1, fun t ht htlt => (h2 t ht (lt_min (lt_of_lt_of_le htlt h1) htlt)).symm⟩
2102
2103/-- A configuration whose pair distances are all `≥ ε > 0` is collision-free. -/
2104theorem mem_liftedFree_of_sep {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) {Z : Fin N → Euc d}
2105    (hZ : ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j → ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))) :
2106    Z ∈ liftedFree d N := by
2107  rintro ⟨i, j, hij, h⟩
2108  have := hZ i j hij
2109  have h' : Torus.mk (Z i) = Torus.mk (Z j) := h
2110  rw [h', (Torus.tdist_eq_zero_iff _ _).2 rfl] at this
2111  linarith
2112
2113theorem isClosed_sepSet (d N : ℕ) (ε : ℝ) :
2114    IsClosed {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
2115      ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))} := by
2116  have e : {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
2117      ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))} = ⋂ i, ⋂ j, ⋂ (_ : i ≠ j),
2118      {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))} := by
2119    ext Z; simp only [Set.mem_iInter, Set.mem_ofPred_eq]
2120  rw [e]
2121  refine isClosed_iInter fun i => isClosed_iInter fun j => isClosed_iInter fun _ => ?_
2122  have h1 : Continuous fun Z : Fin N → Euc d => Torus.mk (Z i) :=
2123    Torus.continuous_mk.comp (continuous_apply i)
2124  have h2 : Continuous fun Z : Fin N → Euc d => Torus.mk (Z j) :=
2125    Torus.continuous_mk.comp (continuous_apply j)
2126  exact isClosed_le continuous_const (Torus.continuous_tdist_comp h1 h2)
2127
2128/-- The lifted drift is bounded on `ε`-separated configurations (periodicity reduces to a
2129compact fundamental box). -/
2130theorem liftedDrift_bounded_of_sep (D : DynData d)
2131    (hK : Torus.IsSmoothOffZero (periodicRieszGrad d D.s)) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
2132    {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
2133    ∃ B : ℝ, ∀ Z : Fin N → Euc d,
2134      (∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j → ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))) →
2135        ‖liftedDrift D Z‖ ≤ B := by
2136  let Box : Set (Fin N → Euc d) :=
2137    Set.pi Set.univ fun _ => (WithLp.toLp 2) '' (Set.pi Set.univ fun _ : Fin d => Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1)
2138  have hBox : IsCompact Box :=
2139    isCompact_univ_pi fun _ => (isCompact_univ_pi fun _ => isCompact_Icc).image
2140      (PiLp.continuous_toLp 2 _)
2141  have hF : IsCompact (Box ∩ {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
2142      ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))}) :=
2143    hBox.inter_right (isClosed_sepSet d N ε)
2144  have hcont : ContinuousOn (liftedDrift (N := N) D) (Box ∩ {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ i j : Fin N,
2145      i ≠ j → ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))}) :=
2146    (contDiffOn_liftedDrift D hK hV).continuousOn.mono fun Z hZ => mem_liftedFree_of_sep hε hZ.2
2147  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := hF.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hcont
2148  refine ⟨B, fun Z hZ => ?_⟩
2149  let Z' : Fin N → Euc d := fun i => Torus.rep (Torus.mk (Z i))
2150  have hmk : (fun i => Torus.mk (Z' i)) = fun i => Torus.mk (Z i) :=
2151    funext fun i => Torus.mk_rep _
2152  have hdr : liftedDrift D Z = liftedDrift D Z' := by
2153    unfold liftedDrift
2154    rw [hmk]
2155  rw [hdr]
2156  refine hB Z' ⟨?_, ?_⟩
2157  · intro i _
2158    refine ⟨fun a => Torus.rep (Torus.mk (Z i)) a, fun a _ => ?_, rfl⟩
2159    have := (AddCircle.equivIco (1 : ℝ) 0 (Torus.mk (Z i) a)).2
2160    simp only [zero_add] at this
2161    exact ⟨this.1, this.2.le⟩
2162  · intro i j hij
2163    have := hZ i j hij
2164    have e : Torus.mk (Z' i) = Torus.mk (Z i) := congrFun hmk i
2165    have e' : Torus.mk (Z' j) = Torus.mk (Z j) := congrFun hmk j
2166    rw [e, e']
2167    exact this
2168
2169/-- The collision alternative `B:eq:particle-collision-alternative` (M L11734): on `{τ < ∞}`,
2170`liminf_{t↑τ} min_{i<j} dist(x_i^t, x_j^t) = 0`.  From the exit property: if the pair distances
2171stayed `≥ ε` on `[t₀, τ)`, the drift would be bounded there and the path would stay in a compact
2172subset of `liftedFree d N` (bounded in the covering space by the integrated equation and the
2173continuity of the noise), contradicting `exit`. -/
2174theorem IsMaximalParticleSolution.collision_alternative [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d)
2175    (hK : KernelLocal d D.s) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
2176    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
2177    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) :
2178    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, τ ω < ⊤ → ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ t₀ : ℝ, 0 ≤ t₀ → (t₀ : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω →
2179      ∃ t : ℝ, t₀ < t ∧ (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧
2180        ∃ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j ∧ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) < ε := by
2181  filter_upwards [hsol.sol.eq] with ω hω hτ ε hε t₀ ht₀ ht₀τ
2182  by_contra hcon
2183  have hsep : ∀ t : ℝ, t₀ < t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
2184      ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) := by
2185    intro t ht htτ i j hij
2186    by_contra h
2187    exact hcon ⟨t, ht, htτ, i, j, hij, not_le.1 h⟩
2188  obtain ⟨T₁, hT₁⟩ := WithTop.ne_top_iff_exists.1 hτ.ne
2189  have hlt : ∀ t : ℝ, (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → t < T₁ := fun t ht => by
2190    rw [← hT₁] at ht
2191    exact_mod_cast ht
2192  have ht₀T : t₀ < T₁ := hlt t₀ ht₀τ
2193  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := liftedDrift_bounded_of_sep (N := N) D hK.smoothOffZero hV hε
2194  -- bound on `[0, t₀]`
2195  obtain ⟨R₁, hR₁⟩ := (isCompact_Icc.image_of_continuousOn
2196    (hsol.sol.cont ω t₀ ht₀ ht₀τ)).isBounded.exists_norm_le
2197  have hY₁ : ‖Y t₀ ω‖ ≤ R₁ := hR₁ _ ⟨t₀, ⟨ht₀, le_rfl⟩, rfl⟩
2198  -- bound on the noise
2199  have hNc : Continuous (addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) fun s => W' s ω) :=
2200    continuous_addNoise _ (hW'.cont ω)
2201  obtain ⟨R₂, hR₂⟩ := (isCompact_Icc (a := (0 : ℝ)) (b := T₁)).exists_bound_of_continuousOn
2202    hNc.continuousOn
2203  -- the path stays in a ball
2204  have hbound : ∀ t : ℝ, t₀ < t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω →
2205      ‖Y t ω‖ ≤ R₁ + |B| * |T₁| + 2 * R₂ := by
2206    intro t ht htτ
2207    have htT := hlt t htτ
2208    have ht0 : 0 ≤ t := ht₀.trans ht.le
2209    obtain ⟨hi₀, he₀⟩ := hω t₀ ht₀ ht₀τ
2210    obtain ⟨hi, he⟩ := hω t ht0 htτ
2211    have hii₀ : IntervalIntegrable (fun s => liftedDrift D (Y s ω)) volume 0 t₀ :=
2212      (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Icc_of_le ht₀).2 hi₀
2213    have hii : IntervalIntegrable (fun s => liftedDrift D (Y s ω)) volume 0 t :=
2214      (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Icc_of_le ht0).2 hi
2215    have hI : ∀ u : ℝ, 0 ≤ u → ∫ s in Set.Icc 0 u, liftedDrift D (Y s ω) =
2216        ∫ s in (0 : ℝ)..u, liftedDrift D (Y s ω) := fun u hu => by
2217      rw [intervalIntegral.integral_of_le hu, integral_Icc_eq_integral_Ioc]
2218    have hdiff : ∫ s in t₀..t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω) =
2219        (∫ s in Set.Icc 0 t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω)) -
2220          ∫ s in Set.Icc 0 t₀, liftedDrift D (Y s ω) := by
2221      rw [hI t ht0, hI t₀ ht₀, intervalIntegral.integral_interval_sub_left hii hii₀]
2222    have hint : ‖∫ s in t₀..t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω)‖ ≤ B * |t - t₀| := by
2223      refine intervalIntegral.norm_integral_le_of_norm_le_const fun s hs => ?_
2224      rw [Set.uIoc_of_le ht.le] at hs
2225      exact hB _ (hsep s hs.1 (by
2226        rw [← hT₁]; exact_mod_cast lt_of_le_of_lt hs.2 htT))
2227    have hnt := hR₂ t ⟨ht0, htT.le⟩
2228    have hnt₀ := hR₂ t₀ ⟨ht₀, ht₀T.le⟩
2229    have hNN : ∀ u : ℝ, ∑ k, ofNNRealTime W' u ω k • liftedNoise (N := N) D k =
2230        addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) u := fun u => rfl
2231    rw [hNN] at he he₀
2232    have hYt : Y t ω = Y t₀ ω + (∫ s in t₀..t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω)) +
2233        (addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) t -
2234          addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) t₀) := by
2235      rw [hdiff, he, he₀]
2236      abel
2237    have habs : |t - t₀| ≤ |T₁| := by
2238      rw [abs_of_nonneg (by linarith)]
2239      exact (by linarith : t - t₀ ≤ T₁).trans (le_abs_self T₁)
2240    have hBt : B * |t - t₀| ≤ |B| * |T₁| :=
2241      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (le_abs_self B) (abs_nonneg _)).trans
2242        (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left habs (abs_nonneg _))
2243    rw [hYt]
2244    have h1 := norm_add_le (Y t₀ ω + ∫ s in t₀..t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω))
2245      (addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) t -
2246        addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) t₀)
2247    have h2 := norm_add_le (Y t₀ ω) (∫ s in t₀..t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω))
2248    have h3 := norm_sub_le (addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) t)
2249      (addNoise (liftedNoise (N := N) D) (fun s => W' s ω) t₀)
2250    linarith
2251  -- the compact set
2252  set R := R₁ + |B| * |T₁| + 2 * R₂ with hR
2253  let K : Set (Fin N → Euc d) := Metric.closedBall 0 R ∩ {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ i j : Fin N,
2254    i ≠ j → ε ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Z i)) (Torus.mk (Z j))}
2255  have hKc : IsCompact K := (isCompact_closedBall _ _).inter_right (isClosed_sepSet d N ε)
2256  have hKU : K ⊆ liftedFree d N := fun Z hZ => mem_liftedFree_of_sep hε hZ.2
2257  obtain ⟨t, ht, htτ, hnot⟩ := hsol.exit ω hτ K hKc hKU t₀ ht₀τ
2258  refine hnot ⟨?_, hsep t ht htτ⟩
2259  rw [mem_closedBall_zero_iff]
2260  exact hbound t ht htτ
2261
2262/-! ### The Itô equation for `Q_I` along the maximal solution (Lemma `B:lem:cluster-ito`) -/
2263
2264/-- The martingale integrand `k ↦ D Q_I(Y_s)[σ_k]` of `B:eq:cluster-ito`. -/
2265def clusterNoiseIntegrand (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) :
2266    ℝ → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ :=
2267  fun s ω k => fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) (Y s ω) (liftedNoise D k)
2268
2269/-! ### The logarithmic barrier (Lemma `B:lem:cluster-barrier`, M L11849-11910) -/
2270
2271/-- The event `inf_{0 ≤ t < T ∧ τ} Q_I(X_t) = 0` of `B:eq:larger-cluster-induction-hypothesis`
2272(M L11852), written without a junk infimum: `Q_I(Y_t)` takes values below every `ε > 0` at some
2273time `t ∈ [0, T)` before `τ`. -/
2274def ClusterCollapse (I : Finset (Fin N)) (T : ℝ) (τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ)
2275    (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) (ω : Ω) : Prop :=
2276  ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∃ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t ∧ t < T ∧ (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧
2277    clusterDispersionLift I (Y t ω) < ε
2278
2279/-- The event `{σ_{I,a} ≤ T ∧ η_{I,δ} ∧ τ}` of M L11875-11890: `Q_I` reaches the level `a` at
2280some time `t ≤ T` before `τ` and before any one-point enlargement `Q_{I∪{j}}` reaches `δ`. -/
2281def ClusterReach (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ T : ℝ) (τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ)
2282    (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) (ω : Ω) : Prop :=
2283  ∃ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t ∧ t ≤ T ∧ (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ clusterDispersionLift I (Y t ω) ≤ a ∧
2284    ∀ s : ℝ, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ t → ∀ j, j ∉ I → δ < clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) (Y s ω)
2285
2286namespace ClusterBarrier
2287
2288
2289/-- The smooth step used for the logarithmic barrier: `0` on `x ≤ q/4`, `1` on `x ≥ q/2`. -/
2290def barrierStep (q x : ℝ) : ℝ := Real.smoothTransition ((x - q / 4) / (q / 4))
2291
2292/-- The logarithmic barrier: `-log x` for `x ≤ q/4`, constant `-log (q/2)` for `x ≥ q/2`. -/
2293def barrierPhi (q x : ℝ) : ℝ :=
2294  -Real.log x + barrierStep q x * (Real.log x - Real.log (q / 2))
2295
2296section Barrier
2297
2298variable {q : ℝ}
2299
2300theorem barrierStep_contDiff (hq : 0 < q) : ContDiff ℝ 2 (barrierStep q) := by
2301  unfold barrierStep
2302  exact (Real.smoothTransition.contDiff (n := 2)).comp ((contDiff_id.sub contDiff_const).div_const _)
2303
2304theorem barrierStep_of_le (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ≤ q / 4) : barrierStep q x = 0 := by
2305  unfold barrierStep
2306  apply Real.smoothTransition.zero_of_nonpos
2307  have : 0 < q / 4 := by positivity
2308  rw [div_nonpos_iff]; right; exact ⟨by linarith, this.le⟩
2309
2310theorem barrierStep_of_ge (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : q / 2 ≤ x) : barrierStep q x = 1 := by
2311  unfold barrierStep
2312  apply Real.smoothTransition.one_of_one_le
2313  have : 0 < q / 4 := by positivity
2314  rw [le_div_iff₀ this]; linarith
2315
2316theorem barrierPhi_of_le (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ≤ q / 4) : barrierPhi q x = -Real.log x := by
2317  unfold barrierPhi; rw [barrierStep_of_le hq hx]; ring
2318
2319theorem barrierPhi_of_ge (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : q / 2 ≤ x) :
2320    barrierPhi q x = -Real.log (q / 2) := by
2321  unfold barrierPhi; rw [barrierStep_of_ge hq hx]; ring
2322
2323theorem barrierPhi_contDiffOn (hq : 0 < q) : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 (barrierPhi q) (Set.Ioi 0) := by
2324  unfold barrierPhi
2325  have h1 : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 Real.log (Set.Ioi 0) := fun x hx =>
2326    (Real.contDiffAt_log.2 (ne_of_gt hx)).contDiffWithinAt
2327  have h2 : ContDiff ℝ 2 (barrierStep q) := barrierStep_contDiff hq
2328  exact h1.neg.add (h2.contDiffOn.mul (h1.sub contDiffOn_const))
2329
2330theorem barrierStep_monotone (hq : 0 < q) : Monotone (barrierStep q) := by
2331  intro x y hxy
2332  unfold barrierStep
2333  apply Real.smoothTransition.monotone
2334  have : 0 < q / 4 := by positivity
2335  exact div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) this.le
2336
2337theorem barrierStep_le_one (q x : ℝ) : barrierStep q x ≤ 1 := Real.smoothTransition.le_one _
2338
2339theorem barrierStep_nonneg (q x : ℝ) : 0 ≤ barrierStep q x := Real.smoothTransition.nonneg _
2340
2341theorem barrierPhi_hasDerivAt (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) :
2342    HasDerivAt (barrierPhi q)
2343      (-(1 / x) + (deriv (barrierStep q) x * (Real.log x - Real.log (q / 2)) +
2344        barrierStep q x * (1 / x))) x := by
2345  have hs : DifferentiableAt ℝ (barrierStep q) x :=
2346    ((barrierStep_contDiff hq).differentiable (by norm_num)) x
2347  have hl : HasDerivAt Real.log (1 / x) x := by
2348    simpa [one_div] using Real.hasDerivAt_log hx.ne'
2349  unfold barrierPhi
2350  exact hl.neg.add (hs.hasDerivAt.mul (hl.sub_const _))
2351
2352theorem deriv_barrierPhi_nonpos (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) : deriv (barrierPhi q) x ≤ 0 := by
2353  rw [(barrierPhi_hasDerivAt hq hx).deriv]
2354  have hs' : 0 ≤ deriv (barrierStep q) x := (barrierStep_monotone hq).deriv_nonneg
2355  have h1 := barrierStep_le_one q x
2356  have h0 := barrierStep_nonneg q x
2357  rcases le_or_gt x (q / 2) with hxq | hxq
2358  · have hlog : Real.log x - Real.log (q / 2) ≤ 0 := by
2359      have := Real.log_le_log hx hxq
2360      linarith
2361    have h2 : deriv (barrierStep q) x * (Real.log x - Real.log (q / 2)) ≤ 0 :=
2362      mul_nonpos_of_nonneg_of_nonpos hs' hlog
2363    have h3 : -(1 / x) + barrierStep q x * (1 / x) ≤ 0 := by
2364      have : 0 < 1 / x := by positivity
2365      nlinarith
2366    linarith
2367  · have hconst : barrierStep q =ᶠ[𝓝 x] fun _ => (1 : ℝ) := by
2368      filter_upwards [Ioi_mem_nhds hxq] with y hy
2369      exact barrierStep_of_ge hq (le_of_lt hy)
2370    have hd : deriv (barrierStep q) x = 0 := by
2371      rw [hconst.deriv_eq]; simp
2372    rw [hd, barrierStep_of_ge hq hxq.le]
2373    simp
2374
2375theorem barrierPhi_antitoneOn (hq : 0 < q) : AntitoneOn (barrierPhi q) (Set.Ioi 0) := by
2376  have hd : DifferentiableOn ℝ (barrierPhi q) (Set.Ioi 0) :=
2377    (barrierPhi_contDiffOn hq).differentiableOn (by norm_num)
2378  refine antitoneOn_of_deriv_nonpos (convex_Ioi 0) hd.continuousOn
2379    (by simpa using hd) (fun x hx => ?_)
2380  rw [interior_Ioi] at hx
2381  exact deriv_barrierPhi_nonpos hq hx
2382
2383theorem barrierPhi_ge (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) : -Real.log (q / 2) ≤ barrierPhi q x := by
2384  rcases le_or_gt x (q / 2) with h | h
2385  · have := barrierPhi_antitoneOn hq hx (show (0:ℝ) < q / 2 by positivity) h
2386    rwa [barrierPhi_of_ge hq le_rfl] at this
2387  · rw [barrierPhi_of_ge hq h.le]
2388
2389theorem barrierPhi_eventuallyEq_const (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : q / 2 < x) :
2390    barrierPhi q =ᶠ[𝓝 x] fun _ => -Real.log (q / 2) := by
2391  filter_upwards [Ioi_mem_nhds hx] with y hy
2392  exact barrierPhi_of_ge hq hy.le
2393
2394theorem deriv_barrierPhi_of_gt (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : q / 2 < x) : deriv (barrierPhi q) x = 0 := by
2395  rw [(barrierPhi_eventuallyEq_const hq hx).deriv_eq]; simp
2396
2397theorem deriv2_barrierPhi_of_gt (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx : q / 2 < x) :
2398    deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) x = 0 := by
2399  have : deriv (barrierPhi q) =ᶠ[𝓝 x] fun _ => (0 : ℝ) := by
2400    filter_upwards [Ioi_mem_nhds hx] with y hy
2401    exact deriv_barrierPhi_of_gt hq hy
2402  rw [this.deriv_eq]; simp
2403
2404theorem deriv_barrierPhi_of_le (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx0 : 0 < x) (hx : x < q / 4) :
2405    deriv (barrierPhi q) x = -(1 / x) := by
2406  have : barrierPhi q =ᶠ[𝓝 x] fun y => -Real.log y := by
2407    filter_upwards [Iio_mem_nhds hx] with y hy
2408    exact barrierPhi_of_le hq hy.le
2409  rw [this.deriv_eq, deriv.fun_neg, Real.deriv_log]; simp
2410
2411theorem deriv2_barrierPhi_of_le (hq : 0 < q) {x : ℝ} (hx0 : 0 < x) (hx : x < q / 4) :
2412    deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) x = 1 / x ^ 2 := by
2413  have : deriv (barrierPhi q) =ᶠ[𝓝 x] fun y => -(1 / y) := by
2414    filter_upwards [Iio_mem_nhds hx, Ioi_mem_nhds hx0] with y hy hy0
2415    exact deriv_barrierPhi_of_le hq hy0 hy
2416  rw [this.deriv_eq]
2417  simp [deriv.fun_neg, deriv_inv]
2418
2419end Barrier
2420
2421section ChainRule
2422
2423variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
2424
2425/-- First-derivative chain rule for `Φ ∘ Q` at a point where `Q > 0` and `Φ` is `C²` on `(0,∞)`. -/
2426theorem fderiv_comp_pos {Φ : ℝ → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 Φ (Set.Ioi 0)) {Q : E → ℝ}
2427    (hQ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Q) {Y : E} (hY : 0 < Q Y) :
2428    ∀ᶠ Y' in 𝓝 Y, fderiv ℝ (Φ ∘ Q) Y' = deriv Φ (Q Y') • fderiv ℝ Q Y' := by
2429  have hopen : ∀ᶠ Y' in 𝓝 Y, 0 < Q Y' := hQ.continuous.continuousAt.eventually (lt_mem_nhds hY)
2430  filter_upwards [hopen] with Y' hY'
2431  have hΦd : DifferentiableAt ℝ Φ (Q Y') :=
2432    ((hΦ.differentiableOn (by norm_num)).differentiableAt (Ioi_mem_nhds hY'))
2433  have hQd : DifferentiableAt ℝ Q Y' := (hQ.differentiable (by norm_num)) Y'
2434  have := hΦd.hasDerivAt.comp_hasFDerivAt Y' hQd.hasFDerivAt
2435  rw [this.fderiv]
2436
2437theorem fderiv_comp_pos_apply {Φ : ℝ → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 Φ (Set.Ioi 0)) {Q : E → ℝ}
2438    (hQ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Q) {Y : E} (hY : 0 < Q Y) (v : E) :
2439    fderiv ℝ (Φ ∘ Q) Y v = deriv Φ (Q Y) * fderiv ℝ Q Y v := by
2440  rw [(fderiv_comp_pos hΦ hQ hY).self_of_nhds]; simp
2441
2442theorem fderiv_fderiv_comp_pos_apply {Φ : ℝ → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 Φ (Set.Ioi 0)) {Q : E → ℝ}
2443    (hQ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Q) {Y : E} (hY : 0 < Q Y) (v w : E) :
2444    fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ (Φ ∘ Q)) Y v w =
2445      deriv (deriv Φ) (Q Y) * (fderiv ℝ Q Y v * fderiv ℝ Q Y w) +
2446        deriv Φ (Q Y) * fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Q) Y v w := by
2447  have hnhds : Set.Ioi (0 : ℝ) ∈ 𝓝 (Q Y) := Ioi_mem_nhds hY
2448  have hΦ2 : DifferentiableAt ℝ (deriv Φ) (Q Y) := by
2449    have h1 : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (deriv Φ) (Set.Ioi 0) := by
2450      have := hΦ.deriv_of_isOpen isOpen_Ioi (m := 1) (by norm_num)
2451      exact this
2452    exact (h1.differentiableOn (by norm_num)).differentiableAt hnhds
2453  have hQd : DifferentiableAt ℝ Q Y := (hQ.differentiable (by norm_num)) Y
2454  have hF : HasFDerivAt (fun Y' => deriv Φ (Q Y'))
2455      (deriv (deriv Φ) (Q Y) • fderiv ℝ Q Y) Y := by
2456    exact hΦ2.hasDerivAt.comp_hasFDerivAt Y hQd.hasFDerivAt
2457  have hf : HasFDerivAt (fderiv ℝ Q) (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Q) Y) Y := by
2458    have h1 : ContDiff ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ Q) := hQ.fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_num)
2459    exact ((h1.differentiable (by norm_num)) Y).hasFDerivAt
2460  have hprod : HasFDerivAt (fun Y' => deriv Φ (Q Y') • fderiv ℝ Q Y')
2461      (deriv Φ (Q Y) • fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Q) Y +
2462        (deriv (deriv Φ) (Q Y) • fderiv ℝ Q Y).smulRight (fderiv ℝ Q Y)) Y := hF.smul hf
2463  have heq : fderiv ℝ (Φ ∘ Q) =ᶠ[𝓝 Y] fun Y' => deriv Φ (Q Y') • fderiv ℝ Q Y' :=
2464    fderiv_comp_pos hΦ hQ hY
2465  rw [heq.fderiv_eq, hprod.fderiv]
2466  simp [smul_eq_mul]
2467  ring
2468
2469end ChainRule
2470
2471section Generator
2472
2473variable {d N : ℕ}
2474
2475theorem clusterDispersionLift_pos {I : Finset (Fin N)} (hI : 2 ≤ I.card) {Y : Fin N → Euc d}
2476    (hY : Y ∈ liftedFree d N) : 0 < clusterDispersionLift I Y := by
2477  rcases (clusterDispersion_nonneg I (fun i => Torus.mk (Y i))).lt_or_eq with h | h
2478  · exact h
2479  · exfalso
2480    have := (clusterDispersion_eq_zero_iff I _).1 h.symm
2481    obtain ⟨i, hi, j, hj, hij⟩ := Finset.one_lt_card.1 (by omega : 1 < I.card)
2482    exact hY ⟨i, j, hij, this i hi j hj⟩
2483
2484theorem contDiff_two_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) :
2485    ContDiff ℝ 2 (clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I) :=
2486  (contDiff_clusterDispersionLift I).of_le (WithTop.coe_le_coe.2 le_top)
2487
2488/-- The generator `D g[b] + ½ ∑_k D² g[σ_k, σ_k]` of the lifted particle SDE applied to `g`
2489(the time-independent Itô drift). -/
2490def diffGen (D : DynData d) (g : (Fin N → Euc d) → ℝ) (Y : Fin N → Euc d) : ℝ :=
2491  fderiv ℝ g Y (liftedDrift D Y) + (1 / 2) * ∑ k : Fin N × Fin d,
2492    fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ g) Y (liftedNoise D k) (liftedNoise D k)
2493
2494theorem clusterGenerator_eq_diffGen (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) :
2495    clusterGenerator D I = diffGen D (clusterDispersionLift I) := rfl
2496
2497theorem diffGen_barrier (D : DynData d) (I : Finset (Fin N)) {q : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q)
2498    {Y : Fin N → Euc d} (hY : 0 < clusterDispersionLift I Y) :
2499    diffGen D (barrierPhi q ∘ clusterDispersionLift I) Y =
2500      deriv (barrierPhi q) (clusterDispersionLift I Y) * clusterGenerator D I Y +
2501        (1 / 2) * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) (clusterDispersionLift I Y) *
2502          ∑ k : Fin N × Fin d, (fderiv ℝ (clusterDispersionLift I) Y (liftedNoise D k)) ^ 2 := by
2503  have hQ := contDiff_two_clusterDispersionLift (d := d) (N := N) I
2504  have hΦ := barrierPhi_contDiffOn hq
2505  unfold diffGen clusterGenerator
2506  rw [fderiv_comp_pos_apply hΦ hQ hY]
2507  simp_rw [fderiv_fderiv_comp_pos_apply hΦ hQ hY]
2508  simp only [Finset.sum_add_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, sq]
2509  ring
2510
2511/-- The pointwise generator bound for the barrier `Φ ∘ Q_I`: on the part `S` of the
2512collision-free region where the remainder bound holds, `ℒ(Φ ∘ Q_I) ≤ C'`. -/
2513theorem barrier_generator_bound (D : DynData d) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (I : Finset (Fin N))
2514    (hdk : D.invβ = 0 ∨ 2 ≤ d * (I.card - 1)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card) {q C₀ : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q)
2515    (hqr : q < clusterRadius I) (hC₀ : 0 ≤ C₀) (S : Set (Fin N → Euc d))
2516    (hrem : ∀ Y ∈ S, Y ∈ liftedFree d N → clusterDispersionLift I Y < q →
2517      -C₀ * clusterDispersionLift I Y ≤ clusterRemainder D I Y) :
2518    ∃ C' : ℝ, 0 ≤ C' ∧ ∀ Y ∈ S, Y ∈ liftedFree d N →
2519      diffGen D (barrierPhi q ∘ clusterDispersionLift I) Y ≤ C' := by
2520  have hΦ := barrierPhi_contDiffOn hq
2521  have hd1 : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (deriv (barrierPhi q)) (Set.Ioi 0) :=
2522    hΦ.deriv_of_isOpen isOpen_Ioi (m := 1) (by norm_num)
2523  have hc1 : ContinuousOn (deriv (barrierPhi q)) (Set.Ioi 0) := hd1.continuousOn
2524  have hc2 : ContinuousOn (deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q))) (Set.Ioi 0) :=
2525    hd1.continuousOn_deriv_of_isOpen isOpen_Ioi le_rfl
2526  set f : ℝ → ℝ := fun x => |deriv (barrierPhi q) x| * C₀ * x +
2527    4 * D.invβ * x * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) x| with hf
2528  have hsub : Set.Icc (q / 4) (3 * q / 4) ⊆ Set.Ioi 0 := fun x hx => by
2529    simp only [Set.mem_Icc, Set.mem_Ioi] at hx ⊢; linarith [hx.1]
2530  have hfc : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc (q / 4) (3 * q / 4)) := by
2531    have a1 := (hc1.mono hsub).abs
2532    have a2 := (hc2.mono hsub).abs
2533    exact ((a1.mul continuousOn_const).mul continuousOn_id).add
2534      (((continuousOn_const.mul continuousOn_id)).mul a2)
2535  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := isCompact_Icc.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hfc
2536  have hc₁ : 4 * D.invβ ≤ 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ := by
2537    rcases hdk with h | h
2538    · rw [h]; simp
2539    · have h' : (2 : ℝ) ≤ (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) := by
2540        have : ((2 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ ((d * (I.card - 1) : ℕ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast h
2541        rw [Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_sub (by omega)] at this
2542        simpa using this
2543      nlinarith [D.hβ]
2544  have hc₁0 : 0 ≤ 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ := by
2545    have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ I.card := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 1 ≤ I.card)
2546    have := D.hβ
2547    positivity
2548  refine ⟨C₀ + |B|, by positivity, fun Y hYS hYf => ?_⟩
2549  have hQpos := clusterDispersionLift_pos hI hYf
2550  set a := clusterDispersionLift I Y with ha
2551  rw [diffGen_barrier D I hq hQpos]
2552  rcases le_or_gt (3 * q / 4) a with h34 | h34
2553  · have h1 : deriv (barrierPhi q) a = 0 := deriv_barrierPhi_of_gt hq (by linarith)
2554    have h2 : deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a = 0 := deriv2_barrierPhi_of_gt hq (by linarith)
2555    rw [h1, h2]; simp; positivity
2556  · have hQr : a < clusterRadius I := by linarith
2557    have hqv := cluster_qv D I Y hQr
2558    rw [hqv]
2559    have hG : clusterGenerator D I Y = 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ +
2560        clusterInternal D I Y + clusterRemainder D I Y := by
2561      unfold clusterRemainder; ring
2562    have hA := clusterInternal_nonneg D hM I Y
2563    have hR := hrem Y hYS hYf (by linarith)
2564    have hs := deriv_barrierPhi_nonpos hq hQpos
2565    set s := deriv (barrierPhi q) a with hsdef
2566    set c₁ := 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ with hc₁def
2567    have hGlow : c₁ - C₀ * a ≤ clusterGenerator D I Y := by rw [hG]; linarith
2568    have h3 : s * clusterGenerator D I Y ≤ s * (c₁ - C₀ * a) :=
2569      mul_le_mul_of_nonpos_left hGlow hs
2570    rcases lt_or_ge a (q / 4) with h4 | h4
2571    · have e1 : s = -(1 / a) := deriv_barrierPhi_of_le hq hQpos h4
2572      have e2 : deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a = 1 / a ^ 2 := deriv2_barrierPhi_of_le hq hQpos h4
2573      rw [e2]
2574      have : s * (c₁ - C₀ * a) + 1 / 2 * (1 / a ^ 2) * (8 * D.invβ * a) =
2575          (-(c₁) + 4 * D.invβ) / a + C₀ := by
2576        rw [e1]; field_simp; ring
2577      have h5 : (-(c₁) + 4 * D.invβ) / a ≤ 0 :=
2578        div_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg (by linarith) hQpos.le
2579      have h6 : 1 / 2 * (1 / a ^ 2) * (8 * D.invβ * a) ≥ 0 := by
2580        have := D.hβ; positivity
2581      nlinarith [abs_nonneg B]
2582    · have hmem : a ∈ Set.Icc (q / 4) (3 * q / 4) := ⟨h4, h34.le⟩
2583      have hfa := hB a hmem
2584      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at hfa
2585      have hfa' : f a ≤ |B| := ((le_abs_self (f a)).trans hfa).trans (le_abs_self B)
2586      have habs : |s| = -s := abs_of_nonpos hs
2587      have e3 : s * (c₁ - C₀ * a) ≤ |s| * C₀ * a := by
2588        rw [habs]; nlinarith [mul_nonneg (neg_nonneg.2 hs) hc₁0]
2589      have e4 : 1 / 2 * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a * (8 * D.invβ * a) ≤
2590          4 * D.invβ * a * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a| := by
2591        have := D.hβ
2592        have h0 : 0 ≤ 4 * D.invβ * a := by positivity
2593        calc 1 / 2 * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a * (8 * D.invβ * a)
2594            = 4 * D.invβ * a * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a := by ring
2595          _ ≤ 4 * D.invβ * a * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a| :=
2596            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_abs_self _) h0
2597      have : f a = |s| * C₀ * a + 4 * D.invβ * a * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a| := rfl
2598      nlinarith [abs_nonneg B]
2599  
2600end Generator
2601
2602section ProgressiveStopped
2603
2604variable {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
2605  {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [MeasurableSpace E] [BorelSpace E]
2606  [SecondCountableTopology E]
2607
2608/-- The dyadic time grid below `s`: `max(⌊2^m s⌋, 0) / 2^m ∈ [0, max s 0]`. -/
2609def gridFloor (m : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : ℝ := ((max (⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋) 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m
2610
2611theorem gridFloor_nonneg (m : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : 0 ≤ gridFloor m s := by
2612  unfold gridFloor
2613  have : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ((max (⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋) 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast le_max_right _ _
2614  positivity
2615
2616theorem gridFloor_le (m : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : gridFloor m s ≤ max s 0 := by
2617  unfold gridFloor
2618  have hpos : (0 : ℝ) < 2 ^ m := by positivity
2619  rw [div_le_iff₀ hpos]
2620  rcases le_total (⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋) 0 with h | h
2621  · rw [max_eq_right h]
2622    simp only [Int.cast_zero]
2623    positivity
2624  · rw [max_eq_left h]
2625    calc ((⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ 2 ^ m * s := Int.floor_le _
2626      _ ≤ 2 ^ m * max s 0 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_max_left _ _) hpos.le
2627      _ = max s 0 * 2 ^ m := mul_comm _ _
2628
2629theorem gridFloor_eq_zero_of_nonpos (m : ℕ) {s : ℝ} (hs : s ≤ 0) : gridFloor m s = 0 := by
2630  unfold gridFloor
2631  have : ⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋ ≤ 0 := by
2632    rw [Int.floor_le_iff]
2633    simp only [Int.cast_zero, zero_add]
2634    have : (2 : ℝ) ^ m * s ≤ 0 := mul_nonpos_of_nonneg_of_nonpos (by positivity) hs
2635    linarith
2636  rw [max_eq_right this]; simp
2637
2638theorem tendsto_gridFloor (s : ℝ) : Tendsto (fun m : ℕ => gridFloor m s) atTop (𝓝 (max s 0)) := by
2639  rcases le_or_gt s 0 with hs | hs
2640  · simp only [gridFloor_eq_zero_of_nonpos _ hs, max_eq_right hs]
2641    exact tendsto_const_nhds
2642  · rw [max_eq_left hs.le]
2643    have hlow : ∀ m : ℕ, s - (1 / 2) ^ m ≤ gridFloor m s := by
2644      intro m
2645      unfold gridFloor
2646      have hpos : (0 : ℝ) < 2 ^ m := by positivity
2647      rw [le_div_iff₀ hpos]
2648      have h1 : (2 : ℝ) ^ m * s < ⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋ + 1 := Int.lt_floor_add_one _
2649      have h2 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ((max (⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋) 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast le_max_right _ _
2650      have h3 : ((⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋ : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ ((max (⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * s⌋) 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by
2651        exact_mod_cast le_max_left _ _
2652      have e : (s - (1 / 2) ^ m) * 2 ^ m = 2 ^ m * s - 1 := by
2653        rw [sub_mul, one_div, inv_pow, inv_mul_cancel₀ hpos.ne']; ring
2654      rw [e]; linarith
2655    refine tendsto_of_tendsto_of_tendsto_of_le_of_le ?_ tendsto_const_nhds hlow
2656      (fun m => (gridFloor_le m s).trans (le_of_eq (max_eq_left hs.le)))
2657    have : Tendsto (fun m : ℕ => s - (1 / 2 : ℝ) ^ m) atTop (𝓝 (s - 0)) :=
2658      tendsto_const_nhds.sub (tendsto_pow_atTop_nhds_zero_of_lt_one (by norm_num) (by norm_num))
2659    simpa using this
2660
2661/-- A function of `(s, ω)` that depends on `s` only through an integer-valued measurable
2662function of `s` is jointly measurable as soon as each section is measurable. -/
2663theorem measurable_comp_int_prod {α Ω β : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {m : MeasurableSpace Ω}
2664    [MeasurableSpace β] (n : α → ℤ) (hn : Measurable n) (G : ℤ → Ω → β)
2665    (hG : ∀ k, Measurable[m] (G k)) :
2666    @Measurable (α × Ω) β (@Prod.instMeasurableSpace α Ω _ m) _ (fun p => G (n p.1) p.2) := by
2667  letI : MeasurableSpace Ω := m
2668  have h1 : Measurable fun q : ℤ × Ω => G q.1 q.2 :=
2669    measurable_from_prod_countable_right (fun k => hG k)
2670  exact h1.comp ((hn.comp measurable_fst).prodMk measurable_snd)
2671
2672/-- The set `{(s, ω) : s ≤ ρ ω}` is progressively measurable for a stopping time `ρ`. -/
2673theorem measurableSet_le_stoppingTime (ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ) {ρ : Ω → WithTop ℝ}
2674    (hρ : IsStoppingTime ℱ ρ) (t : ℝ) :
2675    MeasurableSet[Subtype.instMeasurableSpace.prod (ℱ t)]
2676      {p : Set.Iic t × Ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2} := by
2677  have h := isProgressive_indicator_le_stoppingTime ℱ hρ t
2678  have : {p : Set.Iic t × Ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2} =
2679      (fun p : Set.Iic t × Ω => Set.indicator {ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω}
2680        (fun _ => (1 : ℝ)) p.2) ⁻¹' {1} := by
2681    ext p
2682    by_cases hp : ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2
2683    · simp [hp]
2684    · simp [hp]
2685  rw [this]
2686  exact h (measurableSet_singleton 1)
2687
2688/-- **Progressive measurability of a solution stopped before its blow-up time**: if `Y` is adapted,
2689`Y 0` is measurable at every time, and the paths are continuous on `[0, t]` for every
2690`t ≤ ρ` (`ρ` a stopping time), then `1_{s ≤ ρ} Y_{s ∨ 0}` is progressively measurable. -/
2691theorem isProgressive_stoppedPath (ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ) (Y : ℝ → Ω → E) (hY : Adapted ℱ Y)
2692    (hY0 : ∀ t : ℝ, Measurable[ℱ t] (Y 0)) {ρ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} (hρ : IsStoppingTime ℱ ρ)
2693    (hcont : ∀ ω (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → ContinuousOn (fun s => Y s ω) (Set.Icc 0 t)) :
2694    IsProgressive ℱ (fun s ω => Set.indicator {ω | (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω}
2695      (fun ω => Y (max s 0) ω) ω) := by
2696  intro t
2697  let M : MeasurableSpace (Set.Iic t × Ω) := Subtype.instMeasurableSpace.prod (ℱ t)
2698  have hS : MeasurableSet[M] {p : Set.Iic t × Ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2} :=
2699    measurableSet_le_stoppingTime ℱ hρ t
2700  -- approximants
2701  let Yc : ℕ → ℤ × Ω → E := fun m p =>
2702    Y (min (((max p.1 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m) (max t 0)) p.2
2703  have hYc : ∀ m : ℕ, ∀ k : ℤ, Measurable[ℱ t] (fun ω => Y (min (((max k 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m) (max t 0)) ω) := by
2704    intro m k
2705    have h0 : 0 ≤ min (((max k 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m) (max t 0) := by
2706      refine le_min ?_ (le_max_right _ _)
2707      have : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ((max k 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast le_max_right _ _
2708      positivity
2709    rcases le_or_gt 0 t with ht | ht
2710    · rw [max_eq_left ht] at *
2711      exact (hY _).mono (ℱ.mono (min_le_right _ _)) le_rfl
2712    · have : min (((max k 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m) (max t 0) = 0 := by
2713        rw [max_eq_right ht.le] at h0 ⊢; exact le_antisymm (min_le_right _ _) h0
2714      rw [this]; exact hY0 t
2715  have happ : ∀ m : ℕ, Measurable[M] (fun p : Set.Iic t × Ω =>
2716      Set.indicator {ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω}
2717        (fun ω => Y (gridFloor m p.1) ω) p.2) := by
2718    intro m
2719    have hn : Measurable (fun s : Set.Iic t => ⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * (s : ℝ)⌋) :=
2720      (Int.measurable_floor).comp ((measurable_const.mul measurable_subtype_coe))
2721    have hG := measurable_comp_int_prod (m := ℱ t) (β := E) _ hn
2722      (fun k ω => Y (min (((max k 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m) (max t 0)) ω) (hYc m)
2723    have heq : (fun p : Set.Iic t × Ω => Y (gridFloor m p.1) p.2) =
2724        fun p => Y (min (((max ⌊(2 : ℝ) ^ m * (p.1 : ℝ)⌋ 0 : ℤ) : ℝ) / 2 ^ m) (max t 0)) p.2 := by
2725      funext p
2726      congr 1
2727      have h1 : gridFloor m (p.1 : ℝ) ≤ max t 0 :=
2728        (gridFloor_le m _).trans (max_le_max p.1.2 le_rfl)
2729      exact (min_eq_left h1).symm
2730    have hfun : (fun p : Set.Iic t × Ω => Set.indicator {ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω}
2731        (fun ω => Y (gridFloor m p.1) ω) p.2) =
2732        Set.indicator {p : Set.Iic t × Ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2}
2733          (fun p => Y (gridFloor m p.1) p.2) := by
2734      funext p; simp [Set.indicator]
2735    rw [hfun]
2736    refine Measurable.indicator ?_ hS
2737    rw [heq]
2738    exact hG
2739  have hlim : Tendsto (fun m : ℕ => fun p : Set.Iic t × Ω =>
2740      Set.indicator {ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω} (fun ω => Y (gridFloor m p.1) ω) p.2)
2741      atTop (𝓝 (fun p : Set.Iic t × Ω => Set.indicator {ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω}
2742        (fun ω => Y (max (p.1 : ℝ) 0) ω) p.2)) := by
2743    rw [tendsto_pi_nhds]
2744    intro p
2745    by_cases hp : ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2
2746    · simp only [Set.indicator, Set.mem_ofPred_eq, hp, if_true]
2747      rcases le_or_gt (p.1 : ℝ) 0 with hs | hs
2748      · simp only [gridFloor_eq_zero_of_nonpos _ hs, max_eq_right hs]
2749        exact tendsto_const_nhds
2750      · have hc := hcont p.2 (p.1 : ℝ) hs.le hp
2751        rw [max_eq_left hs.le]
2752        have h1 : Tendsto (fun m : ℕ => gridFloor m (p.1 : ℝ)) atTop (𝓝[Set.Icc 0 (p.1 : ℝ)] (p.1 : ℝ)) := by
2753          refine tendsto_nhdsWithin_iff.2 ⟨by simpa [max_eq_left hs.le] using tendsto_gridFloor (p.1 : ℝ),
2754            Eventually.of_forall fun m => ⟨gridFloor_nonneg m _, ?_⟩⟩
2755          simpa [max_eq_left hs.le] using gridFloor_le m (p.1 : ℝ)
2756        exact (hc (p.1 : ℝ) ⟨hs.le, le_rfl⟩).tendsto.comp h1
2757    · simp only [Set.indicator, Set.mem_ofPred_eq, hp, if_false]
2758      exact tendsto_const_nhds
2759  exact @measurable_of_tendsto_metrizable _ _ M _ _ _ _ _ _ happ hlim
2760
2761end ProgressiveStopped
