2763section HittingTime
2764
2765/-- Hitting a closed set by a path that is continuous only on `[0,t]`: the rational criterion. -/
2766theorem exists_mem_Icc_iff_infDist {E : Type*} [PseudoMetricSpace E] {f : ℝ → E} {G : Set E}
2767    (hG : IsClosed G) (hne : G.Nonempty) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 ≤ t)
2768    (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc 0 t)) :
2769    (∃ r, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ t ∧ f r ∈ G) ↔
2770      ∀ k : ℕ, ∃ q : ℚ, Metric.infDist (f (max 0 (min t (q : ℝ)))) G < 1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1) := by
2771  have hc_mem : ∀ x : ℝ, max 0 (min t x) ∈ Set.Icc 0 t := fun x =>
2772    ⟨le_max_left _ _, max_le ht (min_le_left _ _)⟩
2773  have hc_cont : Continuous fun x : ℝ => max 0 (min t x) :=
2774    continuous_const.max (continuous_const.min continuous_id)
2775  have hfc : Continuous fun x : ℝ => f (max 0 (min t x)) :=
2776    hf.comp_continuous hc_cont hc_mem
2777  have hg : Continuous fun x : ℝ => Metric.infDist (f (max 0 (min t x))) G :=
2778    (Metric.continuous_infDist_pt G).comp hfc
2779  constructor
2780  · rintro ⟨r, hr0, hrt, hr⟩ k
2781    have hr0' : Metric.infDist (f r) G = 0 := (hG.mem_iff_infDist_zero hne).1 hr
2782    have hcr : max 0 (min t r) = r := by
2783      rw [min_eq_right hrt, max_eq_right hr0]
2784    have hev : ∀ᶠ x in 𝓝 r, Metric.infDist (f (max 0 (min t x))) G < 1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1) := by
2785      apply hg.continuousAt.eventually_lt continuousAt_const
2786      simp only [hcr, hr0']
2787      positivity
2788    obtain ⟨U, hUsub, hUopen, hrU⟩ := eventually_nhds_iff.1 hev
2789    obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := Rat.denseRange_cast.exists_mem_open hUopen ⟨r, hrU⟩
2790    exact ⟨q, hUsub _ hq⟩
2791  · intro h
2792    have hfi : ContinuousOn (fun r => Metric.infDist (f r) G) (Set.Icc 0 t) :=
2793      (Metric.continuous_infDist_pt G).comp_continuousOn hf
2794    obtain ⟨r₀, hr₀, hmin⟩ := isCompact_Icc.exists_isMinOn (Set.nonempty_Icc.2 ht) hfi
2795    refine ⟨r₀, hr₀.1, hr₀.2, ?_⟩
2796    rw [hG.mem_iff_infDist_zero hne]
2797    refine le_antisymm ?_ Metric.infDist_nonneg
2798    refine le_of_forall_pos_lt_add fun ε hε => ?_
2799    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := exists_nat_one_div_lt hε
2800    obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := h k
2801    have := hmin (hc_mem q)
2802    simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq] at this
2803    linarith
2804
2805open Classical in
2806/-- First time `≥ 0` before `τ` at which the path `y` is in `G` (`⊤` if there is none). -/
2807def pathHit {E : Type*} (τ : WithTop ℝ) (y : ℝ → E) (G : Set E) : WithTop ℝ :=
2808  if h : ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G then
2809    ((sInf {r : ℝ | 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G} : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ)
2810  else ⊤
2811
2812theorem pathHit_eq_top {E : Type*} {τ : WithTop ℝ} {y : ℝ → E} {G : Set E}
2813    (h : ¬ ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G) : pathHit τ y G = ⊤ := by
2814  unfold pathHit; rw [dif_neg h]
2815
2816/-- If the path hits `G` before `τ`, the hitting time is attained and is the first one. -/
2817theorem pathHit_spec {E : Type*} [TopologicalSpace E] {τ : WithTop ℝ} {y : ℝ → E} {G : Set E}
2818    (hG : IsClosed G) (hcont : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ → ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 t))
2819    (h : ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G) :
2820    ∃ r₀ : ℝ, pathHit τ y G = (r₀ : WithTop ℝ) ∧ 0 ≤ r₀ ∧ (r₀ : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r₀ ∈ G ∧
2821      ∀ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r → (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ → y r ∈ G → r₀ ≤ r := by
2822  set S : Set ℝ := {r : ℝ | 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G} with hS
2823  obtain ⟨r₁, hr₁0, hr₁τ, hr₁G⟩ := h
2824  have hr₁S : r₁ ∈ S := ⟨hr₁0, hr₁τ, hr₁G⟩
2825  have hbdd : BddBelow S := ⟨0, fun r hr => hr.1⟩
2826  set S₁ : Set ℝ := Set.Icc 0 r₁ ∩ y ⁻¹' G with hS₁
2827  have hS₁closed : IsClosed S₁ :=
2828    (hcont r₁ hr₁0 hr₁τ).preimage_isClosed_of_isClosed isClosed_Icc hG
2829  have hr₁S₁ : r₁ ∈ S₁ := ⟨⟨hr₁0, le_rfl⟩, hr₁G⟩
2830  have hS₁bdd : BddBelow S₁ := ⟨0, fun r hr => hr.1.1⟩
2831  have hr₀mem : sInf S₁ ∈ S₁ := hS₁closed.csInf_mem ⟨r₁, hr₁S₁⟩ hS₁bdd
2832  have hr₀le : sInf S₁ ≤ r₁ := csInf_le hS₁bdd hr₁S₁
2833  have hr₀S : sInf S₁ ∈ S := ⟨hr₀mem.1.1, lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast hr₀le) hr₁τ, hr₀mem.2⟩
2834  have hlow : ∀ r ∈ S, sInf S₁ ≤ r := by
2835    intro r hr
2836    by_cases hrr : r ≤ r₁
2837    · exact csInf_le hS₁bdd ⟨⟨hr.1, hrr⟩, hr.2.2⟩
2838    · exact hr₀le.trans (not_le.1 hrr).le
2839  have hsInf : sInf S = sInf S₁ :=
2840    le_antisymm (csInf_le hbdd hr₀S) (le_csInf ⟨r₁, hr₁S⟩ hlow)
2841  refine ⟨sInf S₁, ?_, hr₀S.1, hr₀S.2.1, hr₀S.2.2, fun r hr0 hrτ hrG => hlow r ⟨hr0, hrτ, hrG⟩⟩
2842  unfold pathHit
2843  rw [dif_pos ⟨r₁, hr₁0, hr₁τ, hr₁G⟩]
2844  rw [hsInf]
2845
2846theorem pathHit_le_iff {E : Type*} [TopologicalSpace E] {τ : WithTop ℝ} {y : ℝ → E} {G : Set E}
2847    (hG : IsClosed G) (hcont : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ → ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 t))
2848    (t : ℝ) :
2849    pathHit τ y G ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ) ↔
2850      ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ t ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G := by
2851  constructor
2852  · intro h
2853    by_cases hex : ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G
2854    · obtain ⟨r₀, h0, hr0, hr0τ, hr0G, -⟩ := pathHit_spec hG hcont hex
2855      rw [h0] at h
2856      exact ⟨r₀, hr0, by exact_mod_cast h, hr0τ, hr0G⟩
2857    · rw [pathHit_eq_top hex] at h
2858      exact absurd h (by simp)
2859  · rintro ⟨r, hr0, hrt, hrτ, hrG⟩
2860    obtain ⟨r₀, h0, -, -, -, hmin⟩ := pathHit_spec hG hcont ⟨r, hr0, hrτ, hrG⟩
2861    rw [h0]
2862    exact_mod_cast (hmin r hr0 hrτ hrG).trans hrt
2863
2864/-- Before (and at) the hitting time of `G`, a path starting in the closed set `K ⊇ Gᶜ` stays in `K`. -/
2865theorem pathHit_mem_of_le {E : Type*} [TopologicalSpace E] {τ : WithTop ℝ} {y : ℝ → E}
2866    {G K : Set E} (hG : IsClosed G) (hK : IsClosed K) (hGK : Gᶜ ⊆ K)
2867    (hcont : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ → ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 t))
2868    (h0 : y 0 ∈ K) {t : ℝ} (ht0 : 0 ≤ t) (htτ : (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ)
2869    (ht : (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ pathHit τ y G) : y t ∈ K := by
2870  rcases ht.lt_or_eq with hlt | heq
2871  · by_contra hyK
2872    have hyG : y t ∈ G := by
2873      by_contra hyG
2874      exact hyK (hGK hyG)
2875    have := (pathHit_le_iff hG hcont t).2 ⟨t, ht0, le_rfl, htτ, hyG⟩
2876    exact absurd hlt (not_lt.2 this)
2877  · have hex : ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G := by
2878      by_contra hne
2879      rw [pathHit_eq_top hne] at heq
2880      exact absurd heq (by simp)
2881    obtain ⟨r₀, h0', -, -, -, hmin⟩ := pathHit_spec hG hcont hex
2882    rw [h0'] at heq
2883    have htr : t = r₀ := by exact_mod_cast heq
2884    rcases eq_or_lt_of_le ht0 with h00 | hpos
2885    · rw [← h00]; exact h0
2886    · have hsub : ∀ s ∈ Set.Ico 0 t, y s ∈ K := by
2887        intro s hs
2888        by_contra hsK
2889        have hsG : y s ∈ G := by
2890          by_contra hsG
2891          exact hsK (hGK hsG)
2892        have hsτ : (s : WithTop ℝ) < τ := lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast hs.2.le) htτ
2893        have := hmin s hs.1 hsτ hsG
2894        linarith [hs.2]
2895      have hcl : t ∈ closure (Set.Ico 0 t) := by
2896        rw [closure_Ico hpos.ne]; exact ⟨ht0, le_rfl⟩
2897      have hcw : ContinuousWithinAt y (Set.Ico 0 t) t :=
2898        ((hcont t ht0 htτ) t ⟨ht0, le_rfl⟩).mono Set.Ico_subset_Icc_self
2899      have := hcw.mem_closure_image hcl
2900      have hsub' : y '' Set.Ico 0 t ⊆ K := by
2901        rintro _ ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩; exact hsub s hs
2902      exact (closure_minimal hsub' hK) this
2903
2904variable {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
2905  {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [MeasurableSpace E] [BorelSpace E]
2906
2907/-- The first hitting time of a closed set `G` by the pre-blow-up path of a solution. -/
2908def solHit (τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (Y : ℝ → Ω → E) (G : Set E) (ω : Ω) : WithTop ℝ :=
2909  pathHit (τ ω) (fun t => Y t ω) G
2910
2911/-- The first hitting time of a closed set by an adapted process that is continuous before the
2912stopping time `τ`, and which is forced to hit the set before `τ` when `τ < ∞`, is a stopping time. -/
2913theorem isStoppingTime_solHit (ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ) {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} (hτ : IsStoppingTime ℱ τ)
2914    {Y : ℝ → Ω → E} (hY : Adapted ℱ Y)
2915    (hcont : ∀ ω (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → ContinuousOn (fun s => Y s ω) (Set.Icc 0 t))
2916    {G : Set E} (hG : IsClosed G)
2917    (hexit : ∀ ω, τ ω < ⊤ → ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ Y r ω ∈ G) :
2918    IsStoppingTime ℱ (solHit τ Y G) := by
2919  intro t
2920  rcases G.eq_empty_or_nonempty with rfl | hne
2921  · have : {ω | solHit τ Y (∅ : Set E) ω ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)} = ∅ := by
2922      ext ω
2923      simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq, Set.mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false]
2924      intro h
2925      rw [solHit, pathHit_eq_top (by simp)] at h
2926      exact absurd h (by simp)
2927    rw [this]; exact @MeasurableSet.empty _ (ℱ t)
2928  have hset : {ω | solHit τ Y G ω ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)} =
2929      {ω | ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ t ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ Y r ω ∈ G} := by
2930    ext ω
2931    exact pathHit_le_iff hG (hcont ω) t
2932  rw [hset]
2933  by_cases ht : 0 ≤ t
2934  · have hkey : {ω | ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ t ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ Y r ω ∈ G} =
2935        {ω | τ ω ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)} ∪ ({ω | τ ω ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)}ᶜ ∩
2936          ⋂ k : ℕ, ⋃ q : ℚ, {ω | Metric.infDist (Y (max 0 (min t (q : ℝ))) ω) G < 1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1)}) := by
2937      ext ω
2938      simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq, Set.mem_union, Set.mem_inter_iff, Set.mem_compl_iff,
2939        Set.mem_iInter, Set.mem_iUnion]
2940      by_cases hτt : τ ω ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)
2941      · have hlt : τ ω < ⊤ := lt_of_le_of_lt hτt (WithTop.coe_lt_top t)
2942        obtain ⟨r, hr0, hrτ, hrG⟩ := hexit ω hlt
2943        have : r ≤ t := by
2944          have := hrτ.le.trans hτt
2945          exact_mod_cast this
2946        simp only [hτt, true_or, iff_true]
2947        exact ⟨r, hr0, this, hrτ, hrG⟩
2948      · have htτ : (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω := not_le.1 hτt
2949        have hc := hcont ω t ht htτ
2950        simp only [hτt, false_or, not_false_eq_true, true_and]
2951        rw [← exists_mem_Icc_iff_infDist hG hne ht hc]
2952        constructor
2953        · rintro ⟨r, h0, h1, -, h3⟩; exact ⟨r, h0, h1, h3⟩
2954        · rintro ⟨r, h0, h1, h3⟩
2955          exact ⟨r, h0, h1, lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast h1) htτ, h3⟩
2956    rw [hkey]
2957    refine MeasurableSet.union (hτ t) (MeasurableSet.inter (hτ t).compl ?_)
2958    refine MeasurableSet.iInter fun k => MeasurableSet.iUnion fun q => ?_
2959    have hmem : max 0 (min t (q : ℝ)) ≤ t := max_le ht (min_le_left _ _)
2960    have hm : Measurable[ℱ t] (fun ω => Metric.infDist (Y (max 0 (min t (q : ℝ))) ω) G) :=
2961      (Metric.continuous_infDist_pt G).measurable.comp
2962        ((hY _).mono (ℱ.mono hmem) le_rfl)
2963    exact hm measurableSet_Iio
2964  · have : {ω | ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ t ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ Y r ω ∈ G} = ∅ := by
2965      ext ω
2966      simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq, Set.mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false]
2967      rintro ⟨r, h0, h1, -⟩
2968      exact ht (h0.trans h1)
2969    rw [this]; exact @MeasurableSet.empty _ (ℱ t)
2970
2971end HittingTime

2975/-- A time-independent function which is `C²` on an open set is `C^{1,2}` there. -/
2976theorem isC12On_of_contDiffOn {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] (T : ℝ)
2977    {U : Set E} (hU : IsOpen U) {g : E → ℝ} (hg : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 g U) :
2978    IsC12On T U (fun _ x => g x) := by
2979  have hg1 : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ g) U := hg.fderiv_of_isOpen hU (m := 1) (by norm_num)
2980  have hca : ∀ x ∈ U, ContDiffAt ℝ 2 g x := fun x hx => hg.contDiffAt (hU.mem_nhds hx)
2981  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
2982  · exact hg.continuousOn.comp continuousOn_snd (fun p hp => hp.2)
2983  · intro t _ x hx
2984    exact ((hca x hx).differentiableAt (by norm_num)).hasFDerivAt
2985  · intro t _ x hx
2986    have : ContDiffAt ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ g) x := (hca x hx).fderiv_right (m := 1) (by norm_num)
2987    exact (this.differentiableAt (by norm_num)).hasFDerivAt
2988  · exact (hg.continuousOn_fderiv_of_isOpen hU (by norm_num)).comp continuousOn_snd
2989      (fun p hp => hp.2)
2990  · exact (hg1.continuousOn_fderiv_of_isOpen hU le_rfl).comp continuousOn_snd (fun p hp => hp.2)
2991  · intro x _ t _
2992    have h0 : HasDerivWithinAt (fun _ : ℝ => g x) 0 (Set.Icc 0 T) t := hasDerivWithinAt_const t _ _
2993    have : derivWithin (fun _ : ℝ => g x) (Set.Icc 0 T) t = 0 := by simp
2994    rw [this]; exact h0
2995  · have : (fun p : ℝ × E => derivWithin (fun r => (fun (_ : ℝ) (x : E) => g x) r p.2)
2996        (Set.Icc 0 T) p.1) = fun _ => 0 := by
2997      funext p; simp
2998    rw [this]
2999    exact continuousOn_const
3000
3001end Ito

3005variable {d N : ℕ}

3007/-- The pair separation `ψ(x_i - x_j)` on the covering space. -/
3008def pairSep (Z : Fin N → Euc d) (i j : Fin N) : ℝ := Torus.lift (pairDispersion (d := d)) (Z i - Z j)
3009
3010theorem continuous_pairSep (i j : Fin N) : Continuous fun Z : Fin N → Euc d => pairSep Z i j := by
3011  have h : ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift (pairDispersion (d := d))) := by
3012    have := pairDispersion_smooth (d := d)
3013    unfold Torus.IsSmooth Torus.IsCk at this
3014    exact this
3015  exact h.continuous.comp ((continuous_apply i).sub (continuous_apply j))
3016
3017theorem pairSep_pos {Z : Fin N → Euc d} (hZ : Z ∈ liftedFree d N) {i j : Fin N} (hij : i ≠ j) :
3018    0 < pairSep Z i j := by
3019  rcases (pairDispersion_nonneg (Torus.mk (Z i - Z j))).lt_or_eq with h | h
3020  · exact h
3021  · exfalso
3022    have h0 := (pairDispersion_eq_zero_iff _).1 h.symm
3023    rw [mk_sub', sub_eq_zero] at h0
3024    exact hZ ⟨i, j, hij, h0⟩
3025
3026theorem exists_pairSep_gt {Z : Fin N → Euc d} (hZ : Z ∈ liftedFree d N) :
3027    ∃ n : ℕ, ∀ i j, i ≠ j → 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) < pairSep Z i j := by
3028  have : ∃ c : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ i j, i ≠ j → c ≤ pairSep Z i j := by
3029    classical
3030    by_cases hN : (Finset.univ.filter fun p : Fin N × Fin N => p.1 ≠ p.2).Nonempty
3031    · obtain ⟨p, hp, hmin⟩ := Finset.exists_min_image _ (fun p : Fin N × Fin N => pairSep Z p.1 p.2) hN
3032      refine ⟨pairSep Z p.1 p.2, pairSep_pos hZ (Finset.mem_filter.1 hp).2, fun i j hij => ?_⟩
3033      exact hmin (i, j) (Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, hij⟩)
3034    · refine ⟨1, one_pos, fun i j hij => ?_⟩
3035      exact absurd ⟨(i, j), Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, hij⟩⟩ hN
3036  obtain ⟨c, hc, hcle⟩ := this
3037  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := exists_nat_one_div_lt hc
3038  exact ⟨n, fun i j hij => lt_of_lt_of_le hn (hcle i j hij)⟩
3039
3040/-- The compact set `K_n` on which the barrier argument is localised. -/
3041def barrierK (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) : Set (Fin N → Euc d) :=
3042  Metric.closedBall 0 ((n : ℝ) + 1) ∩ {Z | ∀ i j, i ≠ j → 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) ≤ pairSep Z i j} ∩
3043    {Z | a ≤ clusterDispersionLift I Z} ∩
3044    {Z | ∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z}
3045
3046/-- The closed set whose first hitting time stops the barrier argument. -/
3047def barrierG (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) : Set (Fin N → Euc d) :=
3048  {Z | clusterDispersionLift I Z ≤ a} ∪ {Z | ∃ j, j ∉ I ∧ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z ≤ δ} ∪
3049    {Z | (n : ℝ) + 1 ≤ ‖Z‖} ∪ {Z | ∃ i j, i ≠ j ∧ pairSep Z i j ≤ 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1)}
3050
3051theorem continuous_clusterDispersionLift (I : Finset (Fin N)) :
3052    Continuous (clusterDispersionLift (d := d) I) :=
3053  (contDiff_two_clusterDispersionLift I).continuous
3054
3055theorem isClosed_barrierK (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
3056    IsClosed (barrierK (d := d) I a δ n) := by
3057  unfold barrierK
3058  refine ((Metric.isClosed_closedBall.inter ?_).inter (isClosed_le continuous_const
3059    (continuous_clusterDispersionLift I))).inter ?_
3060  swap
3061  · have : {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z} =
3062        ⋂ j, ⋂ (_ : j ∉ I), {Z | δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z} := by
3063      ext Z; simp
3064    rw [this]
3065    exact isClosed_iInter fun j => isClosed_iInter fun _ =>
3066      isClosed_le continuous_const (continuous_clusterDispersionLift _)
3067  have : {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∀ i j, i ≠ j → 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) ≤ pairSep Z i j} =
3068      ⋂ i, ⋂ j, ⋂ (_ : i ≠ j), {Z | 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) ≤ pairSep Z i j} := by
3069    ext Z; simp
3070  rw [this]
3071  exact isClosed_iInter fun i => isClosed_iInter fun j => isClosed_iInter fun _ =>
3072    isClosed_le continuous_const (continuous_pairSep i j)
3073
3074theorem isCompact_barrierK (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
3075    IsCompact (barrierK (d := d) I a δ n) :=
3076  (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Fin N → Euc d) ((n : ℝ) + 1)).of_isClosed_subset
3077    (isClosed_barrierK I a δ n)
3078    (Set.inter_subset_left.trans (Set.inter_subset_left.trans Set.inter_subset_left))
3079
3080theorem isClosed_barrierG (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
3081    IsClosed (barrierG (d := d) I a δ n) := by
3082  unfold barrierG
3083  refine ((( isClosed_le (continuous_clusterDispersionLift I) continuous_const).union ?_).union
3084    (isClosed_le continuous_const continuous_norm)).union ?_
3085  · have : {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∃ j, j ∉ I ∧ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z ≤ δ} =
3086        ⋃ j, ⋃ (_ : j ∉ I), {Z | clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z ≤ δ} := by
3087      ext Z; simp
3088    rw [this]
3089    exact isClosed_iUnion_of_finite fun j => isClosed_iUnion_of_finite fun _ =>
3090      isClosed_le (continuous_clusterDispersionLift _) continuous_const
3091  · have : {Z : Fin N → Euc d | ∃ i j, i ≠ j ∧ pairSep Z i j ≤ 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1)} =
3092        ⋃ i, ⋃ j, ⋃ (_ : i ≠ j), {Z | pairSep Z i j ≤ 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1)} := by
3093      ext Z; simp
3094    rw [this]
3095    exact isClosed_iUnion_of_finite fun i => isClosed_iUnion_of_finite fun j =>
3096      isClosed_iUnion_of_finite fun _ => isClosed_le (continuous_pairSep i j) continuous_const
3097
3098theorem barrierG_compl_subset (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
3099    (barrierG (d := d) I a δ n)ᶜ ⊆ barrierK I a δ n := by
3100  intro Z hZ
3101  simp only [barrierG, Set.mem_compl_iff, Set.mem_union, Set.mem_ofPred_eq, not_or,
3102    not_exists, not_le, not_and] at hZ
3103  obtain ⟨⟨⟨h1, h2⟩, h3⟩, h4⟩ := hZ
3104  refine ⟨⟨⟨?_, fun i j hij => (h4 i j hij).le⟩, h1.le⟩, fun j hj => (h2 j hj).le⟩
3105  rw [mem_closedBall_zero_iff]; exact h3.le
3106
3107theorem barrierK_subset_free (I : Finset (Fin N)) (a δ : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
3108    barrierK (d := d) I a δ n ⊆ liftedFree d N := by
3109  intro Z hZ hcoll
3110  obtain ⟨i, j, hij, h⟩ := hcoll
3111  have h1 := hZ.1.1.2 i j hij
3112  have h2 : pairSep Z i j = 0 := by
3113    unfold pairSep Torus.lift
3114    simp only [Function.comp, mk_sub', h, sub_self]
3115    exact (pairDispersion_eq_zero_iff 0).2 rfl
3116  rw [h2] at h1
3117  have : (0 : ℝ) < 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) := by positivity
3118  linarith
3119

3130theorem progressive_measurable_min {β : Type*} [MeasurableSpace β] {ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ}
3131    {u : ℝ → Ω → β} (hu : IsProgressive ℱ u) (T : ℝ) :
3132    Measurable fun x : ℝ × Ω => u (min x.1 T) x.2 := by
3133  have hq : Measurable[Prod.instMeasurableSpace, Subtype.instMeasurableSpace.prod (ℱ T)]
3134      fun x : ℝ × Ω => ((⟨min x.1 T, Set.mem_Iic.2 (min_le_right _ _)⟩ : Set.Iic T), x.2) :=
3135    Measurable.prodMk (Measurable.subtype_mk (measurable_fst.min measurable_const))
3136      (measurable_snd.mono le_rfl (ℱ.le T))
3137  exact (hu T).comp hq

3139/-- Sections of a progressively measurable process are a.e. strongly measurable on `[0, T]`. -/
3140theorem progressive_aestronglyMeasurable_Icc {β : Type*} [MeasurableSpace β]
3141    [TopologicalSpace β] [TopologicalSpace.PseudoMetrizableSpace β] [BorelSpace β] [SecondCountableTopology β]
3142    {ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ} {u : ℝ → Ω → β} (hu : IsProgressive ℱ u) (T : ℝ) (ω : Ω) :
3143    AEStronglyMeasurable (fun s => u s ω) (volume.restrict (Set.Icc 0 T)) := by
3144  have h1 : Measurable fun s : ℝ => u (min s T) ω :=
3145    (progressive_measurable_min hu T).comp (measurable_id.prodMk measurable_const)
3146  refine h1.aestronglyMeasurable.congr ?_
3147  filter_upwards [ae_restrict_mem measurableSet_Icc] with s hs
3148  simp [min_eq_left hs.2]

3154variable {d N : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}

3157/-- The drift of the lifted SDE, cut off outside a compact subset of the collision-free region
3158and stopped at `ρ` and `T`: the progressive drift process used in Itô's formula. -/
3159def stoppedDrift (D : DynData d) (K : Set (Fin N → Euc d)) (T : ℝ) (ρ : Ω → WithTop ℝ)
3160    (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d) :=
3161  fun s ω => open Classical in
3162    if (0 ≤ s ∧ s ≤ T ∧ (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω) then
3163      K.piecewise (liftedDrift D) (fun _ => 0)
3164        (Set.indicator {ω | (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω} (fun ω => Y (max s 0) ω) ω)
3165    else 0
3166
3167theorem ito_along_solution [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
3168    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
3169    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3170    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) {T : ℝ} (hT : 0 ≤ T)
3171    (ρ : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (hρ : IsStoppingTime ℱ ρ) (hρτ : ∀ ω, ρ ω < τ ω)
3172    {U : Set (Fin N → Euc d)} (hU : IsOpen U) (Kc : Set (Fin N → Euc d)) (hKc : IsCompact Kc)
3173    (hKU : Kc ⊆ U) (hKfree : Kc ⊆ liftedFree d N) {g : (Fin N → Euc d) → ℝ}
3174    (hg : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 g U)
3175    (hYK : ∀ ω, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → Y t ω ∈ Kc) :
3176    ∃ Ψ : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ, IsProgressiveL2 P ℱ Ψ ∧
3177      (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ s : ℝ, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ T → (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → ∀ k,
3178        Ψ s ω k = fderiv ℝ g (Y s ω) (liftedNoise D k)) ∧
3179      ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω →
3180        IntegrableOn (fun s => diffGen D g (Y s ω)) (Set.Icc 0 t) ∧
3181        g (Y t ω) = g (Y₀ ω) + (∫ s in Set.Icc 0 t, diffGen D g (Y s ω)) +
3182          localItoIntegral P ℱ W' (stopIntegrand Ψ ρ) t ω := by
3183  classical
3184  have hY0 : ∀ t : ℝ, Measurable[ℱ t] (Y 0) := by
3185    have : Y 0 = Y₀ := funext hsol.sol.init
3186    rw [this]; exact hsol.init_meas
3187  have hcont : ∀ ω (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω →
3188      ContinuousOn (fun s => Y s ω) (Set.Icc 0 t) := fun ω t ht h =>
3189    hsol.sol.cont ω t ht (lt_of_le_of_lt h (hρτ ω))
3190  have hVprog := isProgressive_stoppedPath ℱ Y hsol.sol.adapted hY0 hρ hcont
3191  have hlip := liftedDrift_locLipschitz (N := N) D hK.smoothOffZero hV
3192  have hFc : ContinuousOn (liftedDrift D) (liftedFree d N) := by
3193    have h1 : ContinuousOn (Function.uncurry fun (_ : ℝ) (Z : Fin N → Euc d) => liftedDrift D Z)
3194        (Set.Ici 0 ×ˢ liftedFree d N) := hlip.cont
3195    have h2 : ContinuousOn (fun z : Fin N → Euc d => ((0 : ℝ), z)) (liftedFree d N) :=
3196      (continuousOn_const.prodMk continuousOn_id)
3197    have h3 : Set.MapsTo (fun z : Fin N → Euc d => ((0 : ℝ), z)) (liftedFree d N)
3198        (Set.Ici 0 ×ˢ liftedFree d N) := fun z hz => ⟨Set.mem_Ici.2 le_rfl, hz⟩
3199    have h4 := ContinuousOn.comp h1 h2 h3
3200    exact h4
3201  have hFKm : Measurable (Kc.piecewise (liftedDrift D) (fun _ => (0 : Fin N → Euc d))) :=
3202    ContinuousOn.measurable_piecewise (hFc.mono hKfree) continuousOn_const
3203      hKc.isClosed.measurableSet
3204  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := hKc.exists_bound_of_continuousOn (hFc.mono hKfree)
3205  set b := stoppedDrift D Kc T ρ Y with hbdef
3206  have hb_apply : ∀ ω s, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ T → (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω →
3207      b s ω = liftedDrift D (Y s ω) := by
3208    intro ω s hs0 hsT hsρ
3209    have hmem : Y s ω ∈ Kc := hYK ω s ⟨hs0, hsT⟩ hsρ
3210    simp only [hbdef, stoppedDrift, hs0, hsT, hsρ, and_self, ite_true]
3211    rw [Set.indicator_of_mem (show ω ∈ {ω | (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω} from hsρ), max_eq_left hs0,
3212      Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ hmem]
3213  have hb_bound : ∀ ω s, ‖b s ω‖ ≤ max B 0 := by
3214    intro ω s
3215    simp only [hbdef, stoppedDrift]
3216    split_ifs with hc
3217    · by_cases hmem : Set.indicator {ω | (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω} (fun ω => Y (max s 0) ω) ω ∈ Kc
3218      · rw [Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ hmem]
3219        exact (hB _ hmem).trans (le_max_left _ _)
3220      · rw [Set.piecewise_eq_of_notMem _ _ _ hmem]
3221        simp
3222    · simp
3223  have hb_prog : IsProgressive ℱ b := by
3224    intro i
3225    let M : MeasurableSpace (Set.Iic i × Ω) := Subtype.instMeasurableSpace.prod (ℱ i)
3226    have hS : MeasurableSet[M] {p : Set.Iic i × Ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2} :=
3227      measurableSet_le_stoppingTime ℱ hρ i
3228    have hc : MeasurableSet[M] {p : Set.Iic i × Ω |
3229        0 ≤ (p.1 : ℝ) ∧ (p.1 : ℝ) ≤ T ∧ ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ p.2} := by
3230      have h1 : MeasurableSet[M] {p : Set.Iic i × Ω | 0 ≤ (p.1 : ℝ)} :=
3231        measurableSet_le measurable_const (measurable_subtype_coe.comp measurable_fst)
3232      have h2 : MeasurableSet[M] {p : Set.Iic i × Ω | (p.1 : ℝ) ≤ T} :=
3233        measurableSet_le (measurable_subtype_coe.comp measurable_fst) measurable_const
3234      exact h1.inter (h2.inter hS)
3235    have hm : Measurable[M] (fun p : Set.Iic i × Ω =>
3236        Kc.piecewise (liftedDrift D) (fun _ => (0 : Fin N → Euc d))
3237          (Set.indicator {ω | ((p.1 : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω} (fun ω => Y (max (p.1 : ℝ) 0) ω) p.2)) :=
3238      hFKm.comp (hVprog i)
3239    exact Measurable.ite hc hm measurable_const
3240  have hb_L1 : IsProgressiveLocL1 P ℱ b := by
3241    refine ⟨hb_prog, fun T' => Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_⟩
3242    exact Integrable.of_bound (C := max B 0) (progressive_aestronglyMeasurable_Icc hb_prog T' ω)
3243      (Eventually.of_forall fun s => hb_bound ω s)
3244  -- the constant noise integrand
3245  have hΦ : IsProgressiveLocL2E P ℱ (fun (_ : ℝ) (_ : Ω) => liftedNoise (N := N) D) := by
3246    refine ⟨isProgressive_const ℱ (liftedNoise (N := N) D), fun T' => Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_⟩
3247    simp only [lintegral_const, Measure.restrict_apply MeasurableSet.univ, Set.univ_inter]
3248    refine ENNReal.mul_lt_top ?_ (by simp)
3249    exact ENNReal.sum_lt_top.2 fun k _ => ENNReal.pow_lt_top (enorm_lt_top)
3250  set X := itoProcess P ℱ W' Y₀ b (fun (_ : ℝ) (_ : Ω) => liftedNoise (N := N) D) with hXdef
3251  have hXY : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → X t ω = Y t ω := by
3252    filter_upwards [localItoIntegralE_const hW' (liftedNoise (N := N) D), hsol.sol.eq] with ω h1 h2 t ht htρ
3253    have htτ : (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω := lt_of_le_of_lt htρ (hρτ ω)
3254    have h3 := (h2 t ht.1 htτ).2
3255    have h4 : (∫ s in Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t, b s ω) = ∫ s in Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t, liftedDrift D (Y s ω) :=
3256      setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Icc fun s hs =>
3257        hb_apply ω s hs.1 (hs.2.trans ht.2) (le_trans (by exact_mod_cast hs.2) htρ)
3258    simp only [hXdef, itoProcess]
3259    rw [h1 t, h4, h3]
3260  have hXK : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → X t ω ∈ Kc := by
3261    filter_upwards [hXY] with ω h t ht htρ
3262    rw [h t ht htρ]; exact hYK ω t ht htρ
3263  have hf := isC12On_of_contDiffOn T hU hg
3264  have hmain := ito_formula_localized hW' hT U hU Kc hKc hKU (fun _ x => g x) hf Y₀
3265    hsol.init_meas b hb_L1 (fun (_ : ℝ) (_ : Ω) => liftedNoise (N := N) D) hΦ ρ hρ hXK
3266  have hXad : Adapted ℱ X := adapted_itoProcess Y₀ hsol.init_meas b hb_prog _
3267  have hXc : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Continuous fun t => X t ω := continuous_itoProcess Y₀ b hb_L1 _
3268  have hprogLoc := isProgressiveLocL2_itoIntegrand Kc hKc.isClosed U hKU
3269    (fun _ x => g x) hf X (isProgressive_itoProcess Y₀ hsol.init_meas b hb_prog _) hXc _ hΦ
3270  -- boundedness of the martingale integrand
3271  have hdg : ContinuousOn (fderiv ℝ g) Kc :=
3272    (hg.continuousOn_fderiv_of_isOpen hU (by norm_num)).mono hKU
3273  obtain ⟨C₁, hC₁⟩ := hKc.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hdg
3274  set C₂ : ℝ := ∑ k, ‖liftedNoise (N := N) D k‖ with hC₂
3275  have hΨbdd : ∀ s ω k, |itoIntegrand T Kc (fun _ x => g x) X (fun (_ : ℝ) (_ : Ω) => liftedNoise (N := N) D) s ω k|
3276      ≤ max C₁ 0 * C₂ := by
3277    intro s ω k
3278    simp only [itoIntegrand, Set.indicator]
3279    split_ifs with h1 h2
3280    · rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
3281      calc ‖fderiv ℝ g (X s ω) (liftedNoise D k)‖
3282          ≤ ‖fderiv ℝ g (X s ω)‖ * ‖liftedNoise D k‖ := ContinuousLinearMap.le_opNorm _ _
3283        _ ≤ max C₁ 0 * C₂ := by
3284          apply mul_le_mul ((hC₁ _ h2).trans (le_max_left _ _))
3285            (Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun k => ‖liftedNoise (N := N) D k‖) (fun _ _ => norm_nonneg _)
3286              (Finset.mem_univ k)) (norm_nonneg _) (le_max_right _ _)
3287    · simp [hC₂]; positivity
3288    · simp [hC₂]; positivity
3289  have hΨ2 : IsProgressiveL2 P ℱ
3290      (itoIntegrand T Kc (fun _ x => g x) X (fun (_ : ℝ) (_ : Ω) => liftedNoise (N := N) D)) :=
3291    IsProgressiveL2.of_bounded P hprogLoc.prog _ hΨbdd
3292  refine ⟨_, hΨ2, ?_, ?_⟩
3293  · filter_upwards [hXY] with ω h s hs0 hsT hsρ k
3294    have hXs := h s ⟨hs0, hsT⟩ hsρ
3295    simp only [itoIntegrand, Set.indicator, Set.mem_Icc, Set.mem_ofPred_eq, hs0, hsT, and_self,
3296      ite_true, hXs, hYK ω s ⟨hs0, hsT⟩ hsρ]
3297  · filter_upwards [hmain, hXY] with ω h1 h2 t ht htρ
3298    obtain ⟨hint, heq⟩ := h1 t ht htρ
3299    have hdrift : ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t,
3300        itoDrift T (fun _ x => g x) X b (fun (_ : ℝ) (_ : Ω) => liftedNoise (N := N) D) s ω =

3302      intro s hs

3305      have hXs := h2 s ⟨hs.1, hsT⟩ hsρ

3309    rw [setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Icc hdrift] at heq
3310    have hX0 : X 0 ω = Y₀ ω := by
3311      simp [hXdef, itoProcess_zero]
3312    rw [← h2 t ht htρ]
3313    simpa [hX0] using heq

3320variable {d N : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}

3323theorem pathHit_nonneg {E : Type*} [TopologicalSpace E] {τ : WithTop ℝ} {y : ℝ → E} {G : Set E}
3324    (hG : IsClosed G)
3325    (hcont : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ → ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 t)) :
3326    (0 : WithTop ℝ) ≤ pathHit τ y G := by
3327  by_cases hex : ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ y r ∈ G
3328  · obtain ⟨r₀, h0, hr0, -⟩ := pathHit_spec hG hcont hex
3329    rw [h0]; exact_mod_cast hr0
3330  · rw [pathHit_eq_top hex]; exact le_top
3331

3333
3334theorem untopA_coe' (r : ℝ) : (r : WithTop ℝ).untopA = r := by
3335  rw [WithTop.untopA_eq_untop WithTop.coe_ne_top]; simp
3336
3337theorem stopped_estimate [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
3338    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
3339    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3340    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) {T : ℝ} (hT : 0 ≤ T)
3341    {U : Set (Fin N → Euc d)} (hU : IsOpen U) (Kc : Set (Fin N → Euc d)) (hKc : IsCompact Kc)
3342    (hKU : Kc ⊆ U) (hKfree : Kc ⊆ liftedFree d N) {g : (Fin N → Euc d) → ℝ}
3343    (hg : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 g U) {G : Set (Fin N → Euc d)} (hG : IsClosed G) (hGK : Gᶜ ⊆ Kc)
3344    {C' : ℝ} (hC' : 0 ≤ C') (hgen : ∀ Z ∈ Kc, diffGen D g Z ≤ C')
3345    {A : Set Ω} (hA : ∀ t : ℝ, MeasurableSet[ℱ t] A) (hAK : ∀ ω ∈ A, Y₀ ω ∈ Kc) :
3346    ∃ Z : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d), Measurable Z ∧ (∀ ω ∈ A, Z ω ∈ Kc) ∧
3347      (∀ ω ∈ A, ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → t ≤ T → solHit τ Y G ω = (t : WithTop ℝ) → Z ω = Y t ω) ∧
3348      IntegrableOn (fun ω => g (Z ω)) A P ∧ IntegrableOn (fun ω => g (Y₀ ω)) A P ∧
3349      ∫ ω in A, g (Z ω) ∂P ≤ ∫ ω in A, g (Y₀ ω) ∂P + C' * T * (P A).toReal := by
3350  classical
3351  have hY0 : ∀ t : ℝ, Measurable[ℱ t] (Y 0) := by
3352    have : Y 0 = Y₀ := funext hsol.sol.init
3353    rw [this]; exact hsol.init_meas
3354  have hcontY := hsol.sol.cont
3355  have hexit : ∀ ω, τ ω < ⊤ → ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ (r : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧ Y r ω ∈ G := by
3356    intro ω hω
3357    obtain ⟨t, ht0, htτ, htK⟩ := hsol.exit ω hω Kc hKc hKfree 0 (by simpa using hsol.pos ω)
3358    exact ⟨t, ht0.le, htτ, by_contra fun h => htK (hGK h)⟩
3359  have hρ₀ : IsStoppingTime ℱ (solHit τ Y G) :=
3360    isStoppingTime_solHit ℱ hsol.stopping hsol.sol.adapted hcontY hG hexit
3361  have hcontP : ∀ ω (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω →
3362      ContinuousOn (fun s => Y s ω) (Set.Icc 0 t) := hcontY
3363  have hρ₀nn : ∀ ω, (0 : WithTop ℝ) ≤ solHit τ Y G ω := fun ω =>
3364    pathHit_nonneg hG (hcontP ω)
3365  -- the stopping time
3366  set ρ' : Ω → WithTop ℝ := fun ω =>
3367    if ω ∈ A then min (solHit τ Y G ω) (T : WithTop ℝ) else (((-1 : ℝ)) : WithTop ℝ) with hρ'def
3368  have hρ'A : ∀ ω ∈ A, ρ' ω = min (solHit τ Y G ω) (T : WithTop ℝ) := fun ω hω => by
3369    simp [hρ'def, hω]
3370  have hρ'nA : ∀ ω, ω ∉ A → ρ' ω = (((-1 : ℝ)) : WithTop ℝ) := fun ω hω => by
3371    simp [hρ'def, hω]
3372  have hρ' : IsStoppingTime ℱ ρ' := by
3373    intro t
3374    have : {ω | ρ' ω ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)} =
3375        (A ∩ {ω | min (solHit τ Y G ω) (T : WithTop ℝ) ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)}) ∪
3376          (Aᶜ ∩ {_ω | (((-1 : ℝ)) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ (t : WithTop ℝ)}) := by
3377      ext ω
3378      by_cases hω : ω ∈ A
3379      · simp [hρ'A ω hω, hω]
3380      · simp [hρ'nA ω hω, hω]
3381    rw [this]
3382    exact ((hA t).inter ((hρ₀.min_const T) t)).union ((hA t).compl.inter (MeasurableSet.const _))
3383  have hρ'T : ∀ ω, ρ' ω ≤ (T : WithTop ℝ) := by
3384    intro ω
3385    by_cases hω : ω ∈ A
3386    · rw [hρ'A ω hω]; exact min_le_right _ _
3387    · rw [hρ'nA ω hω]; exact_mod_cast (by linarith : (-1 : ℝ) ≤ T)
3388  have hρ'τ : ∀ ω, ρ' ω < τ ω := by
3389    intro ω
3390    by_cases hω : ω ∈ A
3391    · rw [hρ'A ω hω]
3392      by_cases hτ : τ ω = ⊤
3393      · rw [hτ]; exact lt_of_le_of_lt (min_le_right _ _) (WithTop.coe_lt_top T)
3394      · have hlt : τ ω < ⊤ := lt_top_iff_ne_top.2 hτ
3395        obtain ⟨r, hr0, hrτ, hrG⟩ := hexit ω hlt
3396        have := (pathHit_le_iff hG (hcontP ω) r).2 ⟨r, hr0, le_rfl, hrτ, hrG⟩
3397        exact lt_of_le_of_lt (min_le_left _ _) (lt_of_le_of_lt this hrτ)
3398    · rw [hρ'nA ω hω]
3399      exact lt_of_lt_of_le (by exact_mod_cast (by norm_num : (-1 : ℝ) < 0)) (hsol.pos ω).le
3400  have hρ'nn : ∀ ω ∈ A, (0 : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ' ω := fun ω hω => by
3401    rw [hρ'A ω hω]; exact le_min (hρ₀nn ω) (by exact_mod_cast hT)
3402  have hYK : ∀ ω, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ' ω → Y t ω ∈ Kc := by
3403    intro ω t ht htρ
3404    by_cases hω : ω ∈ A
3405    · have htτ : (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω := lt_of_le_of_lt htρ (hρ'τ ω)
3406      rw [hρ'A ω hω] at htρ
3407      have h0 : (fun s => Y s ω) 0 ∈ Kc := by
3408        simp only [hsol.sol.init ω]; exact hAK ω hω
3409      exact pathHit_mem_of_le hG hKc.isClosed hGK (hcontP ω) h0 ht.1
3410        htτ (htρ.trans (min_le_left _ _))
3411    · rw [hρ'nA ω hω] at htρ
3412      exfalso
3413      have : t ≤ -1 := by exact_mod_cast htρ
3414      linarith [ht.1]
3415  obtain ⟨Ψ, hΨ, -, hmain⟩ := ito_along_solution D hK hV hW' hsol hT ρ' hρ' hρ'τ hU Kc hKc hKU
3416    hKfree hg hYK
3417  have hprog := isProgressive_stoppedPath ℱ Y hsol.sol.adapted hY0 hρ'
3418    (fun ω t ht htρ => hcontP ω t ht (lt_of_le_of_lt htρ (hρ'τ ω)))
3419  set Wp : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d) := fun s ω =>
3420    Set.indicator {ω | (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ' ω} (fun ω => Y (max s 0) ω) ω with hWp
3421  have hZmeasT : StronglyMeasurable[ℱ T] (stoppedValue Wp ρ') :=
3422    stronglyMeasurable_stoppedValue_of_le hprog.isStronglyProgressive hρ' hρ'T
3423  have hZm : Measurable (stoppedValue Wp ρ') := hZmeasT.measurable.mono (ℱ.le T) le_rfl
3424  have hZ : ∀ ω ∈ A, ∃ r : ℝ, ρ' ω = (r : WithTop ℝ) ∧ 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ T ∧
3425      stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω = Y r ω := by
3426    intro ω hω
3427    have hne : ρ' ω ≠ ⊤ := ne_top_of_le_ne_top (WithTop.coe_ne_top) (hρ'T ω)
3428    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := WithTop.ne_top_iff_exists.1 hne
3429    have hr' : ρ' ω = (r : WithTop ℝ) := hr.symm
3430    have hr0 : 0 ≤ r := by have := hρ'nn ω hω; rw [hr'] at this; exact_mod_cast this
3431    have hrT : r ≤ T := by have := hρ'T ω; rw [hr'] at this; exact_mod_cast this
3432    refine ⟨r, hr', hr0, hrT, ?_⟩
3433    simp only [stoppedValue, hr', hWp, untopA_coe']
3434    rw [Set.indicator_of_mem (show ω ∈ {ω | ((r : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ' ω} from by
3435      simp [hr']), max_eq_left hr0]
3436  have hZK : ∀ ω ∈ A, stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω ∈ Kc := by
3437    intro ω hω
3438    obtain ⟨r, hr, hr0, hrT, hZr⟩ := hZ ω hω
3439    rw [hZr]; exact hYK ω r ⟨hr0, hrT⟩ (by rw [hr])
3440  refine ⟨stoppedValue Wp ρ', hZm, hZK, ?_, ?_⟩
3441  · intro ω hω t ht0 htT hhit
3442    obtain ⟨r, hr, hr0, hrT, hZr⟩ := hZ ω hω
3443    rw [hρ'A ω hω, hhit, min_eq_left (by exact_mod_cast htT)] at hr
3444    have : t = r := by exact_mod_cast hr
3445    rw [hZr, this]
3446  have hAm : MeasurableSet A := ℱ.le 0 _ (hA 0)
3447  set u := itoIntegral P ℱ W' Ψ with hu
3448  have hmart := martingale_stoppedProcess_itoIntegral hW' hΨ hρ'
3449  have hΨs : IsProgressiveL2 P ℱ (stopIntegrand Ψ ρ') := hΨ.stopIntegrand hρ'
3450  have hloc := localItoIntegral_eq_itoIntegral hW' hΨs
3451  have hst := itoIntegral_stopIntegrand hW' hΨ hρ'
3452  have hae : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ω ∈ A → g (stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω) ≤
3453      g (Y₀ ω) + C' * T + stoppedProcess u ρ' T ω := by
3454    filter_upwards [hmain, hloc, hst] with ω h1 h2 h3 hω
3455    obtain ⟨r, hr, hr0, hrT, hZr⟩ := hZ ω hω
3456    obtain ⟨hint, heq⟩ := h1 r ⟨hr0, hrT⟩ (by rw [hr])
3457    have hM : localItoIntegral P ℱ W' (stopIntegrand Ψ ρ') r ω = stoppedProcess u ρ' T ω := by
3458      rw [h2 r, h3 r]
3459      simp only [stoppedProcess, hr, min_self]
3460      rw [min_eq_right (by exact_mod_cast hrT : (r : WithTop ℝ) ≤ T)]
3461    have hbd : ∫ s in Set.Icc 0 r, diffGen D g (Y s ω) ≤ C' * r := by
3462      have := setIntegral_mono_on hint (integrableOn_const (by simp)) measurableSet_Icc
3463        (fun s hs => hgen _ (hYK ω s ⟨hs.1, hs.2.trans hrT⟩ (by rw [hr]; exact_mod_cast hs.2)))
3464      simpa [Measure.real, hr0, mul_comm] using this
3465    rw [hZr, heq, hM]
3466    have : C' * r ≤ C' * T := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hrT hC'
3467    linarith
3468  have hgc : ContinuousOn g Kc := hg.continuousOn.mono hKU
3469  set gm : (Fin N → Euc d) → ℝ := Kc.piecewise g (fun _ => 0) with hgm
3470  have hgm_meas : Measurable gm :=
3471    ContinuousOn.measurable_piecewise hgc continuousOn_const hKc.isClosed.measurableSet
3472  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := hKc.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hgc
3473  have hgm_bd : ∀ x, ‖gm x‖ ≤ max B 0 := by
3474    intro x
3475    by_cases hx : x ∈ Kc
3476    · rw [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ hx]; exact (hB x hx).trans (le_max_left _ _)
3477    · rw [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_notMem _ _ _ hx]; simp
3478  have hY₀m : Measurable Y₀ := (hsol.init_meas 0).mono (ℱ.le 0) le_rfl
3479  have hint1 : Integrable (fun ω => gm (stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω)) P :=
3480    Integrable.of_bound (hgm_meas.comp hZm).aestronglyMeasurable (max B 0)
3481      (ae_of_all _ fun ω => hgm_bd _)
3482  have hint0 : Integrable (fun ω => gm (Y₀ ω)) P :=
3483    Integrable.of_bound (hgm_meas.comp hY₀m).aestronglyMeasurable (max B 0)
3484      (ae_of_all _ fun ω => hgm_bd _)
3485  have hintM : Integrable (stoppedProcess u ρ' T) P := hmart.integrable T
3486  have h1 : ∫ ω in A, g (stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω) ∂P = ∫ ω in A, gm (stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω) ∂P :=
3487    setIntegral_congr_fun hAm fun ω hω => by
3488      simp only [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ (hZK ω hω)]
3489  have h2 : ∫ ω in A, g (Y₀ ω) ∂P = ∫ ω in A, gm (Y₀ ω) ∂P :=
3490    setIntegral_congr_fun hAm fun ω hω => by
3491      simp only [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ (hAK ω hω)]
3492  have hM0 : ∫ ω in A, stoppedProcess u ρ' T ω ∂P = 0 := by
3493    rw [← hmart.setIntegral_eq hT (hA 0)]
3494    have : ∀ ω ∈ A, stoppedProcess u ρ' 0 ω = 0 := by
3495      intro ω hω
3496      simp only [stoppedProcess]
3497      rw [min_eq_left (by simpa using hρ'nn ω hω)]
3498      simpa [hu] using itoIntegral_of_nonpos P ℱ W' Ψ (le_refl (0 : ℝ)) ω
3499    rw [setIntegral_congr_fun hAm this]; simp
3500  have hae' : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P.restrict A, gm (stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω) ≤
3501      gm (Y₀ ω) + C' * T + stoppedProcess u ρ' T ω := by
3502    rw [ae_restrict_iff' hAm]
3503    filter_upwards [hae] with ω hω hωA
3504    have := hω hωA
3505    simpa only [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ (hZK ω hωA),
3506      Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ (hAK ω hωA)] using this
3507  have e1 : Integrable (fun ω => gm (Y₀ ω) + C' * T) P := hint0.add (integrable_const _)
3508  have e2 : Integrable (fun ω => gm (Y₀ ω) + C' * T + stoppedProcess u ρ' T ω) P := e1.add hintM
3509  refine ⟨hint1.integrableOn.congr_fun (fun ω hω => (by
3510      simp only [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ (hZK ω hω)] : gm _ = g _)) hAm,
3511    hint0.integrableOn.congr_fun (fun ω hω => (by
3512      simp only [hgm, Set.piecewise_eq_of_mem _ _ _ (hAK ω hω)] : gm _ = g _)) hAm, ?_⟩
3513  rw [h1, h2]
3514  calc ∫ ω in A, gm (stoppedValue Wp ρ' ω) ∂P
3515      ≤ ∫ ω in A, (gm (Y₀ ω) + C' * T + stoppedProcess u ρ' T ω) ∂P :=
3516        setIntegral_mono_ae_restrict hint1.integrableOn
3517          e2.integrableOn hae'
3518    _ = ∫ ω in A, gm (Y₀ ω) ∂P + C' * T * (P A).toReal := by
3519        rw [integral_add e1.integrableOn hintM.integrableOn,
3520          integral_add hint0.integrableOn (integrable_const (C' * T)).integrableOn, hM0,
3521          setIntegral_const]
3522        simp [Measure.real, mul_comm]
3523
3524end StoppedEstimate

3526section PathEventually
3527
3528variable {d N : ℕ}
3529
3530/-- A compact piece of a collision-free path is bounded and uniformly separated. -/
3531theorem path_uniform_bounds {y : ℝ → (Fin N → Euc d)} {t : ℝ}
3532    (hcont : ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 t)) (hfree : ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t, y s ∈ liftedFree d N) :
3533    ∃ n₀ : ℕ, ∀ n : ℕ, n₀ ≤ n → ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t, ‖y s‖ < (n : ℝ) + 1 ∧
3534      ∀ i j, i ≠ j → 1 / ((n : ℝ) + 1) < pairSep (y s) i j := by
3535  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := (isCompact_Icc (a := (0 : ℝ)) (b := t)).exists_bound_of_continuousOn hcont
3536  have hinv : ContinuousOn (fun s => ∑ p : Fin N × Fin N,
3537      if p.1 ≠ p.2 then (pairSep (y s) p.1 p.2)⁻¹ else 0) (Set.Icc 0 t) := by
3538    refine continuousOn_finset_sum _ fun p _ => ?_
3539    by_cases hp : p.1 ≠ p.2
3540    · simp only [hp, ne_eq, not_false_eq_true, if_true]
3541      refine ContinuousOn.inv₀ ?_ fun s hs => (pairSep_pos (hfree s hs) hp).ne'
3542      exact (continuous_pairSep p.1 p.2).comp_continuousOn hcont
3543    · simp [hp]; exact continuousOn_const
3544  obtain ⟨B', hB'⟩ := (isCompact_Icc (a := (0 : ℝ)) (b := t)).exists_bound_of_continuousOn hinv
3545  obtain ⟨n₀, hn₀⟩ := exists_nat_gt (max B (max B' 0) + 1)
3546  refine ⟨n₀, fun n hn s hs => ⟨?_, fun i j hij => ?_⟩⟩
3547  · have h1 := hB s hs
3548    have : (n₀ : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3549    have := le_max_left B (max B' 0)
3550    linarith
3551  · have hpos := pairSep_pos (hfree s hs) hij
3552    have hle : (pairSep (y s) i j)⁻¹ ≤ B' := by
3553      have hterm : (pairSep (y s) i j)⁻¹ ≤ ∑ p : Fin N × Fin N,
3554          if p.1 ≠ p.2 then (pairSep (y s) p.1 p.2)⁻¹ else 0 := by
3555        have := Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun p : Fin N × Fin N =>
3556          if p.1 ≠ p.2 then (pairSep (y s) p.1 p.2)⁻¹ else 0)
3557          (fun p _ => by
3558            by_cases hp : p.1 ≠ p.2
3559            · simp only [hp, ne_eq, not_false_eq_true, if_true]
3560              exact (inv_pos.2 (pairSep_pos (hfree s hs) hp)).le
3561            · simp [hp]) (Finset.mem_univ (i, j))
3562        simpa [hij] using this
3563      exact hterm.trans ((le_abs_self _).trans (by simpa using hB' s hs))
3564    have hn' : (n₀ : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3565    have hB'pos : B' < (n : ℝ) + 1 := by
3566      have := le_max_left B' 0
3567      have := le_max_right B (max B' 0)
3568      linarith [le_max_right B' 0, le_max_left B (max B' 0)]
3569    have : (pairSep (y s) i j)⁻¹ < (n : ℝ) + 1 := lt_of_le_of_lt hle hB'pos
3570    rw [one_div]
3571    exact (inv_lt_comm₀ hpos (by positivity)).1 this
3572
3573
3574/-- The deterministic eventuality: if the path reaches `{Q_I ≤ a}` by time `T` before any
3575one-point enlargement reaches `δ`, then for all large `n` it starts in `K_n` and its first
3576hitting time of `G_n` is a time `≤ T` at which `Q_I ≤ a`. -/
3577theorem reach_eventually {τ : WithTop ℝ} {y : ℝ → (Fin N → Euc d)} (I : Finset (Fin N))
3578    {a δ q₁ T : ℝ} (haq : a ≤ q₁)
3579    (hcont : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ → ContinuousOn y (Set.Icc 0 t))
3580    (hfree : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ → y t ∈ liftedFree d N)
3581    (hq : q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (y 0))
3582    (hreach : ∃ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t ∧ t ≤ T ∧ (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ∧ clusterDispersionLift I (y t) ≤ a ∧
3583      ∀ s : ℝ, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ t → ∀ j, j ∉ I → δ < clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) (y s)) :
3584    ∃ n₀ : ℕ, ∀ n : ℕ, n₀ ≤ n → y 0 ∈ barrierK I a δ n ∧
3585      ∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ T ∧ pathHit τ y (barrierG I a δ n) = (r : WithTop ℝ) ∧
3586        clusterDispersionLift I (y r) ≤ a := by
3587  obtain ⟨t, ht0, htT, htτ, hQt, hδ⟩ := hreach
3588  have hc := hcont t ht0 htτ
3589  have hf : ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t, y s ∈ liftedFree d N := fun s hs =>
3590    hfree s hs.1 (lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast hs.2) htτ)
3591  obtain ⟨n₀, hn₀⟩ := path_uniform_bounds hc hf
3592  set S : Set ℝ := Set.Icc 0 t ∩ (fun s => clusterDispersionLift I (y s)) ⁻¹' Set.Iic a with hS
3593  have hQc : ContinuousOn (fun s => clusterDispersionLift I (y s)) (Set.Icc 0 t) :=
3594    (continuous_clusterDispersionLift I).comp_continuousOn hc
3595  have hScl : IsClosed S := hQc.preimage_isClosed_of_isClosed isClosed_Icc isClosed_Iic
3596  have hSc : IsCompact S := isCompact_Icc.of_isClosed_subset hScl Set.inter_subset_left
3597  have hSne : S.Nonempty := ⟨t, ⟨ht0, le_rfl⟩, hQt⟩
3598  set ts := sInf S with hts
3599  have htsS : ts ∈ S := hSc.sInf_mem hSne
3600  have hts_le : ∀ r ∈ S, ts ≤ r := fun r hr => csInf_le hSc.bddBelow hr
3601  have hts0 : 0 ≤ ts := htsS.1.1
3602  have htst : ts ≤ t := htsS.1.2
3603  have htsτ : (ts : WithTop ℝ) < τ := lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast htst) htτ
3604  refine ⟨n₀, fun n hn => ?_⟩
3605  have hbd := hn₀ n hn
3606  have h0 : (0 : ℝ) ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t := ⟨le_rfl, ht0⟩
3607  refine ⟨⟨⟨⟨?_, fun i j hij => (hbd 0 h0).2 i j hij |>.le⟩, haq.trans hq⟩,
3608    fun j hj => (hδ 0 le_rfl ht0 j hj).le⟩, ?_⟩
3609  · rw [mem_closedBall_zero_iff]; exact (hbd 0 h0).1.le
3610  have hGcl := isClosed_barrierG (d := d) I a δ n
3611  have hyG : y ts ∈ barrierG (d := d) I a δ n := Or.inl (Or.inl (Or.inl htsS.2))
3612  obtain ⟨r₀, hr₀, hr₀0, hr₀τ, hr₀G, hmin⟩ := pathHit_spec hGcl hcont ⟨ts, hts0, htsτ, hyG⟩
3613  have hr₀le : r₀ ≤ ts := hmin ts hts0 htsτ hyG
3614  have hr₀eq : r₀ = ts := by
3615    refine le_antisymm hr₀le ?_
3616    by_contra hlt
3617    push_neg at hlt
3618    have hmem : r₀ ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) t := ⟨hr₀0, hr₀le.trans htst⟩
3619    have hnS : r₀ ∉ S := fun hr => absurd (hts_le r₀ hr) (not_le.2 hlt)
3620    have hQ : a < clusterDispersionLift I (y r₀) := by
3621      by_contra hh; exact hnS ⟨hmem, not_lt.1 hh⟩
3622    obtain ⟨hb1, hb2⟩ := hbd r₀ hmem
3623    rcases hr₀G with ((h1 | h2) | h3) | h4
3624    · exact absurd h1 (not_le.2 hQ)
3625    · obtain ⟨j, hj, hjle⟩ := h2
3626      exact absurd hjle (not_le.2 (hδ r₀ hr₀0 hmem.2 j hj))
3627    · exact absurd h3 (not_le.2 hb1)
3628    · obtain ⟨i, j, hij, hle⟩ := h4
3629      exact absurd hle (not_le.2 (hb2 i j hij))
3630  refine ⟨ts, hts0, htst.trans htT, ?_, htsS.2.out⟩
3631  rw [hr₀, hr₀eq]
3632
3633end PathEventually
3634
3635section LevelEstimate
3636
3637variable {d N : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
3638variable {P : Measure Ω} {ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ} {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ}
3639
3640/-- The level estimate for the logarithmic barrier: with `m = -log (q/2)` the minimum of `Φ`,
3641`(Φ(a) - m) P(B) ≤ Φ(q₁ ∧ q/2) - m + C' T`, where `B` contains the event that
3642the process starts in `K_n ∩ {Q_I ≥ q₁}` and its `G_n`-hitting time is `≤ T` with `Q_I ≤ a` there. -/
3643theorem barrier_level_estimate [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
3644    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
3645    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3646    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card)
3647    {q C' : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) (hC' : 0 ≤ C') {δ : ℝ}
3648    (hgen : ∀ Z : Fin N → Euc d, (∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z) →
3649      Z ∈ liftedFree d N → diffGen D (barrierPhi q ∘ clusterDispersionLift I) Z ≤ C')
3650    {T : ℝ} (hT : 0 ≤ T) {a q₁ : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hq₁ : 0 < q₁) (n : ℕ) :
3651    ∃ B : Set Ω, MeasurableSet B ∧
3652      (∀ ω, Y₀ ω ∈ barrierK I a δ n → q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) →
3653        (∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ T ∧ solHit τ Y (barrierG I a δ n) ω = (r : WithTop ℝ) ∧
3654          clusterDispersionLift I (Y r ω) ≤ a) → ω ∈ B) ∧
3655      (barrierPhi q a + Real.log (q / 2)) * (P B).toReal ≤
3656        barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) + Real.log (q / 2) + C' * T := by
3657  classical
3658  set Kc := barrierK (d := d) I a δ n with hKc_def
3659  set G := barrierG (d := d) I a δ n with hG_def
3660  have hKc : IsCompact Kc := isCompact_barrierK I a δ n
3661  have hG : IsClosed G := isClosed_barrierG I a δ n
3662  have hGK : Gᶜ ⊆ Kc := barrierG_compl_subset I a δ n
3663  have hKfree : Kc ⊆ liftedFree d N := barrierK_subset_free I a δ n
3664  set Φ := barrierPhi q with hΦdef
3665  set Q := clusterDispersionLift (d := d) (N := N) I with hQdef
3666  have hQc : Continuous Q := continuous_clusterDispersionLift I
3667  have hU : IsOpen {Z : Fin N → Euc d | 0 < Q Z} := isOpen_lt continuous_const hQc
3668  have hKU : Kc ⊆ {Z | 0 < Q Z} := barrierK_subset_pos I ha δ n
3669  have hg : ContDiffOn ℝ 2 (Φ ∘ Q) {Z | 0 < Q Z} :=
3670    (barrierPhi_contDiffOn hq).comp (contDiff_two_clusterDispersionLift I).contDiffOn
3671      (fun Z hZ => hZ)
3672  have hgenK : ∀ Z ∈ Kc, diffGen D (Φ ∘ Q) Z ≤ C' := fun Z hZ => hgen Z hZ.2 (hKfree hZ)
3673  set A : Set Ω := Y₀ ⁻¹' (Kc ∩ {Z | q₁ ≤ Q Z}) with hAdef
3674  have hA : ∀ t : ℝ, MeasurableSet[ℱ t] A := fun t =>
3675    (hsol.init_meas t) ((hKc.isClosed.inter (isClosed_le continuous_const hQc)).measurableSet)
3676  have hAK : ∀ ω ∈ A, Y₀ ω ∈ Kc := fun ω hω => hω.1
3677  obtain ⟨Z, hZm, hZK, hZhit, hintZ, hint0, hineq⟩ := stopped_estimate D hK hV hW' hsol hT hU Kc hKc
3678    hKU hKfree hg hG hGK hC' hgenK hA hAK
3679  have hAm : MeasurableSet A := ℱ.le 0 _ (hA 0)
3680  set R : Set Ω := Z ⁻¹' {W | Q W ≤ a} with hR
3681  have hRm : MeasurableSet R := hZm (isClosed_le hQc continuous_const).measurableSet
3682  refine ⟨A ∩ R, hAm.inter hRm, ?_, ?_⟩
3683  · intro ω h1 h2 ⟨r, hr0, hrT, hhit, hQ⟩
3684    have hωA : ω ∈ A := ⟨h1, h2⟩
3685    refine ⟨hωA, ?_⟩
3686    show Q (Z ω) ≤ a
3687    rw [hZhit ω hωA r hr0 hrT hhit]; exact hQ
3688  set m : ℝ := -Real.log (q / 2) with hm
3689  have hmle : ∀ x, 0 < x → m ≤ Φ x := fun x hx => barrierPhi_ge hq hx
3690  set c := Φ a - m with hc
3691  have hc0 : 0 ≤ c := by have := hmle a ha; linarith
3692  have hanti := barrierPhi_antitoneOn hq
3693  have hQZpos : ∀ ω ∈ A, 0 < Q (Z ω) := fun ω hω => hKU (hZK ω hω)
3694  -- lower bound
3695  have hfint : Integrable (fun ω => m + R.indicator (fun _ => c) ω) P :=
3696    (integrable_const m).add ((integrable_const c).indicator hRm)
3697  have hlow : ∫ ω in A, (m + R.indicator (fun _ => c) ω) ∂P ≤ ∫ ω in A, (Φ ∘ Q) (Z ω) ∂P := by
3698    refine setIntegral_mono_on hfint.integrableOn hintZ hAm fun ω hω => ?_
3699    by_cases hωR : ω ∈ R
3700    · rw [Set.indicator_of_mem hωR]
3701      have : Φ a ≤ Φ (Q (Z ω)) := hanti (show Q (Z ω) ∈ Set.Ioi 0 from hQZpos ω hω)
3702        (show a ∈ Set.Ioi 0 from ha) hωR
3703      simp only [hc, Function.comp]; linarith
3704    · rw [Set.indicator_of_notMem hωR]
3705      simpa using hmle _ (hQZpos ω hω)
3706  have hlow' : ∫ ω in A, (m + R.indicator (fun _ => c) ω) ∂P =
3707      m * (P A).toReal + c * (P (A ∩ R)).toReal := by
3708    rw [integral_add (integrable_const m).integrableOn
3709      ((integrable_const c).indicator hRm).integrableOn, setIntegral_const,
3710      setIntegral_indicator hRm, setIntegral_const]
3711    simp [Measure.real, mul_comm]
3712  -- upper bound on the initial value
3713  set c₁ := Φ (min q₁ (q / 2)) with hc₁
3714  have hmin0 : 0 < min q₁ (q / 2) := lt_min hq₁ (by linarith)
3715  have hupp : ∫ ω in A, (Φ ∘ Q) (Y₀ ω) ∂P ≤ c₁ * (P A).toReal := by
3716    have : ∫ ω in A, (Φ ∘ Q) (Y₀ ω) ∂P ≤ ∫ ω in A, c₁ ∂P := by
3717      refine setIntegral_mono_on hint0 (integrableOn_const (by simp)) hAm fun ω hω => ?_
3718      have hQ : min q₁ (q / 2) ≤ Q (Y₀ ω) := (min_le_left _ _).trans hω.2
3719      exact hanti (show min q₁ (q / 2) ∈ Set.Ioi 0 from hmin0)
3720        (show Q (Y₀ ω) ∈ Set.Ioi 0 from lt_of_lt_of_le hmin0 hQ) hQ
3721    simpa [Measure.real, mul_comm] using this
3722  have hc₁m : m ≤ c₁ := hmle _ hmin0
3723  have hX1 : (P A).toReal ≤ 1 := measureReal_le_one
3724  have hX0 : 0 ≤ (P A).toReal := ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
3725  have hK0 : 0 ≤ c₁ - m + C' * T := by have := mul_nonneg hC' hT; linarith
3726  have hfin : c * (P (A ∩ R)).toReal ≤ (c₁ - m + C' * T) * (P A).toReal := by
3727    have h1 := hlow.trans hineq
3728    rw [hlow'] at h1
3729    have : C' * T * (P A).toReal = C' * T * (P A).toReal := rfl
3730    nlinarith [hupp]
3731  have : (c₁ - m + C' * T) * (P A).toReal ≤ c₁ - m + C' * T :=
3732    mul_le_of_le_one_right hK0 hX1
3733  simp only [hc, hm, hc₁] at hfin this ⊢
3734  linarith
3735
3736end LevelEstimate
3737
3738end ClusterBarrier
3739
3740open ClusterBarrier
3741
3742/-- Lemma `B:lem:cluster-ito`, the Itô equation `B:eq:cluster-ito` (M L11784-11788), before
3743`τ_⋆` and below the cluster radius.  Let `ρ < τ` be a stopping time and `K ⊆ liftedFree d N` a
3744compact set on which `Q_I < clusterRadius I`, such that the path stays in `K` on `[0, T]` up to
3745`ρ`.  Then there is a progressively measurable square-integrable integrand `Ψ` which agrees with
3746the noise coefficient `s ↦ D Q_I(Y_s)[σ_k]` (`clusterNoiseIntegrand`) on `[0, T]` up to `ρ`, and
3747a.s. for all `t ∈ [0, T]` with `t ≤ ρ`,
3748`Q_I(Y_t) = Q_I(Y_0) + ∫_0^t (2d(k-1)/β + 𝒜_I + ℛ_I)(Y_s) ds + ℳ_I(t)`, where `ℳ_I` is the
3749local Itô integral of `Ψ` stopped at `ρ`; its quadratic-variation density is `(8/β) Q_I` by
3750`cluster_qv`.
3751
3752The martingale part is stated through such a `Ψ` rather than through `clusterNoiseIntegrand`
3753itself because `IsMaximalParticleSolution` constrains `Y` only on `[0, τ)`: off that interval
3754`clusterNoiseIntegrand D I Y` need not be progressively measurable, and the local Itô integral of
3755a non-progressive integrand is a junk value.  The hypotheses `hK`, `hV` are the standing
3756hypotheses of Proposition B.1 (continuity of the drift on the collision-free region). -/
3757theorem cluster_ito_equation [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
3758    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W') {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3759    {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3760    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y)
3761    (I : Finset (Fin N)) {T : ℝ} (hT : 0 ≤ T) (ρ : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (hρ : IsStoppingTime ℱ ρ)
3762    (hρτ : ∀ ω, ρ ω < τ ω) (K : Set (Fin N → Euc d)) (hKc : IsCompact K)
3763    (hKU : K ⊆ liftedFree d N) (hKq : ∀ Z ∈ K, clusterDispersionLift I Z < clusterRadius I)
3764    (hYK : ∀ ω, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → Y t ω ∈ K) :
3765    ∃ Ψ : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ, IsProgressiveL2 P ℱ Ψ ∧
3766      (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ s : ℝ, 0 ≤ s → s ≤ T → (s : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω → ∀ k,
3767        Ψ s ω k = clusterNoiseIntegrand D I Y s ω k) ∧
3768      ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, (t : WithTop ℝ) ≤ ρ ω →
3769        IntegrableOn (fun s => clusterGenerator D I (Y s ω)) (Set.Icc 0 t) ∧
3770        clusterDispersionLift I (Y t ω) = clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) +
3771          (∫ s in Set.Icc 0 t, (2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ +
3772            clusterInternal D I (Y s ω) + clusterRemainder D I (Y s ω))) +
3773          localItoIntegral P ℱ W' (stopIntegrand Ψ ρ) t ω := by
3774  obtain ⟨Ψ, hΨ, hΨeq, hito⟩ := ito_along_solution D hK hV hW' hsol hT ρ hρ hρτ isOpen_univ K
3775    hKc (Set.subset_univ K) hKU (((contDiff_clusterDispersionLift I).of_le
3776      (WithTop.coe_le_coe.2 le_top)).contDiffOn) hYK
3777  refine ⟨Ψ, hΨ, hΨeq, ?_⟩
3778  filter_upwards [hito] with ω hω t ht htρ
3779  obtain ⟨hint, heq⟩ := hω t ht htρ
3780  have hfun : (fun s => 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ + clusterInternal D I (Y s ω) +
3781      clusterRemainder D I (Y s ω)) = fun s => diffGen D (clusterDispersionLift I) (Y s ω) := by
3782    funext s
3783    simp only [clusterRemainder, clusterGenerator_eq_diffGen]
3784    ring
3785  refine ⟨by simpa only [clusterGenerator_eq_diffGen] using hint, ?_⟩
3786  rw [hfun]
3787  exact heq
3788
3789/-- The hitting-probability estimate `B:eq:cluster-hitting-probability` (M L11889-11892), with
3790the random initial configuration localised to `{Q_I(Y₀) ≥ q₁}` (an `ℱ_0`-measurable event):
3791`P(Q_I(Y₀) ≥ q₁, σ_{I,a} ≤ T ∧ η_{I,δ} ∧ τ) ≤ C(I,δ,T,q₁)/|log a|` for `0 < a < a₀`.  Valid when
3792`d(k-1) ≥ 2` or `invβ = 0` (the only use of `d(k-1) ≥ 2` is the sign of the diffusion
3793contribution `(4 - 2d(k-1)) invβ / Q_I` in `B:eq:cluster-log-generator`, M L11867-11872).
3794Proof: the barrier `Φ` of M L11863, `cluster_ito_equation` up to
3795`T ∧ η_{I,δ} ∧ σ_{I,a} ∧ τ_n`, `clusterInternal_nonneg`, `clusterRemainder_bound`, the
3796set-integral form of optional stopping on `{Q_I(Y₀) ≥ q₁}` for the stopped `L²` martingale, and
3797monotone convergence in `n`. -/
3798theorem cluster_hitting_probability [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d)
3799    (hK : KernelLocal d D.s) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
3800    (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W') {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ}
3801    {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y)
3802    (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card) (hdk : D.invβ = 0 ∨ 2 ≤ d * (I.card - 1))
3803    (δ : ℝ) (hδ : 0 < δ) (q₁ : ℝ) (hq₁ : 0 < q₁) (T : ℝ) :
3804    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧ ∀ a : ℝ, 0 < a → a < a₀ →
3805      P {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} ≤
3806        ENNReal.ofReal (C / |Real.log a|) := by
3807  classical
3808  obtain ⟨q, C₀, hq, hqr, hC₀, hrem⟩ := clusterRemainder_bound D hK hV I hI δ hδ
3809  obtain ⟨C', hC'0, hC'⟩ := barrier_generator_bound D hM I hdk hI hq hqr hC₀
3810    {Z | ∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z}
3811    (fun Y hYS hYf hYq => hrem Y hYf hYq hYS)
3812  have hgen : ∀ Z : Fin N → Euc d, (∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z) →
3813      Z ∈ liftedFree d N → diffGen D (barrierPhi q ∘ clusterDispersionLift I) Z ≤ C' :=
3814    fun Z hZS hZf => hC' Z hZS hZf
3815  set T' := max T 0 with hT'
3816  have hT'0 : 0 ≤ T' := le_max_right _ _
3817  set m : ℝ := Real.log (q / 2) with hm
3818  set Kb : ℝ := barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) + m + C' * T' with hKb
3819  have hmin0 : 0 < min q₁ (q / 2) := lt_min hq₁ (by linarith)
3820  have hKb0 : 0 ≤ Kb := by
3821    have h1 : -Real.log (q / 2) ≤ barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) := barrierPhi_ge hq hmin0
3822    have := mul_nonneg hC'0 hT'0
3823    simp only [hKb, hm]; linarith
3824  refine ⟨min (q / 4) (min q₁ (Real.exp (-(2 * |m|)))), 2 * Kb, ?_, ?_⟩
3825  · exact lt_min (by linarith) (lt_min hq₁ (Real.exp_pos _))
3826  intro a ha hlt
3827  have haq : a < q / 4 := lt_of_lt_of_le hlt (min_le_left _ _)
3828  have haq₁ : a < q₁ := lt_of_lt_of_le hlt ((min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_left _ _))
3829  have hae : a < Real.exp (-(2 * |m|)) :=
3830    lt_of_lt_of_le hlt ((min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_right _ _))
3831  have hlog : Real.log a < -(2 * |m|) := by
3832    rw [Real.log_lt_iff_lt_exp ha]; exact hae
3833  have hlogneg : Real.log a < 0 := by have := abs_nonneg m; linarith
3834  have habs : |Real.log a| = -Real.log a := abs_of_neg hlogneg
3835  have hΦa : barrierPhi q a = -Real.log a := barrierPhi_of_le hq haq.le
3836  have hca : |Real.log a| / 2 ≤ barrierPhi q a + m := by
3837    rw [hΦa, habs]
3838    have := neg_abs_le m
3839    have := le_abs_self m
3840    linarith
3841  have hca0 : 0 < barrierPhi q a + m := lt_of_lt_of_le (by rw [habs]; linarith) hca
3842  -- level estimates
3843  have hB : ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ B : Set Ω, MeasurableSet B ∧
3844      (∀ ω, Y₀ ω ∈ barrierK I a δ n → q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) →
3845        (∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ T' ∧ solHit τ Y (barrierG I a δ n) ω = (r : WithTop ℝ) ∧
3846          clusterDispersionLift I (Y r ω) ≤ a) → ω ∈ B) ∧
3847      (barrierPhi q a + Real.log (q / 2)) * (P B).toReal ≤
3848        barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) + Real.log (q / 2) + C' * T' := fun n =>
3849    barrier_level_estimate D hK hV hW' hsol I hI hq hC'0 hgen hT'0 ha hq₁ n
3850  choose B hBm hBsub hBest using hB
3851  have hPB : ∀ n, P (B n) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * Kb / |Real.log a|) := by
3852    intro n
3853    have h1 : (P (B n)).toReal ≤ 2 * Kb / |Real.log a| := by
3854      have hlogpos : 0 < |Real.log a| := by rw [habs]; linarith
3855      rw [le_div_iff₀ hlogpos]
3856      have h2 := hBest n
3857      have h3 : (P (B n)).toReal * (|Real.log a| / 2) ≤
3858          (P (B n)).toReal * (barrierPhi q a + m) :=
3859        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hca ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
3860      nlinarith
3861    calc P (B n) = ENNReal.ofReal (P (B n)).toReal := (ENNReal.ofReal_toReal (measure_ne_top _ _)).symm
3862      _ ≤ _ := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h1
3863  -- the event is contained in the liminf
3864  have hsub : {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} ⊆
3865      ⋃ n₀ : ℕ, ⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n := by
3866    rintro ω ⟨hq0, t, ht0, htT, htτ, hQt, hδt⟩
3867    have hY0 : Y 0 ω = Y₀ ω := hsol.sol.init ω
3868    obtain ⟨n₀, hn₀⟩ := reach_eventually (τ := τ ω) (y := fun t => Y t ω) I (δ := δ) (q₁ := q₁)
3869      (T := T') haq₁.le (hsol.sol.cont ω) (hsol.mem ω) (by simpa [hY0] using hq0)
3870      ⟨t, ht0, htT.trans (le_max_left _ _), htτ, hQt, hδt⟩
3871    refine Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨n₀, Set.mem_iInter₂.2 fun n hn => ?_⟩
3872    obtain ⟨hK0, r, hr0, hrT, hhit, hQr⟩ := hn₀ n hn
3873    exact hBsub n ω (by simpa [hY0] using hK0) hq0 ⟨r, hr0, hrT, hhit, hQr⟩
3874  have hmono : Monotone fun n₀ : ℕ => ⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n := by
3875    intro k l hkl
3876    exact Set.iInter₂_mono' fun n hn => ⟨n, hkl.trans hn, subset_rfl⟩
3877  calc P {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω}
3878      ≤ P (⋃ n₀ : ℕ, ⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n) := measure_mono hsub
3879    _ = ⨆ n₀ : ℕ, P (⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n) := hmono.measure_iUnion
3880    _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * Kb / |Real.log a|) := by
3881        refine iSup_le fun n₀ => ?_
3882        exact (measure_mono (Set.iInter₂_subset n₀ le_rfl)).trans (hPB n₀)
3883
3884namespace ClusterBarrier
3885
3886section Barrier
3887
3888variable {d N : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
3889variable {P : Measure Ω} {ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ} {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ}
3890
3891theorem cluster_barrier_of_hdk [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
3892    (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
3893    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3894    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card)
3895    (hdk : D.invβ = 0 ∨ 2 ≤ d * (I.card - 1))
3896    (hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), I ⊂ J → J.card = I.card + 1 →
3897      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0)
3898    (T : ℝ) : P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
3899  classical
3900  set F : ℝ → Set Ω := fun δ => {ω | ∃ s : ℝ, 0 ≤ s ∧ s < T ∧ (s : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧
3901    ∃ j, j ∉ I ∧ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) (Y s ω) ≤ δ} with hF
3902  set X : ℕ → Set Ω := fun k => {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω ∧
3903    1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1) ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω)} with hX
3904  -- Step A
3905  have hA : ∀ k : ℕ, ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ → P (X k ∩ (F δ)ᶜ) = 0 := by
3906    intro k δ hδ
3907    obtain ⟨a₀, C, ha₀, hC⟩ := cluster_hitting_probability D hK hM hV hW' hsol I hI hdk δ hδ
3908      (1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1)) (by positivity) T
3909    have hsub : ∀ a : ℝ, 0 < a → X k ∩ (F δ)ᶜ ⊆
3910        {ω | 1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1) ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} := by
3911      intro a ha ω ⟨⟨hcol, hq0⟩, hnF⟩
3912      refine ⟨hq0, ?_⟩
3913      obtain ⟨t, ht0, htT, htτ, hQt⟩ := hcol a ha
3914      refine ⟨t, ht0, htT.le, htτ, hQt.le, fun s hs0 hst j hj => ?_⟩
3915      by_contra hle
3916      push_neg at hle
3917      exact hnF ⟨s, hs0, lt_of_le_of_lt hst htT,
3918        lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast hst) htτ, j, hj, hle⟩
3919    obtain ⟨n₀, hn₀⟩ := exists_nat_gt (-Real.log a₀)
3920    have hev : ∀ n : ℕ, n₀ ≤ n → P (X k ∩ (F δ)ᶜ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C / (n : ℝ)) := by
3921      intro n hn
3922      have hnR : (n₀ : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
3923      have hlt : Real.exp (-(n : ℝ)) < a₀ := by
3924        rw [← Real.lt_log_iff_exp_lt ha₀]; linarith
3925      have := hC _ (Real.exp_pos _) hlt
3926      rw [Real.log_exp, abs_neg, Nat.abs_cast] at this
3927      exact (measure_mono (hsub _ (Real.exp_pos _))).trans this
3928    have htend : Tendsto (fun n : ℕ => ENNReal.ofReal (C / (n : ℝ))) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3929      have := ENNReal.tendsto_ofReal (tendsto_const_div_atTop_nhds_zero_nat C)
3930      simpa using this
3931    exact le_antisymm (ge_of_tendsto htend (Filter.eventually_atTop.2 ⟨n₀, hev⟩)) bot_le
3932  -- Step B
3933  have hB : ∀ k : ℕ, X k ⊆ (⋃ j ∈ ({j | j ∉ I} : Set (Fin N)),
3934        {ω | ClusterCollapse (insert j I) T τ Y ω}) ∪ ⋃ l : ℕ, X k ∩ (F (1 / ((l : ℝ) + 1)))ᶜ := by
3935    intro k ω hω
3936    by_cases hcol : ω ∈ ⋃ j ∈ ({j | j ∉ I} : Set (Fin N)), {ω | ClusterCollapse (insert j I) T τ Y ω}
3937    · exact Or.inl hcol
3938    right
3939    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq, not_exists] at hcol
3940    have hnc : ∀ j, ∃ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε ∧ (j ∉ I → ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → t < T → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω →
3941        ε ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) (Y t ω)) := by
3942      intro j
3943      by_cases hj : j ∈ I
3944      · exact ⟨1, one_pos, fun h => absurd hj h⟩
3945      · have := hcol j hj
3946        unfold ClusterCollapse at this
3947        push_neg at this
3948        obtain ⟨ε, hε, h⟩ := this
3949        exact ⟨ε, hε, fun _ t h0 h1 h2 => h t h0 h1 h2⟩
3950    choose ε hε0 hε using hnc
3951    set ε₀ : ℝ := (1 + ∑ j, 1 / ε j)⁻¹ with hε₀
3952    have hS : 0 ≤ ∑ j, 1 / ε j := Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => (one_div_pos.2 (hε0 j)).le
3953    have hε₀pos : 0 < ε₀ := inv_pos.2 (by linarith)
3954    have hε₀le : ∀ j, ε₀ ≤ ε j := by
3955      intro j
3956      have h1 : 1 / ε j ≤ ∑ j, 1 / ε j :=
3957        Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => 1 / ε j) (fun j _ => (one_div_pos.2 (hε0 j)).le)
3958          (Finset.mem_univ j)
3959      rw [hε₀]
3960      have : (1 + ∑ j, 1 / ε j)⁻¹ ≤ (1 / ε j)⁻¹ := inv_anti₀ (one_div_pos.2 (hε0 j)) (by linarith)
3961      simpa using this
3962    obtain ⟨l, hl⟩ := exists_nat_one_div_lt hε₀pos
3963    refine Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨l, hω, ?_⟩
3964    rintro ⟨s, hs0, hsT, hsτ, j, hj, hle⟩
3965    have := hε j hj s hs0 hsT hsτ
3966    linarith [hε₀le j]
3967  -- conclusion
3968  have hk : {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} ⊆ ⋃ k : ℕ, X k := by
3969    intro ω hω
3970    have hfree : Y₀ ω ∈ liftedFree d N := by
3971      have := hsol.mem ω 0 le_rfl (by simpa using hsol.pos ω)
3972      rwa [hsol.sol.init ω] at this
3973    have hpos := clusterDispersionLift_pos hI hfree
3974    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := exists_nat_one_div_lt hpos
3975    exact Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨k, hω, hk.le⟩
3976  refine measure_mono_null hk (measure_iUnion_null fun k => ?_)
3977  refine measure_mono_null (hB k) (measure_union_null ?_ (measure_iUnion_null fun l => ?_))
3978  · refine measure_biUnion_null_iff (Set.to_countable _) |>.2 fun j hj => ?_
3979    exact hind (insert j I) (Finset.ssubset_insert hj) (Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hj) T
3980  · exact hA k _ (by positivity)
3981
3982end Barrier
3983
3984end ClusterBarrier

3986/-- Lemma `B:lem:cluster-barrier` (M L11849-11856): if `d(k-1) ≥ 2` and no strict one-point
3987enlargement of `I` collapses before `T ∧ τ` (for every `T`), then `I` does not collapse before
3988`T ∧ τ`.  Proof: `cluster_hitting_probability` with `a ↓ 0` (M L11893) and then `δ ↓ 0` through
3989the decreasing events `E_δ = {η_{I,δ} < T ∧ τ}`, whose intersection is contained in the union
3990of the collapse events of the finitely many enlargements (M L11895-11903). -/
3991theorem cluster_barrier [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hK : KernelLocal d D.s)
3992    (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
3993    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
3994    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card)
3995    (hdk : 2 ≤ d * (I.card - 1))
3996    (hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), I ⊂ J → J.card = I.card + 1 →
3997      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0)
3998    (T : ℝ) : P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
3999  exact cluster_barrier_of_hdk D hK hM hV hW' hsol I hI (Or.inr hdk) hind T
4000
4001/-- Lemma `B:lem:cluster-barrier` at zero temperature (M L11905-11909): for `invβ = 0` the
4002conclusion holds for every cluster with `|I| ≥ 2` in every dimension (the equation is a random
4003ODE and `d/dt Q_I ≥ -C Q_I` is a Grönwall barrier).  Same proof as `cluster_barrier` through
4004`cluster_hitting_probability` with `hdk := Or.inl`. -/
4005theorem cluster_barrier_zero_temp [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d)
4006    (hK : KernelLocal d D.s) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
4007    (hβ : D.invβ = 0) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W') {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
4008    {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
4009    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card)
4010    (hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), I ⊂ J → J.card = I.card + 1 →
4011      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0)
4012    (T : ℝ) : P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4013  exact cluster_barrier_of_hdk D hK hM hV hW' hsol I hI (Or.inl hβ) hind T

4021variable {d N : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}

4024/-- A finite family of positive numbers has a common positive lower bound. -/
4025theorem exists_pos_le_fintype {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] (ε : ι → ℝ) (h : ∀ i, 0 < ε i) :
4026    ∃ e : ℝ, 0 < e ∧ ∀ i, e ≤ ε i := by
4027  have hS : 0 ≤ ∑ j, 1 / ε j := Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => (one_div_pos.2 (h j)).le
4028  refine ⟨(1 + ∑ j, 1 / ε j)⁻¹, inv_pos.2 (by linarith), fun j => ?_⟩
4029  have h1 : 1 / ε j ≤ ∑ j, 1 / ε j :=
4030    Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => 1 / ε j) (fun j _ => (one_div_pos.2 (h j)).le)
4031      (Finset.mem_univ j)
4032  have : (1 + ∑ j, 1 / ε j)⁻¹ ≤ (1 / ε j)⁻¹ := inv_anti₀ (one_div_pos.2 (h j)) (by linarith)
4033  simpa using this

4035/-- For a pair cluster `{i,j}` at distance `r < 1/8`, `Q = r²/2`. -/
4036theorem clusterDispersionLift_pair {i j : Fin N} (hij : i ≠ j) (Y : Fin N → Euc d)
4037    (hr : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y i)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) < 1 / 8) :
4038    clusterDispersionLift ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) Y =
4039      (Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y i)) (Torus.mk (Y j))) ^ 2 / 2 := by
4040  have hr' : ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8 := by
4041    simpa [Torus.tdist, geoRep, mk_sub'] using hr
4042  have hanti := geoRep_antisymm Y i j hr'
4043  unfold clusterDispersionLift clusterDispersion
4044  rw [Finset.card_pair hij, Finset.sum_pair hij, Finset.sum_pair hij, Finset.sum_pair hij]
4045  have h00 : ∀ k : Fin N, pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y k) - Torus.mk (Y k)) = 0 := fun k => by
4046    rw [sub_self]; exact (pairDispersion_eq_zero_iff 0).2 rfl
4047  have e1 : pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y i) - Torus.mk (Y j)) = ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 := by
4048    rw [← mk_sub']
4049    exact pairDispersion_eq_sq _ hr'.le
4050  have e2 : pairDispersion (Torus.mk (Y j) - Torus.mk (Y i)) = ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ 2 := by
4051    rw [← mk_sub']
4052    rw [pairDispersion_eq_sq _ (by
4053      have : Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk (Y j - Y i)) = geoRep (Y j - Y i) := rfl
4054      rw [this, hanti, norm_neg]; exact hr'.le)]
4055    have : Torus.centeredRep (Torus.mk (Y j - Y i)) = geoRep (Y j - Y i) := rfl
4056    rw [this, hanti, norm_neg]
4057  have e3 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y i)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) = ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ := by
4058    simp [Torus.tdist, geoRep, mk_sub']
4059  rw [h00, h00, e1, e2, e3]
4060  push_cast
4061  ring
4062
4063
4064end NoCollision
4065
4066end ClusterBarrier

4068/-- Proposition `B:prop:particle-no-collision-dge2` (M L11912-11918): for `d ≥ 2` and
4069`Sym M ≤ 0`, no cluster `I` with `|I| ≥ 2` collapses before `T ∧ τ_⋆`
4070(`B:eq:no-cluster-collapse-dge2`), and consequently `τ_⋆ = ∞` a.s.  Proof: descending induction
4071on `|I|` (strong induction on `N - |I|`) with `cluster_barrier`, which applies at every step
4072since `d(|I|-1) ≥ 2`; then the collision alternative at a pair cluster
4073(`IsMaximalParticleSolution.collision_alternative`) on `{τ ≤ T}`, for integer `T`. -/
4074theorem particle_no_collision_dge2 [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hd : 2 ≤ d)
4075    (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
4076    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
4077    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) :
4078    (∀ I : Finset (Fin N), 2 ≤ I.card → ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0) ∧
4079      ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, τ ω = ⊤ := by
4080  classical
4081  have hK : KernelLocal d D.s := kernelLocal_of_two_le hd D.hs0 D.hsd
4082  have key : ∀ m : ℕ, ∀ I : Finset (Fin N), 2 ≤ I.card → N - I.card = m →
4083      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4084    intro m
4085    induction m using Nat.strong_induction_on with
4086    | _ m ih =>
4087      intro I hI hm T
4088      have hIN : I.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_univ I
4089      refine cluster_barrier D hK hM hV hW' hsol I hI ?_ ?_ T
4090      · have h1 : 1 ≤ I.card - 1 := by omega
4091        calc 2 = 2 * 1 := rfl
4092          _ ≤ d * (I.card - 1) := Nat.mul_le_mul hd h1
4093      · intro J _ hJc T'
4094        have hJN : J.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_univ J
4095        exact ih (N - J.card) (by omega) J (by omega) rfl T'
4096  have h1 : ∀ I : Finset (Fin N), 2 ≤ I.card → ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 :=
4097    fun I hI T => key _ I hI rfl T
4098  refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
4099  have hcol := IsMaximalParticleSolution.collision_alternative D hK hV hW' hsol
4100  have hnull : P (⋃ T : ℕ, ⋃ i : Fin N, ⋃ j : Fin N, ⋃ (_ : i ≠ j),
4101      {ω | ClusterCollapse ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) (T : ℝ) τ Y ω}) = 0 := by
4102    refine measure_iUnion_null fun T => measure_iUnion_null fun i => measure_iUnion_null fun j =>
4103      measure_iUnion_null fun hij => h1 _ ?_ _
4104    rw [Finset.card_pair hij]
4105  have hn2 := measure_eq_zero_iff_ae_notMem.1 hnull
4106  filter_upwards [hcol, hn2] with ω hω hnot
4107  by_contra hne
4108  have hlt : τ ω < ⊤ := lt_top_iff_ne_top.2 hne
4109  obtain ⟨τ₀, hτ₀⟩ := WithTop.ne_top_iff_exists.1 hne
4110  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := exists_nat_gt τ₀
4111  have hτT : ∀ t : ℝ, (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → t < (T : ℝ) := by
4112    intro t ht
4113    rw [← hτ₀] at ht
4114    have : t < τ₀ := by exact_mod_cast ht
4115    linarith
4116  apply hnot
4117  simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq]
4118  by_contra hno
4119  push_neg at hno
4120  -- every pair has a positive lower bound
4121  have hpair : ∀ p : Fin N × Fin N, ∃ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε ∧ (p.1 ≠ p.2 → ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → t < T →
4122      (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → ε ≤ clusterDispersionLift ({p.1, p.2} : Finset (Fin N)) (Y t ω)) := by
4123    intro p
4124    by_cases hp : p.1 = p.2
4125    · exact ⟨1, one_pos, fun h => absurd hp h⟩
4126    · have := hno T p.1 p.2 hp
4127      unfold ClusterCollapse at this
4128      push_neg at this
4129      obtain ⟨ε, hε, h⟩ := this
4130      exact ⟨ε, hε, fun _ t h0 h1 h2 => h t h0 h1 h2⟩
4131  choose ε hε0 hε using hpair
4132  obtain ⟨e, he0, hele⟩ := exists_pos_le_fintype ε hε0
4133  obtain ⟨t, ht1, htτ, i, j, hij, hdist⟩ := hω hlt (min (1 / 8) (16 * e)) (lt_min (by norm_num)
4134    (by linarith)) 0 le_rfl (by simpa using hsol.pos ω)
4135  have hr8 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) < 1 / 8 :=
4136    lt_of_lt_of_le hdist (min_le_left _ _)
4137  have hr16 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) < 16 * e :=
4138    lt_of_lt_of_le hdist (min_le_right _ _)
4139  have hQ := clusterDispersionLift_pair hij (Y t ω) hr8
4140  have hnn : 0 ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) := norm_nonneg _
4141  have hlow := hε (i, j) hij t ht1.le (hτT t htτ) htτ
4142  have := hele (i, j)
4143  set r := Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j))
4144  simp only at hlow
4145  rw [hQ] at hlow
4146  nlinarith

4153theorem d1_force_term (D : DynData 1) (hs : 0 < D.s) (z : Euc 1) (hz : z ≠ 0) :
4154    inner ℝ z (Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s z)) =
4155      -(D.M 0 0) * D.s * ‖z‖ ^ (-D.s) := by
4156  have hn : 0 < ‖z‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hz
4157  have e : Torus.matVec D.M (eucRieszGrad D.s z) = -(rieszRadial D.s z) • Torus.matVec D.M z := by
4158    unfold eucRieszGrad Torus.matVec; exact map_smul _ _ _
4159  rw [e, real_inner_smul_right, inner_matVec_eq]
4160  have h1 : z.ofLp ⬝ᵥ (D.M *ᵥ z.ofLp) = D.M 0 0 * ‖z‖ ^ 2 := by
4161    rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_sq_eq]
4162    simp [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct]
4163    ring
4164  rw [h1]
4165  have h2 : rieszRadial D.s z = D.s * ‖z‖ ^ (-D.s - 2) := by
4166    unfold rieszRadial; simp [hs.ne']
4167  rw [h2]
4168  have h3 : ‖z‖ ^ (-D.s - 2) * ‖z‖ ^ 2 = ‖z‖ ^ (-D.s) := by
4169    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_add hn]; norm_num
4170  calc -(D.s * ‖z‖ ^ (-D.s - 2)) * (D.M 0 0 * ‖z‖ ^ 2)
4171      = -(D.M 0 0) * D.s * (‖z‖ ^ (-D.s - 2) * ‖z‖ ^ 2) := by ring
4172    _ = _ := by rw [h3]

4175theorem d1_clusterInternal_pair (D : DynData 1) (hs : 0 < D.s) {i j : Fin N} (hij : i ≠ j)
4176    (Y : Fin N → Euc 1) (hr : ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8) (hr0 : geoRep (Y i - Y j) ≠ 0) :
4177    clusterInternal D ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) Y =
4178      (2 / (N : ℝ)) * (-(D.M 0 0) * D.s * ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ ^ (-D.s)) := by
4179  have hanti := geoRep_antisymm Y i j hr
4180  unfold clusterInternal
4181  rw [Finset.sum_pair hij, Finset.sum_pair hij, Finset.sum_pair hij]
4182  have h0 : ∀ k : Fin N, geoRep (Y k - Y k) = 0 := fun k => by rw [sub_self]; exact geoRep_zero
4183  rw [h0, h0, hanti, d1_force_term D hs _ hr0, d1_force_term D hs _ (neg_ne_zero.2 hr0),
4184    norm_neg]
4185  simp [eucRieszGrad, Torus.matVec]
4186  ring
