4189theorem d1_internal_ge (D : DynData 1) (hs : 0 < D.s) (ha : D.M 0 0 < 0) {i j : Fin N}
4190    (hij : i ≠ j) :
4191    ∃ q₂ : ℝ, 0 < q₂ ∧ q₂ ≤ clusterRadius ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) ∧
4192      ∀ Y : Fin N → Euc 1, 0 < clusterDispersionLift ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) Y →
4193        clusterDispersionLift ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) Y < q₂ →
4194        2 * D.invβ ≤ clusterInternal D ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) Y := by
4195  have hN : (0 : ℝ) < N := by exact_mod_cast Fin.pos i
4196  set κ : ℝ := -(D.M 0 0) * D.s with hκ
4197  have hκ0 : 0 < κ := mul_pos (by linarith) hs
4198  set B : ℝ := N * D.invβ / κ with hB
4199  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := by have := D.hβ; positivity
4200  set r₀ : ℝ := (B + 1) ^ (-1 / D.s) with hr₀
4201  have hr₀0 : 0 < r₀ := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by linarith) _
4202  have hr₀s : r₀ ^ (-D.s) = B + 1 := by
4203    rw [hr₀, ← Real.rpow_mul (by linarith)]
4204    have : -1 / D.s * -D.s = 1 := by field_simp
4205    rw [this, Real.rpow_one]
4206  have hrad : clusterRadius ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) = 1 / 256 := by
4207    unfold clusterRadius; rw [Finset.card_pair hij]; norm_num
4208  refine ⟨min (r₀ ^ 2 / 2) (1 / 256), lt_min (by positivity) (by norm_num),
4209    hrad ▸ min_le_right _ _, fun Y hQ0 hQ => ?_⟩
4210  have hQrad : clusterDispersionLift ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) Y <
4211      clusterRadius ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) := by
4212    rw [hrad]; exact lt_of_lt_of_le hQ (min_le_right _ _)
4213  have hr8 : ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ < 1 / 8 :=
4214    (clusterDispersionLift_eq_euclid _ Y hQrad).1 i (by simp) j (by simp)
4215  have hQeq := clusterDispersionLift_pair hij Y (by simpa [Torus.tdist, geoRep, mk_sub'] using hr8)
4216  have he3 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y i)) (Torus.mk (Y j)) = ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ := by
4217    simp [Torus.tdist, geoRep, mk_sub']
4218  rw [he3] at hQeq
4219  set r := ‖geoRep (Y i - Y j)‖ with hr
4220  have hrpos : 0 < r := by
4221    by_contra h
4222    have : r = 0 := le_antisymm (not_lt.1 h) (norm_nonneg _)
4223    rw [hQeq, this] at hQ0; simp at hQ0
4224  have hz : geoRep (Y i - Y j) ≠ 0 := fun h => by rw [h] at hr; simp [hr] at hrpos
4225  have hrlt : r < r₀ := by
4226    have h1 : r ^ 2 / 2 < r₀ ^ 2 / 2 := by
4227      rw [← hQeq]; exact lt_of_lt_of_le hQ (min_le_left _ _)
4228    nlinarith
4229  have hpow : B + 1 < r ^ (-D.s) := by
4230    rw [← hr₀s]
4231    exact Real.rpow_lt_rpow_of_neg hrpos hrlt (by linarith)
4232  rw [d1_clusterInternal_pair D hs hij Y hr8 hz]
4233  have : B * κ = N * D.invβ := by rw [hB]; field_simp
4234  have h2 : (2 / (N : ℝ)) * (κ * r ^ (-D.s)) ≥ (2 / (N : ℝ)) * (κ * B) := by
4235    apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ (by positivity)
4236    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (by linarith) hκ0.le
4237  calc 2 * D.invβ = (2 / (N : ℝ)) * (κ * B) := by field_simp; nlinarith
4238    _ ≤ _ := h2

4244theorem barrier_generator_bound_of_internal (D : DynData d) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (I : Finset (Fin N))
4245    (hI : 2 ≤ I.card) {q C₀ : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q)
4246    (hqr : q < clusterRadius I) (hC₀ : 0 ≤ C₀) (S : Set (Fin N → Euc d))
4247    (hrem : ∀ Y ∈ S, Y ∈ liftedFree d N → clusterDispersionLift I Y < q →
4248      -C₀ * clusterDispersionLift I Y ≤ clusterRemainder D I Y)
4249    (hA : ∀ Y ∈ S, Y ∈ liftedFree d N → clusterDispersionLift I Y < q / 4 →
4250      4 * D.invβ ≤ 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ + clusterInternal D I Y) :
4251    ∃ C' : ℝ, 0 ≤ C' ∧ ∀ Y ∈ S, Y ∈ liftedFree d N →
4252      diffGen D (barrierPhi q ∘ clusterDispersionLift I) Y ≤ C' := by
4253  have hΦ := barrierPhi_contDiffOn hq
4254  have hd1 : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 (deriv (barrierPhi q)) (Set.Ioi 0) :=
4255    hΦ.deriv_of_isOpen isOpen_Ioi (m := 1) (by norm_num)
4256  have hc1 : ContinuousOn (deriv (barrierPhi q)) (Set.Ioi 0) := hd1.continuousOn
4257  have hc2 : ContinuousOn (deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q))) (Set.Ioi 0) :=
4258    hd1.continuousOn_deriv_of_isOpen isOpen_Ioi le_rfl
4259  set f : ℝ → ℝ := fun x => |deriv (barrierPhi q) x| * C₀ * x +
4260    4 * D.invβ * x * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) x| with hf
4261  have hsub : Set.Icc (q / 4) (3 * q / 4) ⊆ Set.Ioi 0 := fun x hx => by
4262    simp only [Set.mem_Icc, Set.mem_Ioi] at hx ⊢; linarith [hx.1]
4263  have hfc : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc (q / 4) (3 * q / 4)) := by
4264    have a1 := (hc1.mono hsub).abs
4265    have a2 := (hc2.mono hsub).abs
4266    exact ((a1.mul continuousOn_const).mul continuousOn_id).add
4267      (((continuousOn_const.mul continuousOn_id)).mul a2)
4268  obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := isCompact_Icc.exists_bound_of_continuousOn hfc
4269  have hc₁0 : 0 ≤ 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ := by
4270    have : (1 : ℝ) ≤ I.card := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 1 ≤ I.card)
4271    have := D.hβ
4272    positivity
4273  refine ⟨C₀ + |B|, by positivity, fun Y hYS hYf => ?_⟩
4274  have hQpos := clusterDispersionLift_pos hI hYf
4275  set a := clusterDispersionLift I Y with ha
4276  rw [diffGen_barrier D I hq hQpos]
4277  rcases le_or_gt (3 * q / 4) a with h34 | h34
4278  · have h1 : deriv (barrierPhi q) a = 0 := deriv_barrierPhi_of_gt hq (by linarith)
4279    have h2 : deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a = 0 := deriv2_barrierPhi_of_gt hq (by linarith)
4280    rw [h1, h2]; simp; positivity
4281  · have hQr : a < clusterRadius I := by linarith
4282    have hqv := cluster_qv D I Y hQr
4283    rw [hqv]
4284    have hG : clusterGenerator D I Y = 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ +
4285        clusterInternal D I Y + clusterRemainder D I Y := by
4286      unfold clusterRemainder; ring
4287    have hA := clusterInternal_nonneg D hM I Y
4288    have hR := hrem Y hYS hYf (by linarith)
4289    have hs := deriv_barrierPhi_nonpos hq hQpos
4290    set s := deriv (barrierPhi q) a with hsdef
4291    set c₁ := 2 * (d : ℝ) * ((I.card : ℝ) - 1) * D.invβ with hc₁def
4292    have hGlow : c₁ - C₀ * a ≤ clusterGenerator D I Y := by rw [hG]; linarith
4293    have h3 : s * clusterGenerator D I Y ≤ s * (c₁ - C₀ * a) :=
4294      mul_le_mul_of_nonpos_left hGlow hs
4295    rcases lt_or_ge a (q / 4) with h4 | h4
4296    · have e1 : s = -(1 / a) := deriv_barrierPhi_of_le hq hQpos h4
4297      have e2 : deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a = 1 / a ^ 2 := deriv2_barrierPhi_of_le hq hQpos h4
4298      rw [e2]
4299      have hGlow' : c₁ + clusterInternal D I Y - C₀ * a ≤ clusterGenerator D I Y := by
4300        rw [hG]; linarith
4301      have h3' : s * clusterGenerator D I Y ≤ s * (c₁ + clusterInternal D I Y - C₀ * a) :=
4302        mul_le_mul_of_nonpos_left hGlow' hs
4303      have : s * (c₁ + clusterInternal D I Y - C₀ * a) + 1 / 2 * (1 / a ^ 2) * (8 * D.invβ * a) =
4304          (-(c₁ + clusterInternal D I Y) + 4 * D.invβ) / a + C₀ := by
4305        rw [e1]; field_simp; ring
4306      have h5 : (-(c₁ + clusterInternal D I Y) + 4 * D.invβ) / a ≤ 0 :=
4307        div_nonpos_of_nonpos_of_nonneg (by linarith [hA Y hYS hYf h4]) hQpos.le
4308      have h6 : 1 / 2 * (1 / a ^ 2) * (8 * D.invβ * a) ≥ 0 := by
4309        have := D.hβ; positivity
4310      nlinarith [abs_nonneg B]
4311    · have hmem : a ∈ Set.Icc (q / 4) (3 * q / 4) := ⟨h4, h34.le⟩
4312      have hfa := hB a hmem
4313      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at hfa
4314      have hfa' : f a ≤ |B| := ((le_abs_self (f a)).trans hfa).trans (le_abs_self B)
4315      have habs : |s| = -s := abs_of_nonpos hs
4316      have e3 : s * (c₁ - C₀ * a) ≤ |s| * C₀ * a := by
4317        rw [habs]; nlinarith [mul_nonneg (neg_nonneg.2 hs) hc₁0]
4318      have e4 : 1 / 2 * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a * (8 * D.invβ * a) ≤
4319          4 * D.invβ * a * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a| := by
4320        have := D.hβ
4321        have h0 : 0 ≤ 4 * D.invβ * a := by positivity
4322        calc 1 / 2 * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a * (8 * D.invβ * a)
4323            = 4 * D.invβ * a * deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a := by ring
4324          _ ≤ 4 * D.invβ * a * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a| :=
4325            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_abs_self _) h0
4326      have : f a = |s| * C₀ * a + 4 * D.invβ * a * |deriv (deriv (barrierPhi q)) a| := rfl
4327      nlinarith [abs_nonneg B]
4328  
4329end Generator

4332section HitBarrier
4333
4334variable {d N : ℕ} {Ω : Type*} {mΩ : MeasurableSpace Ω}
4335variable {P : Measure Ω} {ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ} {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ}
4336
4337theorem cluster_hitting_of_gen [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d)
4338    (hK : KernelLocal d D.s) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
4339    (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W') {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ}
4340    {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y)
4341    (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card) {q C' : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) (hC'0 : 0 ≤ C')
4342    (δ : ℝ) (hgen : ∀ Z : Fin N → Euc d, (∀ j, j ∉ I → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) Z) →
4343      Z ∈ liftedFree d N → diffGen D (barrierPhi q ∘ clusterDispersionLift I) Z ≤ C')
4344    (q₁ : ℝ) (hq₁ : 0 < q₁) (T : ℝ) :
4345    ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧ ∀ a : ℝ, 0 < a → a < a₀ →
4346      P {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} ≤
4347        ENNReal.ofReal (C / |Real.log a|) := by
4348  classical
4349  set T' := max T 0 with hT'
4350  have hT'0 : 0 ≤ T' := le_max_right _ _
4351  set m : ℝ := Real.log (q / 2) with hm
4352  set Kb : ℝ := barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) + m + C' * T' with hKb
4353  have hmin0 : 0 < min q₁ (q / 2) := lt_min hq₁ (by linarith)
4354  have hKb0 : 0 ≤ Kb := by
4355    have h1 : -Real.log (q / 2) ≤ barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) := barrierPhi_ge hq hmin0
4356    have := mul_nonneg hC'0 hT'0
4357    simp only [hKb, hm]; linarith
4358  refine ⟨min (q / 4) (min q₁ (Real.exp (-(2 * |m|)))), 2 * Kb, ?_, ?_⟩
4359  · exact lt_min (by linarith) (lt_min hq₁ (Real.exp_pos _))
4360  intro a ha hlt
4361  have haq : a < q / 4 := lt_of_lt_of_le hlt (min_le_left _ _)
4362  have haq₁ : a < q₁ := lt_of_lt_of_le hlt ((min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_left _ _))
4363  have hae : a < Real.exp (-(2 * |m|)) :=
4364    lt_of_lt_of_le hlt ((min_le_right _ _).trans (min_le_right _ _))
4365  have hlog : Real.log a < -(2 * |m|) := by
4366    rw [Real.log_lt_iff_lt_exp ha]; exact hae
4367  have hlogneg : Real.log a < 0 := by have := abs_nonneg m; linarith
4368  have habs : |Real.log a| = -Real.log a := abs_of_neg hlogneg
4369  have hΦa : barrierPhi q a = -Real.log a := barrierPhi_of_le hq haq.le
4370  have hca : |Real.log a| / 2 ≤ barrierPhi q a + m := by
4371    rw [hΦa, habs]
4372    have := neg_abs_le m
4373    have := le_abs_self m
4374    linarith
4375  have hca0 : 0 < barrierPhi q a + m := lt_of_lt_of_le (by rw [habs]; linarith) hca
4376  -- level estimates
4377  have hB : ∀ n : ℕ, ∃ B : Set Ω, MeasurableSet B ∧
4378      (∀ ω, Y₀ ω ∈ barrierK I a δ n → q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) →
4379        (∃ r : ℝ, 0 ≤ r ∧ r ≤ T' ∧ solHit τ Y (barrierG I a δ n) ω = (r : WithTop ℝ) ∧
4380          clusterDispersionLift I (Y r ω) ≤ a) → ω ∈ B) ∧
4381      (barrierPhi q a + Real.log (q / 2)) * (P B).toReal ≤
4382        barrierPhi q (min q₁ (q / 2)) + Real.log (q / 2) + C' * T' := fun n =>
4383    barrier_level_estimate D hK hV hW' hsol I hI hq hC'0 hgen hT'0 ha hq₁ n
4384  choose B hBm hBsub hBest using hB
4385  have hPB : ∀ n, P (B n) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * Kb / |Real.log a|) := by
4386    intro n
4387    have h1 : (P (B n)).toReal ≤ 2 * Kb / |Real.log a| := by
4388      have hlogpos : 0 < |Real.log a| := by rw [habs]; linarith
4389      rw [le_div_iff₀ hlogpos]
4390      have h2 := hBest n
4391      have h3 : (P (B n)).toReal * (|Real.log a| / 2) ≤
4392          (P (B n)).toReal * (barrierPhi q a + m) :=
4393        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hca ENNReal.toReal_nonneg
4394      nlinarith
4395    calc P (B n) = ENNReal.ofReal (P (B n)).toReal := (ENNReal.ofReal_toReal (measure_ne_top _ _)).symm
4396      _ ≤ _ := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h1
4397  -- the event is contained in the liminf
4398  have hsub : {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} ⊆
4399      ⋃ n₀ : ℕ, ⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n := by
4400    rintro ω ⟨hq0, t, ht0, htT, htτ, hQt, hδt⟩
4401    have hY0 : Y 0 ω = Y₀ ω := hsol.sol.init ω
4402    obtain ⟨n₀, hn₀⟩ := reach_eventually (τ := τ ω) (y := fun t => Y t ω) I (δ := δ) (q₁ := q₁)
4403      (T := T') haq₁.le (hsol.sol.cont ω) (hsol.mem ω) (by simpa [hY0] using hq0)
4404      ⟨t, ht0, htT.trans (le_max_left _ _), htτ, hQt, hδt⟩
4405    refine Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨n₀, Set.mem_iInter₂.2 fun n hn => ?_⟩
4406    obtain ⟨hK0, r, hr0, hrT, hhit, hQr⟩ := hn₀ n hn
4407    exact hBsub n ω (by simpa [hY0] using hK0) hq0 ⟨r, hr0, hrT, hhit, hQr⟩
4408  have hmono : Monotone fun n₀ : ℕ => ⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n := by
4409    intro k l hkl
4410    exact Set.iInter₂_mono' fun n hn => ⟨n, hkl.trans hn, subset_rfl⟩
4411  calc P {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω}
4412      ≤ P (⋃ n₀ : ℕ, ⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n) := measure_mono hsub
4413    _ = ⨆ n₀ : ℕ, P (⋂ n : ℕ, ⋂ (_ : n₀ ≤ n), B n) := hmono.measure_iUnion
4414    _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (2 * Kb / |Real.log a|) := by
4415        refine iSup_le fun n₀ => ?_
4416        exact (measure_mono (Set.iInter₂_subset n₀ le_rfl)).trans (hPB n₀)
4417
4418theorem cluster_barrier_of_hit [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) 
4419    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)}
4420    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) (I : Finset (Fin N)) (hI : 2 ≤ I.card)
4421    (hhit : ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ → ∀ q₁ : ℝ, 0 < q₁ → ∀ T : ℝ, ∃ a₀ C : ℝ, 0 < a₀ ∧
4422      ∀ a : ℝ, 0 < a → a < a₀ →
4423        P {ω | q₁ ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} ≤
4424          ENNReal.ofReal (C / |Real.log a|))
4425    (hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), I ⊂ J → J.card = I.card + 1 →
4426      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0)
4427    (T : ℝ) : P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4428  classical
4429  set F : ℝ → Set Ω := fun δ => {ω | ∃ s : ℝ, 0 ≤ s ∧ s < T ∧ (s : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω ∧
4430    ∃ j, j ∉ I ∧ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) (Y s ω) ≤ δ} with hF
4431  set X : ℕ → Set Ω := fun k => {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω ∧
4432    1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1) ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω)} with hX
4433  -- Step A
4434  have hA : ∀ k : ℕ, ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ → P (X k ∩ (F δ)ᶜ) = 0 := by
4435    intro k δ hδ
4436    obtain ⟨a₀, C, ha₀, hC⟩ := hhit δ hδ (1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1)) (by positivity) T
4437    have hsub : ∀ a : ℝ, 0 < a → X k ∩ (F δ)ᶜ ⊆
4438        {ω | 1 / ((k : ℝ) + 1) ≤ clusterDispersionLift I (Y₀ ω) ∧ ClusterReach I a δ T τ Y ω} := by
4439      intro a ha ω ⟨⟨hcol, hq0⟩, hnF⟩
4440      refine ⟨hq0, ?_⟩
4441      obtain ⟨t, ht0, htT, htτ, hQt⟩ := hcol a ha
4442      refine ⟨t, ht0, htT.le, htτ, hQt.le, fun s hs0 hst j hj => ?_⟩
4443      by_contra hle
4444      push_neg at hle
4445      exact hnF ⟨s, hs0, lt_of_le_of_lt hst htT,
4446        lt_of_le_of_lt (by exact_mod_cast hst) htτ, j, hj, hle⟩
4447    obtain ⟨n₀, hn₀⟩ := exists_nat_gt (-Real.log a₀)
4448    have hev : ∀ n : ℕ, n₀ ≤ n → P (X k ∩ (F δ)ᶜ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (C / (n : ℝ)) := by
4449      intro n hn
4450      have hnR : (n₀ : ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast hn
4451      have hlt : Real.exp (-(n : ℝ)) < a₀ := by
4452        rw [← Real.lt_log_iff_exp_lt ha₀]; linarith
4453      have := hC _ (Real.exp_pos _) hlt
4454      rw [Real.log_exp, abs_neg, Nat.abs_cast] at this
4455      exact (measure_mono (hsub _ (Real.exp_pos _))).trans this
4456    have htend : Tendsto (fun n : ℕ => ENNReal.ofReal (C / (n : ℝ))) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4457      have := ENNReal.tendsto_ofReal (tendsto_const_div_atTop_nhds_zero_nat C)
4458      simpa using this
4459    exact le_antisymm (ge_of_tendsto htend (Filter.eventually_atTop.2 ⟨n₀, hev⟩)) bot_le
4460  -- Step B
4461  have hB : ∀ k : ℕ, X k ⊆ (⋃ j ∈ ({j | j ∉ I} : Set (Fin N)),
4462        {ω | ClusterCollapse (insert j I) T τ Y ω}) ∪ ⋃ l : ℕ, X k ∩ (F (1 / ((l : ℝ) + 1)))ᶜ := by
4463    intro k ω hω
4464    by_cases hcol : ω ∈ ⋃ j ∈ ({j | j ∉ I} : Set (Fin N)), {ω | ClusterCollapse (insert j I) T τ Y ω}
4465    · exact Or.inl hcol
4466    right
4467    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq, not_exists] at hcol
4468    have hnc : ∀ j, ∃ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε ∧ (j ∉ I → ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → t < T → (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω →
4469        ε ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j I) (Y t ω)) := by
4470      intro j
4471      by_cases hj : j ∈ I
4472      · exact ⟨1, one_pos, fun h => absurd hj h⟩
4473      · have := hcol j hj
4474        unfold ClusterCollapse at this
4475        push_neg at this
4476        obtain ⟨ε, hε, h⟩ := this
4477        exact ⟨ε, hε, fun _ t h0 h1 h2 => h t h0 h1 h2⟩
4478    choose ε hε0 hε using hnc
4479    set ε₀ : ℝ := (1 + ∑ j, 1 / ε j)⁻¹ with hε₀
4480    have hS : 0 ≤ ∑ j, 1 / ε j := Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => (one_div_pos.2 (hε0 j)).le
4481    have hε₀pos : 0 < ε₀ := inv_pos.2 (by linarith)
4482    have hε₀le : ∀ j, ε₀ ≤ ε j := by
4483      intro j
4484      have h1 : 1 / ε j ≤ ∑ j, 1 / ε j :=
4485        Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => 1 / ε j) (fun j _ => (one_div_pos.2 (hε0 j)).le)
4486          (Finset.mem_univ j)
4487      rw [hε₀]
4488      have : (1 + ∑ j, 1 / ε j)⁻¹ ≤ (1 / ε j)⁻¹ := inv_anti₀ (one_div_pos.2 (hε0 j)) (by linarith)
4489      simpa using this
4490    obtain ⟨l, hl⟩ := exists_nat_one_div_lt hε₀pos
4491    refine Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨l, hω, ?_⟩
4492    rintro ⟨s, hs0, hsT, hsτ, j, hj, hle⟩
4493    have := hε j hj s hs0 hsT hsτ
4494    linarith [hε₀le j]
4495  -- conclusion
4496  have hk : {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} ⊆ ⋃ k : ℕ, X k := by
4497    intro ω hω
4498    have hfree : Y₀ ω ∈ liftedFree d N := by
4499      have := hsol.mem ω 0 le_rfl (by simpa using hsol.pos ω)
4500      rwa [hsol.sol.init ω] at this
4501    have hpos := clusterDispersionLift_pos hI hfree
4502    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := exists_nat_one_div_lt hpos
4503    exact Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨k, hω, hk.le⟩
4504  refine measure_mono_null hk (measure_iUnion_null fun k => ?_)
4505  refine measure_mono_null (hB k) (measure_union_null ?_ (measure_iUnion_null fun l => ?_))
4506  · refine measure_biUnion_null_iff (Set.to_countable _) |>.2 fun j hj => ?_
4507    exact hind (insert j I) (Finset.ssubset_insert hj) (Finset.card_insert_of_notMem hj) T
4508  · exact hA k _ (by positivity)
4509
4510
4511end HitBarrier

4513theorem symNonpos_of_d1 (M : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) ℝ) (h : M 0 0 ≤ 0) : Torus.SymNonpos M := by
4514  unfold Torus.SymNonpos
4515  rw [Matrix.posSemidef_iff_dotProduct_mulVec]
4516  constructor
4517  · ext i j
4518    fin_cases i; fin_cases j
4519    simp [Torus.symPart, Matrix.conjTranspose]
4520  · intro x
4521    simp [Torus.symPart, Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct]
4522    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (x 0)]
4523
4524end ClusterBarrier
4525
4526/-- Lemma `B:lem:d1-pair-barrier` (M L11938-11948), positive temperature: `d = 1`, `I = {i,j}`,
4527`0 < s < 1`, `β_N < ∞` (`invβ > 0`), `M = -a` with `a > 0`; if no three-point enlargement
4528collapses before `T ∧ τ` then the pair does not.  Proof: near the pair diagonal
4529`Q_I = r²/2`, `𝒜_I = (2/N) a s |r|^{-s} = (2/N) a s (2Q_I)^{-s/2}` (from `clusterInternal`
4530with `eucRieszGrad`), so `ℒ(-log Q_I) ≤ 2/(β Q_I) - (2as/N) 2^{-s/2} Q_I^{-1-s/2} + C_δ`
4531(`B:eq:d1-riesz-generator`), whose negative term dominates as `Q_I ↓ 0`; then the stopping
4532argument of `cluster_hitting_probability` with this barrier.  Uses the `d = 1` kernel inputs
4533through `hK`. -/
4534theorem d1_pair_barrier [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData 1) (hK : KernelLocal 1 D.s)
4535    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hs0 : 0 < D.s) (hβ : 0 < D.invβ) (ha : D.M 0 0 < 0)
4536    {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin 1) → ℝ} (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
4537    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)}
4538    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) (i j : Fin N) (hij : i ≠ j)
4539    (hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) ⊂ J → J.card = 3 →
4540      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0)
4541    (T : ℝ) : P {ω | ClusterCollapse ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4542  have hI : 2 ≤ ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)).card := by rw [Finset.card_pair hij]
4543  refine cluster_barrier_of_hit D hsol ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) hI ?_ ?_ T
4544  · intro δ hδ q₁ hq₁ T'
4545    obtain ⟨q, C₀, hq, hqr, hC₀, hrem⟩ := clusterRemainder_bound D hK hV _ hI δ hδ
4546    obtain ⟨q₂, hq₂, -, hint⟩ := d1_internal_ge D hs0 ha hij
4547    obtain ⟨C', hC'0, hC'⟩ := barrier_generator_bound_of_internal D (symNonpos_of_d1 D.M ha.le) _ hI (q := min q q₂)
4548      (lt_min hq hq₂) (lt_of_le_of_lt (min_le_left _ _) hqr) hC₀
4549      {Z | ∀ j', j' ∉ ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) → δ ≤ clusterDispersionLift (insert j' {i, j}) Z}
4550      (fun Y hYS hYf hYq => hrem Y hYf (lt_of_lt_of_le hYq (min_le_left _ _)) hYS)
4551      (fun Y _ hYf hYq => by
4552        have hpos := clusterDispersionLift_pos hI hYf
4553        have h2 := hint Y hpos (by linarith [min_le_right q q₂])
4554        rw [Finset.card_pair hij]
4555        norm_num
4556        linarith)
4557    exact cluster_hitting_of_gen D hK hV hW' hsol _ hI (lt_min hq hq₂) hC'0 δ
4558      (fun Z hZS hZf => hC' Z hZS hZf) q₁ hq₁ T'
4559  · intro J hJ hJc T'
4560    exact hind J hJ (by rw [hJc, Finset.card_pair hij]) T'
4561
4562/-- Lemma `B:lem:d1-pair-barrier`, zero-temperature clause (M L11947, L11966): for
4563`invβ = 0`, every `0 ≤ s < 1` and every `a ≥ 0` (`Sym M ≤ 0`), the pair does not collapse.
4564A corollary of `cluster_barrier_zero_temp`. -/
4565theorem d1_pair_barrier_zero_temp [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData 1)
4566    (hK : KernelLocal 1 D.s) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hβ : D.invβ = 0)
4567    (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin 1) → ℝ}
4568    (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W') {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ}
4569    {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)} (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y)
4570    (i j : Fin N) (hij : i ≠ j)
4571    (hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) ⊂ J → J.card = 3 →
4572      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0)
4573    (T : ℝ) : P {ω | ClusterCollapse ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4574  refine cluster_barrier_zero_temp D hK hM hV hβ hW' hsol ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N))
4575    (by rw [Finset.card_pair hij]) ?_ T
4576  intro J hJ hJc T'
4577  exact hind J hJ (by rw [hJc, Finset.card_pair hij]) T'

4579/-- Proposition `B:prop:particle-no-collision-d1` (M L11969-11975): `d = 1`, `Sym M ≤ 0`, and
4580either `β_N = ∞` (`invβ = 0`), or `β_N < ∞`, `0 < s < 1` and `M = -a` with `a > 0`; then
4581`τ_⋆ = ∞` a.s.  Proof: descending induction; clusters with `|I| ≥ 3` by `cluster_barrier`
4582(`1·(|I|-1) ≥ 2`), pairs by `d1_pair_barrier` / `d1_pair_barrier_zero_temp`; then the collision
4583alternative as in `particle_no_collision_dge2`.  The kernel inputs are `kernelLocal_d1`
4584(section `KernelD1Inputs`, from `HRS/Kernel/D1.lean`). -/
4585theorem particle_no_collision_d1 [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData 1)
4586    (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
4587    (hcase : D.invβ = 0 ∨ (0 < D.invβ ∧ 0 < D.s ∧ D.M 0 0 < 0))
4588    {W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin 1) → ℝ} (hW' : IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W')
4589    {Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)} {τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ} {Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)}
4590    (hsol : IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y) :
4591    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, τ ω = ⊤ := by
4592  classical
4593  have hs1 : D.s < 1 := by simpa using D.hsd
4594  have hK : KernelLocal 1 D.s := kernelLocal_d1 D.hs0 hs1
4595  have key : ∀ m : ℕ, ∀ I : Finset (Fin N), 2 ≤ I.card → N - I.card = m →
4596      ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4597    intro m
4598    induction m using Nat.strong_induction_on with
4599    | _ m ih =>
4600      intro I hI hm T
4601      have hIN : I.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_univ I
4602      have hind : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), I ⊂ J → J.card = I.card + 1 →
4603          ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0 := by
4604        intro J _ hJc T'
4605        have hJN : J.card ≤ N := by simpa using Finset.card_le_univ J
4606        exact ih (N - J.card) (by omega) J (by omega) rfl T'
4607      by_cases h3 : 3 ≤ I.card
4608      · refine cluster_barrier D hK hM hV hW' hsol I hI ?_ hind T
4609        have : 2 ≤ I.card - 1 := by omega
4610        simpa using this
4611      · have h2 : I.card = 2 := by omega
4612        obtain ⟨i, j, hij, rfl⟩ := Finset.card_eq_two.1 h2
4613        have hind' : ∀ J : Finset (Fin N), ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) ⊂ J → J.card = 3 →
4614            ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse J T τ Y ω} = 0 :=
4615          fun J hJ hJc T' => hind J hJ (by rw [hJc, h2]) T'
4616        rcases hcase with hβ | ⟨hβ, hs0, ha⟩
4617        · exact d1_pair_barrier_zero_temp D hK hV hβ hM hW' hsol i j hij hind' T
4618        · exact d1_pair_barrier D hK hV hs0 hβ ha hW' hsol i j hij hind' T
4619  have h1 : ∀ I : Finset (Fin N), 2 ≤ I.card → ∀ T : ℝ, P {ω | ClusterCollapse I T τ Y ω} = 0 :=
4620    fun I hI T => key _ I hI rfl T
4621  have hcol := IsMaximalParticleSolution.collision_alternative D hK hV hW' hsol
4622  have hnull : P (⋃ T : ℕ, ⋃ i : Fin N, ⋃ j : Fin N, ⋃ (_ : i ≠ j),
4623      {ω | ClusterCollapse ({i, j} : Finset (Fin N)) (T : ℝ) τ Y ω}) = 0 := by
4624    refine measure_iUnion_null fun T => measure_iUnion_null fun i => measure_iUnion_null fun j =>
4625      measure_iUnion_null fun hij => h1 _ ?_ _
4626    rw [Finset.card_pair hij]
4627  have hn2 := measure_eq_zero_iff_ae_notMem.1 hnull
4628  filter_upwards [hcol, hn2] with ω hω hnot
4629  by_contra hne
4630  have hlt : τ ω < ⊤ := lt_top_iff_ne_top.2 hne
4631  obtain ⟨τ₀, hτ₀⟩ := WithTop.ne_top_iff_exists.1 hne
4632  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := exists_nat_gt τ₀
4633  have hτT : ∀ t : ℝ, (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → t < (T : ℝ) := by
4634    intro t ht
4635    rw [← hτ₀] at ht
4636    have : t < τ₀ := by exact_mod_cast ht
4637    linarith
4638  apply hnot
4639  simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq]
4640  by_contra hno
4641  push_neg at hno
4642  have hpair : ∀ p : Fin N × Fin N, ∃ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε ∧ (p.1 ≠ p.2 → ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → t < T →
4643      (t : WithTop ℝ) < τ ω → ε ≤ clusterDispersionLift ({p.1, p.2} : Finset (Fin N)) (Y t ω)) := by
4644    intro p
4645    by_cases hp : p.1 = p.2
4646    · exact ⟨1, one_pos, fun h => absurd hp h⟩
4647    · have := hno T p.1 p.2 hp
4648      unfold ClusterCollapse at this
4649      push_neg at this
4650      obtain ⟨ε, hε, h⟩ := this
4651      exact ⟨ε, hε, fun _ t h0 h1 h2 => h t h0 h1 h2⟩
4652  choose ε hε0 hε using hpair
4653  obtain ⟨e, he0, hele⟩ := exists_pos_le_fintype ε hε0
4654  obtain ⟨t, ht1, htτ, i, j, hij, hdist⟩ := hω hlt (min (1 / 8) (16 * e)) (lt_min (by norm_num)
4655    (by linarith)) 0 le_rfl (by simpa using hsol.pos ω)
4656  have hr8 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) < 1 / 8 :=
4657    lt_of_lt_of_le hdist (min_le_left _ _)
4658  have hr16 : Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) < 16 * e :=
4659    lt_of_lt_of_le hdist (min_le_right _ _)
4660  have hQ := clusterDispersionLift_pair hij (Y t ω) hr8
4661  have hnn : 0 ≤ Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j)) := norm_nonneg _
4662  have hlow := hε (i, j) hij t ht1.le (hτT t htτ) htτ
4663  have := hele (i, j)
4664  set r := Torus.tdist (Torus.mk (Y t ω i)) (Torus.mk (Y t ω j))
4665  simp only at hlow
4666  rw [hQ] at hlow
4667  nlinarith

4671/-- **Corrected reduction for existence** (replaces `exists_isStrongSolution_of_no_exit` of
4672`Bridge.lean`, stated through the bundle `IsMaximalParticleSolution`; the Bridge version has been
4673corrected to require `0 < τ` in its `hnoexit` premise, which as first stated was unsatisfiable): with the
4674lifted drift locally Lipschitz on the collision-free region, and given that every maximal
4675solution (`IsMaximalParticleSolution`, for every Brownian filtration carrying `X₀`) has infinite
4676lifetime a.s., the particle system has a global collision-free strong solution for every
4677horizon `T`.  Same proof as in `Bridge.lean`, with positivity carried along. -/
4678theorem exists_isStrongSolution_of_maximal (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
4679    (D : DynData d) (hb : IsLocLipschitzDrift (liftedFree d N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z))
4680    (X₀ : Ω → Config d N) (hX₀ : Measurable X₀) (hdist : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, X₀ ω ∉ collisionSet d N)
4681    (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) (hW : IsVectorBrownian P W)
4682    (hind : IndepFun X₀ (fun ω => fun t => W t ω) P)
4683    (hnoexit : ∀ (ℱ : Filtration ℝ mΩ) (W' : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N × Fin d) → ℝ),
4684      IsBrownianFiltration P ℱ W' → (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t i j, W' t ω (i, j) = W t ω i j) →
4685      ∀ (Y₀ : Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) (τ : Ω → WithTop ℝ) (Y : ℝ → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)),
4686      IsMaximalParticleSolution P ℱ W' D Y₀ τ Y →
4687      (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, (fun i => Torus.mk (Y₀ ω i)) = X₀ ω) →
4688      ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, τ ω = ⊤)
4689    (T : ℝ) : ∃ X, IsStrongSolution P D T X₀ W X := by
4690  classical
4691  obtain ⟨W', hW'm, hWW', hBF, hX₀ℱ⟩ :=
4692    exists_isBrownianFiltration_of_isVectorBrownian P W hW X₀ hX₀ hind
4693  set ℱ := generatedFiltration P X₀ hX₀ W' hW'm with hℱ
4694  obtain ⟨ω₀, hω₀⟩ : ∃ ω₀, X₀ ω₀ ∉ collisionSet d N := hdist.exists
4695  let rep : Ω → Fin N → Euc d := fun ω i => Torus.rep (X₀ ω i)
4696  have hmk : ∀ ω, (fun i => Torus.mk (rep ω i)) = X₀ ω := fun ω =>
4697    funext fun i => Torus.mk_rep _
4698  have hcoll : MeasurableSet (collisionSet d N) := (isClosed_collisionSet d N).measurableSet
4699  let Y₀ : Ω → Fin N → Euc d := fun ω => if X₀ ω ∈ collisionSet d N then rep ω₀ else rep ω
4700  have hY₀m : ∀ t : ℝ, Measurable[ℱ t] Y₀ := by
4701    intro t
4702    let _ : MeasurableSpace Ω := ℱ t
4703    have hrep : Measurable rep := measurable_pi_iff.2 fun i =>
4704      (measurable_torusRep d).comp ((measurable_pi_apply i).comp (hX₀ℱ t))
4705    exact Measurable.ite (hX₀ℱ t hcoll) measurable_const hrep
4706  have hY₀0 : ∀ ω, Y₀ ω ∈ liftedFree d N := by
4707    intro ω
4708    show (fun i => Torus.mk (Y₀ ω i)) ∉ collisionSet d N
4709    by_cases h : X₀ ω ∈ collisionSet d N
4710    · simp only [Y₀, h, ↓reduceIte]
4711      rw [hmk ω₀]
4712      exact hω₀
4713    · simp only [Y₀, h, ↓reduceIte]
4714      rw [hmk ω]
4715      exact h
4716  have hY₀ae : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, (fun i => Torus.mk (Y₀ ω i)) = X₀ ω := by
4717    filter_upwards [hdist] with ω h
4718    simp only [Y₀, h, ↓reduceIte]
4719    exact hmk ω
4720  obtain ⟨τ, Y, hstop, hpos, hsol, hmem, hexit, hmax⟩ := exists_sde_solution_maximal hBF
4721    (liftedFree d N) (isOpen_liftedFree d N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) hb (liftedNoise D) Y₀
4722    hY₀m hY₀0
4723  have htop : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, τ ω = ⊤ :=
4724    hnoexit ℱ W' hBF hWW' Y₀ τ Y ⟨hY₀m, hstop, hpos, hsol, hmem, hexit⟩ hY₀ae
4725  -- redefine on the null set `{τ ≠ ⊤}`
4726  have hAmeas : MeasurableSet {ω | τ ω ≠ ⊤} := by
4727    have : {ω | τ ω ≠ ⊤} = ⋃ n : ℕ, {ω | τ ω ≤ ((n : ℝ) : WithTop ℝ)} := by
4728      ext ω
4729      simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq, Set.mem_iUnion]
4730      constructor
4731      · intro h
4732        obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := WithTop.ne_top_iff_exists.1 h
4733        obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := exists_nat_ge r
4734        exact ⟨n, by rw [← hr]; exact_mod_cast hn⟩
4735      · rintro ⟨n, hn⟩ h
4736        rw [h] at hn
4737        exact absurd hn (by simp)
4738    rw [this]
4739    exact MeasurableSet.iUnion fun n => ℱ.le _ _ (hstop (n : ℝ))
4740  have hA0 : P {ω | τ ω ≠ ⊤} = 0 := ae_iff.1 htop
4741  have hAt : ∀ t : ℝ, MeasurableSet[ℱ t] {ω | τ ω = ⊤} := fun t => by
4742    have := (hBF.complete t _ hAmeas hA0).compl
4743    have e : {ω | τ ω = ⊤} = {ω | τ ω ≠ ⊤}ᶜ := by ext ω; simp
4744    rw [e]
4745    exact this
4746  let Yg : ℝ → Ω → Fin N → Euc d := fun t ω => if τ ω = ⊤ then Y t ω else Y₀ ω
4747  have hsolTop : IsAdditiveSDESolution P ℱ W' (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) (liftedNoise D) Y₀
4748      (fun _ => ⊤) Yg := by
4749    refine ⟨fun t => ?_, fun ω => ?_, fun ω t ht _ => ?_, ?_⟩
4750    · let _ : MeasurableSpace Ω := ℱ t
4751      exact Measurable.ite (hAt t) (hsol.adapted t) (hY₀m t)
4752    · by_cases h : τ ω = ⊤
4753      · simp only [Yg, h, ↓reduceIte]; exact hsol.init ω
4754      · simp only [Yg, h, ↓reduceIte]
4755    · by_cases h : τ ω = ⊤
4756      · simp only [Yg, h, ↓reduceIte]
4757        exact hsol.cont ω t ht (by rw [h]; exact WithTop.coe_lt_top _)
4758      · simp only [Yg, h, ↓reduceIte]
4759        exact continuousOn_const
4760    · filter_upwards [hsol.eq, htop] with ω h1 h2 t ht _
4761      simp only [Yg, h2, ↓reduceIte]
4762      exact h1 t ht (by rw [h2]; exact WithTop.coe_lt_top _)
4763  have hfree : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → Yg t ω ∈ liftedFree d N := by
4764    filter_upwards [htop] with ω h t ht
4765    simp only [Yg, h, ↓reduceIte]
4766    exact hmem ω t ht (by rw [h]; exact WithTop.coe_lt_top _)
4767  have hS := isStrongSolution_of_sde P ℱ D W hW W' hBF hWW' Y₀ Yg hsolTop hfree T
4768  refine ⟨_, { hS with init := ?_ }⟩
4769  filter_upwards [hS.init, hY₀ae] with ω h1 h2
4770  rw [h1]
4771  exact h2

4773/-- The uniform separation `B:eq:particle-uniform-separation` (M L11653-11655, L11990-11994):
4774for every strong solution on `[0,T]`, a.s. `min_{0≤t≤T} min_{i<j} dist(x_i^t, x_j^t) > 0`.
4775From `IsStrongSolution.no_collision` and `IsStrongSolution.cont`: a continuous positive
4776function on the compact `[0,T]` has a positive minimum. -/
4777theorem particle_uniform_separation (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d)
4778    (T : ℝ) (X₀ : Ω → Config d N) (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d))
4779    (X : ℝ≥0 → Ω → Config d N) (hX : IsStrongSolution P D T X₀ W X) :
4780    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∃ c : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ t : ℝ≥0, (t : ℝ) ≤ T → ∀ i j : Fin N, i ≠ j →
4781      c ≤ Torus.tdist (X t ω i) (X t ω j) := by
4782  filter_upwards [hX.cont, hX.no_collision] with ω hcont hnc
4783  let S : Set ℝ≥0 := {t | (t : ℝ) ≤ T}
4784  let P : Set (Fin N × Fin N) := {p | p.1 ≠ p.2}
4785  have hS : IsCompact S := by
4786    refine IsCompact.of_isClosed_subset (isCompact_Icc (a := (0 : ℝ≥0)) (b := Real.toNNReal T))
4787      (isClosed_le NNReal.continuous_coe continuous_const) fun t ht => ⟨bot_le, ?_⟩
4788    simpa using Real.toNNReal_mono (show (t : ℝ) ≤ T from ht)
4789  have hP : IsCompact P := (Set.toFinite P).isCompact
4790  let g : ℝ≥0 × (Fin N × Fin N) → ℝ := fun q => Torus.tdist (X q.1 ω q.2.1) (X q.1 ω q.2.2)
4791  have hg : Continuous g := by
4792    refine continuous_prod_of_discrete_right.2 fun p => ?_
4793    exact Torus.continuous_tdist_comp (hcont p.1) (hcont p.2)
4794  by_cases hne : (S ×ˢ P).Nonempty
4795  · obtain ⟨q, hq, hmin⟩ := (hS.prod hP).exists_isMinOn hne hg.continuousOn
4796    have hmin' : ∀ x ∈ S ×ˢ P, g q ≤ g x := fun x hx => isMinOn_iff.1 hmin x hx
4797    refine ⟨g q, ?_, fun t ht i j hij => ?_⟩
4798    · exact Torus.tdist_pos_of_ne fun h => hnc q.1 hq.1 ⟨q.2.1, q.2.2, hq.2, h⟩
4799    · have hmem : (t, (i, j)) ∈ S ×ˢ P := Set.mk_mem_prod ht hij
4800      have h5 := hmin' (t, (i, j)) hmem
4801      simp only [g] at h5
4802      exact h5
4803  · refine ⟨1, one_pos, fun t ht i j hij => ?_⟩
4804    exact absurd ⟨(t, (i, j)), ht, hij⟩ hne
4805
4806end Probabilistic

4808/-- **Proposition B.1 `B:thm:particle-wp`, case (i)** (M L11645-11656, L11989-12000), with
4809exactly the statement of `HRS.particle_wellposed` (`HRS/Dynamics/Setup.lean`): for `N ≥ 2`,
4810`0 ≤ s < d`, `Sym M ≤ 0`, `V ∈ C²`, a.s. pairwise distinct `X₀` independent of `W`, and
4811`d ≥ 2`, the particle system has a collision-free strong solution on `[0,T]` and is pathwise
4812unique there.  Existence: `exists_isStrongSolution_of_maximal` with `particle_no_collision_dge2`;
4813uniqueness: `pathwiseUnique_of_locLipschitz` with `liftedDrift_locLipschitz`. -/
4814theorem particle_wellposed_proof {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
4815    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData d) (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hN : 2 ≤ N)
4816    (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M) (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V)
4817    (X₀ : Ω → Config d N) (hX₀ : Measurable X₀) (hdist : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, X₀ ω ∉ collisionSet d N)
4818    (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc d)) (hW : IsVectorBrownian P W)
4819    (hind : ProbabilityTheory.IndepFun X₀ (fun ω => fun t => W t ω) P) (T : ℝ) (hT : 0 < T) :
4820    (∃ X, IsStrongSolution P D T X₀ W X) ∧ PathwiseUnique P D T X₀ W := by
4821  have hK : KernelLocal d D.s := kernelLocal_of_two_le hd D.hs0 D.hsd
4822  have hb : IsLocLipschitzDrift (liftedFree d N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) :=
4823    liftedDrift_locLipschitz D hK.smoothOffZero hV
4824  refine ⟨?_, pathwiseUnique_of_locLipschitz P D hb T X₀ W hW⟩
4825  refine exists_isStrongSolution_of_maximal P D hb X₀ hX₀ hdist W hW hind ?_ T
4826  intro ℱ W' hW' _ Y₀ τ Y hsol _
4827  exact (particle_no_collision_dge2 D hd hM hV hW' hsol).2

4829/-- **Proposition B.1 `B:thm:particle-wp`, case (ii)** (M L11649): `d = 1`, `β_N = ∞`
4830(`invβ = 0`), `N ≥ 2`, `0 ≤ s < 1`, `Sym M ≤ 0`, `V ∈ C²`; same conclusion as case (i).
4831Uses the `d = 1` kernel inputs (`kernelLocal_d1`). -/
4832theorem particle_wellposed_d1_zero_temp {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
4833    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData 1) (hN : 2 ≤ N) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M)
4834    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hβ : D.invβ = 0)
4835    (X₀ : Ω → Config 1 N) (hX₀ : Measurable X₀) (hdist : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, X₀ ω ∉ collisionSet 1 N)
4836    (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)) (hW : IsVectorBrownian P W)
4837    (hind : ProbabilityTheory.IndepFun X₀ (fun ω => fun t => W t ω) P) (T : ℝ) (hT : 0 < T) :
4838    (∃ X, IsStrongSolution P D T X₀ W X) ∧ PathwiseUnique P D T X₀ W := by
4839  have hs1 : D.s < 1 := by simpa using D.hsd
4840  have hK : KernelLocal 1 D.s := kernelLocal_d1 D.hs0 hs1
4841  have hb : IsLocLipschitzDrift (liftedFree 1 N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) :=
4842    liftedDrift_locLipschitz D hK.smoothOffZero hV
4843  refine ⟨?_, pathwiseUnique_of_locLipschitz P D hb T X₀ W hW⟩
4844  refine exists_isStrongSolution_of_maximal P D hb X₀ hX₀ hdist W hW hind ?_ T
4845  intro ℱ W' hW' _ Y₀ τ Y hsol _
4846  exact particle_no_collision_d1 D hM hV (Or.inl hβ) hW' hsol

4848/-- **Proposition B.1 `B:thm:particle-wp`, case (iii)** (M L11650): `d = 1`, `β_N < ∞`
4849(`invβ > 0`), `0 < s < 1`, `M = -a` with `a > 0`, `N ≥ 2`, `Sym M ≤ 0`, `V ∈ C²`; same
4850conclusion as case (i).  Uses the `d = 1` kernel inputs (`kernelLocal_d1`). -/
4851theorem particle_wellposed_d1_riesz {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω)
4852    [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (D : DynData 1) (hN : 2 ≤ N) (hM : Torus.SymNonpos D.M)
4853    (hV : Torus.IsCk 2 D.V) (hβ : 0 < D.invβ) (hs0 : 0 < D.s) (ha : D.M 0 0 < 0)
4854    (X₀ : Ω → Config 1 N) (hX₀ : Measurable X₀) (hdist : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, X₀ ω ∉ collisionSet 1 N)
4855    (W : ℝ≥0 → Ω → (Fin N → Euc 1)) (hW : IsVectorBrownian P W)
4856    (hind : ProbabilityTheory.IndepFun X₀ (fun ω => fun t => W t ω) P) (T : ℝ) (hT : 0 < T) :
4857    (∃ X, IsStrongSolution P D T X₀ W X) ∧ PathwiseUnique P D T X₀ W := by
4858  have hs1 : D.s < 1 := by simpa using D.hsd
4859  have hK : KernelLocal 1 D.s := kernelLocal_d1 D.hs0 hs1
4860  have hb : IsLocLipschitzDrift (liftedFree 1 N) (fun _ Z => liftedDrift D Z) :=
4861    liftedDrift_locLipschitz D hK.smoothOffZero hV
4862  refine ⟨?_, pathwiseUnique_of_locLipschitz P D hb T X₀ W hW⟩
4863  refine exists_isStrongSolution_of_maximal P D hb X₀ hX₀ hdist W hW hind ?_ T
4864  intro ℱ W' hW' _ Y₀ τ Y hsol _
4865  exact particle_no_collision_d1 D hM hV (Or.inr ⟨hβ, hs0, ha⟩) hW' hsol
4866
4867end HRS
